<p> The Vancouver Sun - RSS Feedhttps : **27;5621;TOOLONG , 18 Mar 2020 18:13:02 +0000Kasey Wilson : Decadent French onion soup warms the soulhttp : **36;5650;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;5688;TOOLONG ... title= " A golden topping of toasty croutons and gooey cheese crowns a bowl of Decadent French Onion Soup . Photo : Robert Shaer. " alt= " A golden topping of toasty croutons and gooey cheese crowns a bowl of Decadent French Onion Soup . Photo : Robert Shaer. " data-portal-copyright= " Robert Shaer " **28;5724;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1239708 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Judith Jones , the legendary cookbook editor who brought Julia Child and other culinary luminaries into our homes , once recalled how scornful Jacques Pepin was of the waste he encountered in American restaurant kitchens . In his own kitchen , he always kept an empty milk carton into which he would toss the peelings , tough stems and end pieces from vegetables . At the end of the day he would tuck the carton into the freezer , and when it was full he would dump it into a pot and make stock . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; You could follow his example and add those kitchen trimmings when making strong beef stock , the basis for today&amp;rsquo ; s Decadent French Onion Soup . Simply add a green salad to turn this soul-warming dish into a meal . The recipe is adapted from Justin Joyce and Stephan MacIntyre , the talents behind Burgoo , which lists the dish under comfort food , not soups , at its five bistros. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;5754;TOOLONG ... title= " A golden topping of toasty croutons and gooey cheese crowns a bowl of Decadent French Onion Soup . Photo : Robert Shaer. " alt= " A golden topping of toasty croutons and gooey cheese crowns a bowl of Decadent French Onion Soup . Photo : Robert Shaer. " data-portal-copyright= " Robert Shaer " **28;5790;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1239707 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Decadent French Onion Soup&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; You will need six individual ovenproof soup bowls for this recipe , ideally ones that narrow at the top . This classic shape helps to keep the bread and cheese afloat as the soup bakes . Be sure to warn guests that the bowls are burning hot &amp;mdash ; and have ice at the ready in case they forget . I buy my stock at the Granville Island Stock Market ; see today&amp;rsquo ; s second recipe or Kitchen Hack for other options . ( Adapted from Burgoo : Food for Comfort by Justin Joyce and Stephan MacIntyre , Figure 1. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;frac14 ; cup extra-virgin olive oil &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;frac14 ; cup butter &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 6 cups thinly sliced white onions ( about 8 medium ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 4 fat garlic cloves , chopped &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 cups dry red wine &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sea salt and freshly&amp;nbsp ; ground pepper &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 6 cups Strong Beef Stock ( recipe follows ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 cups grated mozzarella cheese &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 1/2 cup grated Emmental cheese &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 1/2 cup grated Gruy&amp;egrave ; re cheese &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 6 slices rustic baguette , cut into in 1-inch cubes &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Heat olive oil and butter in a large , heavy stockpot over low heat . Add onions and saut&amp;eacute ; , stirring often , for 30&amp;nbsp ; minutes . Raise the heat to medium-high , stir in garlic and cook , stirring often , for about 10&amp;nbsp ; minutes , until onions and garlic are brown and caramelized and all the liquid is evaporated . Pour in wine , season with generous pinches of salt and pepper and boil , stirring , until most of the liquid is evaporated , about 5&amp;nbsp ; minutes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Preheat the oven to broil or 450&amp;deg ; F. Set 6 ovenproof soup bowls on a baking tray . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Add the beef stock to the pot , bring to a boil , reduce heat to low and simmer , uncovered , for 30&amp;nbsp ; minutes . Divide soup evenly among prepared bowls . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a small bowl , combine the mozzarella , Emmental and Gruy&amp;egrave ; re , mixing well . Divide bread chunks among the bowls , carefully floating them on the soup to create a raft . Divide mixed cheeses among bowls , sprinkling it evenly over the bread . Bake for 6 to 8&amp;nbsp ; minutes , until cheese is golden and bubbly . Serve immediately . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Serves 6 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Strong Beef Stock&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If don&amp;rsquo ; t have a butcher who can provide the needed bones , substitute oxtail and/or short ribs . You don&amp;rsquo ; t want to leave the simmering broth unattended , so make this recipe on a day when you can be home . ( Adapted from Epicurious.com. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 4&amp;nbsp ; pounds meaty beef bones &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 unpeeled carrots , cut into 2-inch pieces &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 1 large unpeeled onion , quartered &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 1 head garlic , halved crosswise &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;frac12 ; cup dry red or white wine &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 celery ribs , cut into 2-inch pieces &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 bay leaves &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 tablespoons black peppercorns &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Salt &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Preheat oven to 450&amp;deg ; F. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Spread bones , carrots , onion and garlic in a large roasting pan and roast for 20 minutes . Stir well and continue roasting until deeply browned , about 20 minutes more . Transfer to a large stock pot . Place roasting pan on stovetop , deglaze with wine and add pan scrapings to stock pot . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Add celery , bay leaves , peppercorns and about 12 cups water to pot , covering solids completely . Cover pot and bring just to a boil . Reduce to a low simmer and cook , with lid slightly ajar , for 8 to 12 hours , adding water if needed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Set pot aside off heat to cool before straining through a fine-mesh sieve , discarding solids . Refrigerate for several hours or overnight so you can remove solidified fat from the top . Season to taste with salt before using . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Makes about 8 cups &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Kitchen Hack : Enhancing Canned Broth&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; You can make a fine onion soup with canned broth but you must doctor it : Dilute 3 cans of condensed beef broth ( such as Campbells ) with only 1 can of water and add &amp;frac14 ; cup of dry white wine to cut the sweetness. &lt;/p&gt; Kasey WilsonWed , 18 Mar 2020 18:13:02 **45;5820;TOOLONG ... Gismondi : B.C. wine of the week , wine to cellar and calendar itemshttp : **35;5867;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;5904;TOOLONG ... title= " Terrafina Restaurant at Hester Creek Estate Winery in Oliver . " alt= " Terrafina Restaurant at Hester Creek Estate Winery in Oliver . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;5940;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1233647 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Terrafina Restaurant at Hester Creek Estate Winery in Oliver has reopened under the direct management of Hester Creek . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The new team includes executive chef Adair Scott and restaurant manager Paul Henbury . Scott joins Hester Creek after a seven-year stint at Watermark Beach Resort in Osoyoos , and Henbury has over two decades in hospitality , along with an extensive background in music and a culinary career with stops at Montreal&amp;rsquo ; s famed Fairmont Le Reine Elizabeth and Tofino&amp;rsquo ; s Wickaninnish Inn&amp;rsquo ; s The Pointe Restaurant . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Terrafina Restaurant is open Wednesday to Sunday with lunch , happy hour and dinner menus , along with weekend brunch . In May , the restaurant will be open seven days a week . For more details and reservations , go to &lt;a href= " http : **30;5970;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;6002;TOOLONG ... . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; The seventh edition of B.C. Distilled &amp;mdash ; the largest spirits festival in Canada devoted exclusively to local , artisan distilleries &amp;mdash ; returns April 4 to the Croatian Cultural Centre . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The lineup features an impressive 40 distilleries from across the province . In addition to the main event on Saturday , there will be a B.C. whisky tasting hosted by Davin de Kergommeaux , recognized as the world expert on Canadian whisky . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There will also be a distillers dinner at Forage Restaurant on April 3 , where executive chef Welbert Choi will match six cocktail and spirit samples from B.C. with six terroir-based courses . An optional cocktail reception preceding the dinner will be hosted at next door&amp;rsquo ; s Timber Gastropub , with a seventh distillery providing cocktails . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For more information and to purchase tickets , visit &lt;a href= " https : //bcdistilled.ca/tickets " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;6035;TOOLONG ... . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;B.C. wine of the week&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;6068;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;6104;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Clos du Soleil Winemaker 's Series Whispered Secret Vineyard Pinot Gris 2018 , Similkameen Valley&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $21.99 90/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 626990344957 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The first time for this wine , a Pinot Gris from Keremeos&amp;rsquo ; Whispered Secret Vineyard , and it&amp;rsquo ; s fair to say that the secret is out . The light touch is welcome in this beautifully balanced , zippy white , full of orchard fruit and bright and juicy with lime and lemon pith acidity . Super expressive in the mouth , with citrus sherbet and a fine zesty texture . No boring B.C. Gris here . We are no longer surprised that Olivier Humbrech got involved with Phantom Creek&amp;rsquo ; s new vineyard in the Similkameen to grow Pinot Gris . The Similkameen is more than exceptional . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Wine for the cellar&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;6134;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;6170;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Luce della Vite La Vite Lucente 2017 , Tuscany , Italy&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $39.99 90/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 8002366000931 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Lucente is termed a second wine , but this blend of Merlot and Sangiovese is much more than that . The year 2017 was perfect : From a fresh , crisp , spring to a dry summer , with only a little helping rain before harvest to freshen the fruit . The nose is fragrant with notes of wild strawberries , black raspberries , and spice that tumble onto its toasty , silky , umami-laden red fruit palate . There is a slight tug of tannin , but you can easily enjoy this now with steak or chicken . Hold or drink . Love the balance . Food friendly . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;6200;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;6242;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;6278;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;6309;TOOLONG ... about B.C. **26;6345;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;6373;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? Email &lt;a href= " **28;6402;TOOLONG " **46;6432;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Anthony GismondiWed , 18 Mar 2020 18:00:46 **45;6480;TOOLONG ... Gismondi : Slow thoughts on wine in a fast-changing worldhttp : **35;6527;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;6564;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;6600;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1240041 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Self-isolating , self-quarantine , social distancing , flattening the curve , and COVID-19 are terms we never thought much about 90 days ago , or perhaps had even heard of , and now it&amp;rsquo ; s all you hear , day and night &amp;mdash ; and I&amp;rsquo ; m supposed to write about wine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When I think about the speed with which the virus can spread , it reminds me how relatively slow the wine business can be , and what a good thing that is. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It takes three to four years to establish a working vineyard . If you follow the French protocol , ( there&amp;rsquo ; s another one of those words ) , it takes a minimum of seven years before you can make a respectable wine , and 12 to 15 years before you can produce a Grand Vin , a term they use to describe their best wines . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a world that moves too fast in so many ways , vineyards offer a chance to slow down &amp;mdash ; whether you work in them , walk through them or even merely enjoy a glass of wine that can be traced to a specific site . It can be comforting in many ways , especially at these times . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the days ahead , as we stick close to home and our lives slow down , a glass of wine could turn out to be the highlight of our day . At the very least , it&amp;rsquo ; s time we could spend away from the doom and gloom of COVID-19 news sweeping across the globe . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For those who look for silver linings , it is possible wine could be reborn during this crisis . It is no secret its been struggling in the marketplace from challenges by spirits , cocktails , hard seltzers and abstainers. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The current rage is White Claw , a flavoured drink whose ingredients include seltzer , a gluten-free alcohol base , and a variety of refreshing fruit flavours made from natural fruit juice . So far , the flavours include mango , black cherry , lime , ruby red grapefruit , or raspberry &amp;mdash ; and it&amp;rsquo ; s flying off the shelves . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If you reread the previous paragraph and think about wine ( and perhaps many of the columns on this page over the last two or three years ) it will be no surprise White Claw and its ilk are so popular . It is a product responding to young consumer&amp;rsquo ; s wants , and a growing number of voices in the wine community are seeking more transparency about what ingredients are in our wine and where it the product originates . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We have been touting refreshing , red-fruited wines , as opposed to black fruits . We have talked about the need for lower alcohol wines , and we have expressed plenty of concern over origin and appellations , and that the notion of place is where we should be headed . Add in sustainable production , organics , biodynamics and diversity , and you understand the challenge facing an industry working on a three to seven-year model . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Just a look at the growing number of vegans and vegetarians in the marketplace . In what world are they going to order a piece of meat to temper the tannins or alcohol in their red wine ? Gluten-free is another example where wineries that make gluten-free wine , or at least wine with levels that fall below global standards ( which is the vast majority of producers ) , do not go to the trouble telling anyone on the label . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Each of these issues that are important to consumers has been given short shrift by many wineries , leaving them particularly vulnerable to an active , nimble refreshment market that can turn out a new drink on a dime. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s not the end of the world for wine , just as COVID-19 will not be the end of the world for humanity , but there will be lessons to be learned along the way , including changing just a bit , as opposed to the living in the past . Even slow and steady can be overwhelmed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the meantime , consider supporting your local wineries and buy direct . They will ship it right to your door , which may be the safest way to enjoy a glass of wine this month . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;6630;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;6672;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;6708;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;6739;TOOLONG ... about B.C. **26;6775;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **28;6803;TOOLONG " &gt;S ... our quarterly wine and food **32;6833;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Weekend wine picks&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;6867;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;6903;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Selbach Riesling ( Fish Label ) 2017 , Mosel , Germany&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $19.99 90/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 00717215001707 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After another year of the fish label , it&amp;rsquo ; s challenging to find the words to describe just how delicious and unassuming this Riesling is . It is Johannes Selbach , it is ridiculously delicious , and the juicy fruit is backed with the electricity . Back up the truck and drink this all spring and summer , and if you make Riesling in B.C. , this is a beautiful model for an everyday Riesling that maintains its class and standards every year . Incredible value , you can pair it with almost any food . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;6933;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;6969;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; **29;6999;TOOLONG Chardonnay Vintner 's Reserve 2018 , California , United States&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $24.49 90/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 081584013105 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Winemaker Randy Ullom does an admirable job with this label that graphically is even more boring than its Vintner&amp;rsquo ; s Reserve moniker . That said , it&amp;rsquo ; s a wine that has evolved over the decade and now is very reflective of the new California Chardonnay style and its top-flight fruit sourcing . Don&amp;rsquo ; t worry , it is still lush and sweet-smelling with ripe peaches , a touch of caramel candy and buttercream , but it&amp;rsquo ; s the acidity that sets the tone for the palate and its pineapple , peach , Meyer lemon flavours . Pair with lobster , crab , baked salmon or butter chicken . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;7030;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;7066;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Famille J-M Cazes L'Ostal Ros ? 2018 , Languedoc , France&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $15.99 87/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 3760082819903 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Get ready for spring with this pale , pale , pink , with a delightful nose of red berries and flower blossoms . The mix is 50/50 Syrah and Grenache , and the palate is a delicate blend of strawberries and raspberries with a dry , citrus rind finish &amp;mdash ; a fun pink for party appetizers . Good value . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;7096;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;7132;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Hester Creek Block 3 Reserve Cabernet Franc 2017 , Golden Mile Bench , Oliver , Okanagan Valley&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $28.95 89/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 626037001157 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A wet spring was followed by a typical hot , dry Okanagan summer ending in 1422 growing degree days ( GDDs ) , well above the 13-year average of 1054 for the Oliver area . The 50-year-old Franc vines are thinned to one bunch per shoot and fermented for 20 days in stainless steel before aging in a 75/25 mix of French and American oak for one year . Look for an equal blend of vibrant red and black fruits with a peppery , earthy , tobacco leaf underneath , and medium length slightly soupy tannins . Solid and food-friendly . Hester Creek Estate Vineyard Block 3 turned 50 in 2018. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;7162;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;7198;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Croft Vintage Port 2017 , Douro Valley , Portugal&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $125.99 96/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 5602418005292 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Back-to-back vintages in Port is rare as it gets but it will be fun to follow 2016 and 2017 as they head down the road for the next 50-100 years . It&amp;rsquo ; s a highly seductive Croft in 2017 , now fully restored to its former glory under the hands of Fladgate Partnership . Dense , rich , dry and packed with sweet tannins , this juicy , youthful , black and red-fruited port is exotic , intoxicating , and only a baby . One of the rewards of aging is you can better identify those wines that will go the distance but fear not , this is one of them . Climate change challenges notwithstanding , I love this wine , picked on Aug. 31 , the earliest start date for over 70 years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;7228;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;7270;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;7306;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;7337;TOOLONG ... about B.C. **26;7373;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **28;7401;TOOLONG " &gt;S ... our quarterly wine and food **32;7431;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; **27;7465;TOOLONG &lt;img src= " https : **34;7494;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;7530;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Vegan manicotti&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; This satisfying meatless manicotti is from The Buddhist Chef by Jean-Philippe Cyr , a classically trained chef , &lt;a href= " https : **33;7560;TOOLONG ... and practising Buddhist who lives in Quebec . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tofu ricotta&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 ( 350 g ) block extra-firm tofu&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/4 cup ( 15 g ) nutritional yeast&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 tbsp ( 45 mL ) olive oil , divided&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tbsp ( 15 mL ) maple syrup&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 clove garlic , minced&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) lemon juice&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) dried basil&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) dried oregano&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) salt&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Black pepper&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Add all the ingredients to the bowl of a food processor . Pulse to coarsely grind . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **38;7595;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;8 manicotti pasta&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 tbsp ( 45 mL ) olive oil , divided&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 onion , minced&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 ( 5 oz/142 g ) package baby spinach&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 eggplant&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;7635;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;6 cups ( 1.5 L ) tomato sauce&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Cook the manicotti according to the package instructions . Preheat the oven to 375&amp;deg ; F ( 190&amp;deg ; C ) . &amp;nbsp ; In a skillet over medium heat , 1 1&amp;frasl ; 2 tbsp ( 22.5 mL ) oil , then add the onions and sweat for 4 minutes . &amp;nbsp ; Add the baby spinach and keep cooking for 2 minutes . Add the spinach mixture to the tofu ricotta . Stir to combine . Set aside . Cut the eggplant into thick slices ( 3&amp;frasl ; 4 inch/2 cm ) . Generously season the eggplant slices with salt . In the same skillet over medium heat , heat the remaining 1 1&amp;frasl ; 2 tbsp oil , then add the eggplant slices and fry for 7 minutes , until softened , flipping halfway through . Lay the eggplant slices flat in the bottom of a baking dish . Transfer the tofu ricotta mixture to a piping bag , then fill the manicotti . Set the filled manicotti over the eggplant slices , then pour the sauce over top . Bake for 45 minutes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Makes 4 servings. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Wine match&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vegan manicotti brings a blank cheque to the table flavour-wise , so juice up the dish with a spicy red wine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;7662;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;7698;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Torres Sangre de Toro , Pened ? s , Catalunya , Spain , $15.99&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The mix of dark fruits , dried herbs with a dusting of licorice and curry is ready to lift this vegan dish to another level . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;7728;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;7764;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Cusumano Nero d'Avola , Sicily , Italy , $16.29&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Intense cherry , dark chocolate , peppery , earthy , tobacco and licorice flavours and a touch of substance to this soothing dish . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;7794;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;7836;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;7872;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;7903;TOOLONG ... about B.C. **26;7939;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **28;7967;TOOLONG " &gt;S ... our quarterly wine and food **32;7997;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;8031;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? COVID-19 is taking a bite out of the city 's restaurantshttp : **29;8185;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;8216;TOOLONG ... title= " A collective of nearly two dozen restaurants has united in an initiative they are calling " Breaking Bread " to encourage the community to support independent restaurants , their staff and suppliers during the COVID-19 pandemic . Photo : Courtesy of Breaking Bread " alt= " A collective of nearly two dozen restaurants has united in an initiative they are calling " Breaking Bread " to encourage the community to support independent restaurants , their staff and suppliers during the COVID-19 pandemic . Represented here , from left to right : Caf ? Medina , Juke Fried Chicken , Beetbox , L'Abattoir , Havana , Belgard Kitchen , Wildebeest , Nuba , Heritage Asian Eatery , Origo Club . Photo : Courtesy of Breaking Bread " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;8252;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1240057 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; While we&amp;rsquo ; re all hunkered down at home , self-isolating during the coronavirus pandemic , some of our most vulnerable neighbours could well be going hungry . And in a bitter twist of fate , they&amp;rsquo ; re the very people who normally keep us fed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a very scary time , &amp;rdquo ; says Robert Belcham , the chef-owner of Campagnolo restaurant and president of the B.C. Chef&amp;rsquo ; s Table Society . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a time of trying to take care of our customers and take care of our people . But we have to take care of our business , too , or there will be no business to come back to . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Restaurants across B.C. started seeing empty tables and cancelled reservations in January when attendance at Lunar New Year plummeted . Since then , it has just gotten progressively , and rapidly , worse as COVID-19 has spread across the planet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s not just the result of people staying home in an attempt to &amp;ldquo ; flatten the curve&amp;rdquo ; of a viral pandemic . Trips , cruises , conferences , trade shows , festivals and other events have all been cancelled , taking with them the customers who would normally be paying the wages of chefs , servers , bartenders , baristas and sommeliers , as well as the people who make the wine , roast the coffee beans and bake the bread . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In other words , the very people who give Vancouver its unique and welcoming flavour . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; In Vancouver , more than most cities , restaurants &lt;em&gt;are&lt;/em&gt; the culture , &amp;rdquo ; says Justin Tisdall , the owner and operator of the fast-casual restaurant Juke Fried Chicken and Beetbox. &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a viable career , to be a server or to be a chef in one of the most passionate food cities in North America . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It all reminds Belcham of 2009 , after &amp;ldquo ; the markets crashed and the world came to an end , it seemed like at the time , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &amp;ldquo ; But this is different . Back then it was just about money . You can&amp;rsquo ; t just throw money at this . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To stem the bloodletting , many restaurateurs have voluntarily and significantly stepped up their already stringent hygiene , service and sanitary practices . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Everyone understands that all of our livelihoods are at stake , so everyone is taking it really seriously , &amp;rdquo ; says James Iranzad , founder of Gooseneck Hospitality , which employs 170 people across five restaurants . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For instance , they&amp;rsquo ; ve always disinfected surfaces ; now they do it much more often , and visibly , so people can see staff spraying door handles and debit machines every few minutes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Everyone who touches food wears gloves when they do so . Staff are sent home if they have even the slightest hint of a sniffle . Tables have been removed so there is more room between them to prevent the virus spreading . Web orders for takeout are being encouraged so people don&amp;rsquo ; t have to wait in a crowd &amp;mdash ; and takeout cutlery is individually wrapped , even if it is less eco-friendly. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; They&amp;rsquo ; re little things but they all add up , &amp;rdquo ; Tisdall says . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s up to us to protect everyone we can . And it&amp;rsquo ; s changing every day , so we might have to change how we deal with it . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; All of this comes on the heels of several challenging years for the restaurant industry . Just in the last year , some 20 restaurants have closed , including favourites like West and Mamie Taylor&amp;rsquo ; s , falling prey to wave after wave of soaring costs for food , labour , taxes and real estate . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; And then something like this , &amp;rdquo ; Belcham says . &amp;ldquo ; People don&amp;rsquo ; t realize how tight the margins are . Especially in the last couple of years &amp;mdash ; it&amp;rsquo ; s been getting worse and worse and worse . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And however COVID-19 plays out , an enormous number of restaurant workers will be affected . According to 2018 figures from Restaurants Canada , 191,900 people work across some 14,500 restaurants , bars and caterers in B.C. This comprises seven per cent of B.C.&amp;rsquo ; s workforce &amp;mdash ; and a quarter of workers between the ages of 15 and 24. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Those numbers increase significantly when resort , tourism and hotel workers are included . And that has the B.C. Hospitality Foundation increasingly concerned , especially since the organization relies on people in the industry for the donations they use as last-resort funding for hospitality workers dealing with medical issues . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We were not prepared for this . I don&amp;rsquo ; t think anyone was prepared for this , &amp;rdquo ; says Dana Lee Harris , BCHF executive director . &amp;ldquo ; British Columbia has a vibrant and close-knit hospitality community and &amp;hellip ; we need to make sure these people have income . We will do our very best to help hospitality workers that reach out to us and need help financially when they are hit with a financial crisis because of a health condition . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Those interested in offering donations should visit the website at &lt;a href= " http : **29;8282;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;8313;TOOLONG ... . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;How to help&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; There are other ways to support local restaurants , and the people who work for them , too . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;If you feel well , and you 're comfortable going out : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Dine out at your favourite independent restaurants . Yes , the chains are hurting , too , but their owners have a bigger financial cushion to protect themselves . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; In fact , about two dozen independent restaurants have united in an initiative called Breaking Bread ( &lt;a href= " http : //breakingbreadvan.com " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **30;8346;TOOLONG &; ... ) , which is offering special prix fixe menus from March 14 to 31 , hoping to attract diners ( dine in or take out ) so they can continue to pay staff and suppliers . They assure the public that they are doing everything possible to implement all guidelines set out by such regulatory bodies as Vancouver Coastal Health , the World Health Organization and Centres for Disease Control . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;If you 're ill or do n't feel comfortable going out , you can still help : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Buy gift cards from independent restaurants now to use later ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Order their food for takeout or delivery ( that includes the special Breaking Bread menus ) ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Sign up for restaurant newsletters to stay on top of what&amp;rsquo ; s happening ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Support and promote restaurants on social media ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Most of all , be kind . &lt;/p&gt; Joanne SasvariMon , 16 Mar 2020 20:09:30 **45;8378;TOOLONG ... NewsMeet the chef : Claire Livia Lassam is living her bakery dream at LIVIAhttp : **35;8425;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;8462;TOOLONG ... title= " Claire Livia Lassam is living her bakery dream at LIVIA. " alt= " Claire Livia Lassam is living her bakery dream at LIVIA. " data-portal-copyright= " handout " **28;8498;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1224821 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Baker and blogger , Claire Livia Lassam has dreamed of opening her own bakery ever since she was eight years old . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; She got her first job as a prep cook at the age of 17 and has completely immersed herself in food ever since . Claire has done just about every job possible in a restaurant and loved almost every moment of it . With husband and business partner Jordan Pires , she opened LIVIA last year on Commercial Drive &amp;mdash ; right in their own neighbourhood . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What motivates and inspires you as a chef ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; I&amp;rsquo ; m deeply motivated by produce . My husband Jordan is always laughing at me at the farmer&amp;rsquo ; s markets , because I leave grinning like a lunatic , with bushels of berries , and fruit , and greens . I&amp;rsquo ; m almost obsessive about canning . I&amp;rsquo ; m constantly searching for new ways to keep the sunny flavours of summer through the grey months . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : How would you describe the type of food you like to cook ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; You know that old Chanel quote about looking in the mirror before you leave the house , and taking off one piece of jewelry ? That&amp;rsquo ; s how I cook . Growing up my mom rarely made a meal with more than five ingredients in it , and I think of that all the time . I always try to pare food down to it&amp;rsquo ; s most important elements . If you buy great ingredients , your job is to make them shine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What might diners not know about you ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; My happy place is a peach orchard . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Describe a couple of your most recent creations . &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; We&amp;rsquo ; ve had so much fun filling danishes lately with fruit we processed last summer , apricots , peaches , plums . We&amp;rsquo ; ve been nestling them into the pastry with a chamomile pastry cream , and I&amp;rsquo ; m so pleased with how they&amp;rsquo ; ve turned out . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What 's your favourite local product and how do you use it ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; There are so many ! Lately we&amp;rsquo ; ve been braising leeks from Cropthorne Farm and mixing them with ricotta and making galettes topped with pine nuts . But in another month , my answer will be different . I could never chose just one . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : If there 's one important piece of advice you might have for home cooks , what might that be ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; Home cooks are always telling me how intimidated they are by baking , because it&amp;rsquo ; s a science . But I think that&amp;rsquo ; s why you should try to bake . Rather than focus on what can go wrong , focus on how there&amp;rsquo ; s a reason for everything in baking . If you do it properly , it always works . It&amp;rsquo ; s so satisfying , definitely worth using measuring spoons. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;8528;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;8564;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; **33;8594;TOOLONG Buckwheat **30;8629;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; 7 oz ( 200 g ) dark chocolate &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;frac12 ; cup ( 125 mL ) butter &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 4 eggs , separated &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; ? cup ( 160 mL ) brown sugar &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; ? cup ( 80 mL ) cream &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 1 tbsp ( 15 mL ) vanilla &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; ? cup ( 160 mL ) almond flour &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; ? cup ( 80 mL ) buckwheat flour &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Preheat oven to 400&amp;deg ; F ( 205&amp;deg ; C ) . Line an 8-inch round cake with parchment paper . Do not grease the sides of the pan ! Measure chocolate into a large bowl . In a small pot bring the butter to a roaring boil . Pour the butter over the chocolate , let sit for a minute or so , then whisk until smooth . &amp;nbsp ; In a standing mixer fitted with the whisk attachment , mix together the egg whites and half of the brown sugar on medium speed until stiff peaks form . &amp;nbsp ; Transfer egg whites to a separate bowl . In the standing mixer bowl , add the remaining sugar and the egg yolks . Mix on high speed until it is thick and pale , about 7 minutes . &amp;nbsp ; Add cream , vanilla , almond flour , and buckwheat flour to the chocolate mixture . &amp;nbsp ; Fold in the egg yolk mixture . &amp;nbsp ; Fold in the egg white mixture . &amp;nbsp ; Stir until just combined , then pour into the prepared cake pan . &amp;nbsp ; Bake for about 30 minutes , or until the top has domed up and begun to crack . &amp;nbsp ; Allow to cool completely , then run a knife around the edge to loosen it . &amp;nbsp ; Turn out onto a counter , and serve within 2 days . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Randy ShoreSat , 14 Mar 2020 18:00:59 **45;8661;TOOLONG ... Gismondi : B.C. sustainable certification a step in the right directionhttp : **31;8708;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;8741;TOOLONG ... title= " B.C. Certified Sustainable Wine could hit the shelves as early as January , 2021. " alt= " B.C. Certified Sustainable Wine could hit the shelves as early as January , 2021. " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;8777;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1233613 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The wine world has left Vancouver and not a moment too soon for many travellers trying to make their way home through the coronavirus haze . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Most of the world&amp;rsquo ; s prominent trade shows &amp;mdash ; including VinItaly , Prowein , Raw Wine London and the Grands Jour de Bourgogne &amp;mdash ; were cancelled or postponed while 162 wineries were in the city . It left local organizers and benefactors in the very fortunate position of pulling off the 42nd annual winefest just in time . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Will the three-month ( or maybe more ) moratorium on trade shows trigger a rethink of how the wine trade conducts its business worldwide ? The model is ancient and its carbon footprint is vast , and for an industry big on sustainability perhaps it behooves all of us to spend the next three months ( while we are in effect quarantined at home ) , to sit down and think what the next 50 years of wine could look like if we put the planet first . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Climate change has forced a lot of us to think about the future and how we might live better on this Earth , but it would appear the time to change how we exist , maybe even survive on this planet is fast approaching . Who knows , maybe some good will come of the coronavirus as it exposes the many weaknesses we are a slave to because we loathe the thought of change . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There is some promising news on the home front . Very shortly , British Columbia wine drinkers will be able to quickly identify bottles made with people and the planet in mind . Beginning April 1 , earth-friendly vineyards and wineries will take stock of their sustainability efforts and apply for certification , with B.C. Certified Sustainable Wine hitting the shelves as early as January , 2021. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The new certification was developed by volunteer Sustainable Winegrowing B.C. committee&amp;nbsp ; ( SWBC ) members , representing all aspects of the B.C. wine industry . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; When we started promoting sustainable practices in 2008 , we knew the wine industry was going to grow , &amp;rdquo ; said SWBC chairwoman Karen Gillis , vineyard operations manager for Andrew Peller Ltd. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; SWBC has become a hub for wine industry knowledge , sharing best farming practices that contribute positively to the land , our neighbours and our economy . Certification is the next step . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Criteria to become a certified vineyard or winery will be comprehensive , including high standards for water conservation , ecological diversity , soil health , worker safety , social benefit , and more . More important , a third-party will audit participants who will be required to track their progress , and must continually improve to stay certified . So far , so good . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Jane Campardo of Okanagan Crush Pad , has been volunteering for eight years with SWBC. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; After a decade of leadership , our wine industry is ready to game up to the next level . We are holding ourselves accountable to some of the most rigorous sustainability standards in the world . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We listened to growers and wineries to make our certification process as meaningful and easy-to-use as possible . It&amp;rsquo ; s set up so that the smallest family business or the largest multi-holding winery can participate . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The certification process applies to both vineyards that grow grapes and wineries that make wine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For a wine to showcase the B.C. Certified Sustainable Wine logo on the bottle , it must be made with at least 50 per cent certified grapes ( until 2025 , when it increases to 75 per cent ) , and the winery must also have passed the auditing process . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It is a start &amp;mdash ; but , seriously , half sustainable is half unsustainable . One hundred per cent sustainable should be the goal , and no herbicides and no pesticides should be the end game as soon as possible . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I think the high price of B.C. wine demands nothing less . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;8807;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;8849;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;8885;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;8916;TOOLONG ... about B.C. **26;8952;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Weekend wine picks&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;8980;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;9016;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Ken Forrester Old Vine Reserve Chenin Blanc 2018 , Stellenbosch , Coastal Region , South Africa&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $21.99 89/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 6009607660383 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Reserve comes off 39-year-old bush vines from six different vineyards around Stellenbosch . The style is neat and clean , with a lot of intensity of flavour from nectarines , pears and a touch of quinine . Fresh , light , bright , and electric , it is a home run for Chenin Blanc lovers who like their Chenin on the cool , medicinal , citrus side . A delicious food-friendly white for all seasons . It is entirely wild fermented , and only 20 per cent of the French oak it spends nine months aging in is new . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;9046;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;9082;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Domaine Daniel Rion &amp; Fils Bourgogne Aligot ? 2017 , Burgundy , France&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $24.99 89/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 3416110172204 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Aligot&amp;eacute ; is the third grape of Burgundy , often called the third cousin of the more profitable Chardonnay . That said , it&amp;rsquo ; s been planted in Burgundy for over 200 years , and the variety is at its best in the wines of the regional Bourgogne Aligot&amp;eacute ; appellation . At Rion , the Aligot&amp;eacute ; vines are nudging 16 years old , planted over gravelly , silty loams and clays . Aligot&amp;eacute ; is always a skinny wine but in the right way , with fresh acidity that perks up the palate , a touch of lees , and a lemony , green apple bright , refreshing finish . This laser-sharp white is excellent with creamy seafood and pasta dishes . Love the value here . Could we do this in B. C. ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;9112;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;9148;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Cap ? anes Peraj Petita 2017 , Montsant , Catalunya , Spain&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $25.99 I 89/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 8427154011805 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A kosher wine may have never tasted so good . This simple , well-made red is half Grenache with varying amounts of Tempranillo , Merlot and Syrah . The attack is floral and fresh with soft , velvety red fruits &amp;mdash ; a surprisingly pleasing hedonistic style that is well suited to slightly spicy food . It was perfect with a vegetable and pork stir fry . Kosher for Passover . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;9178;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;9214;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Altesino Rosso di Montalcino 2016 , Tuscany , Italy&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $27.99 90/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 662255300337 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Simplicity can be strangely sophisticated when you pull it off . In the case of this rosso , the fruit is all Sangiovese that is only aged for seven months in Slavonian oak barrels followed by three months in bottle before heading to the market three full years before its big brother Brunello will arrive . Lightish in colour and alcohol , Altesino is about elegance . We love its red and black fruits , harmony , drinkability and its fresh , mouth-watering style that complements a wide variety of foods . A classic Euro luncheon wine you can serve with creamy pasta dishes , roasted chicken , barbecued sausages and or pizza . Ready to drink . Superb value , stock up . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;9244;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;9280;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Domaine Ollier Taillfer Grande Reserve Faugeres 2015 , Languedoc , France&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $32.99 90/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 3577075229386 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Domaine Ollier Taillefer&amp;rsquo ; s oldest Carignan vines go into this delicious , balanced red . The mix is Grenache , Syrah and a dash of Mourv&amp;egrave ; dre , grown organically on schist soils . The attack is all red berries , particularly silky raspberries with a stony mineral underside , dusting of garrigue , and a round , soft , long supple finish . Perfect drinking now but will no doubt age five to seven years effortlessly . Lamb , pigeon , mushrooms all work here . Excellent value and real wine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;9310;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;9352;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;9388;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;9419;TOOLONG ... about B.C. **26;9455;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Recipe match&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Green Shashuka , with all its ingredients and eggs , is a tough one , but we suggest off-dry Prosecco as the wine that can get it done . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;9483;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;9519;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Villa Teresa Prosecco Vino Frizzante Organic N/V , Veneto , Italy , $18.99&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rich yellow apple , dried pear mix with faint cider notes . It should be just the right tonic for this dish and its cumin flavouring . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;9549;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;9585;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Anna Spinato Prosecco Organic N/V , Veneto , Italy , $17.99&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Pear , honey , nutty , candied grapefruit flavours round out this Prosecco that will wake up this dish with a jolt of fun and just enough sweetness to stand up to the spice . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;9615;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;9657;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;9693;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;9724;TOOLONG ... about B.C. **26;9760;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;9788;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? Gismondi : B.C. wine of the week , wine to cellar and calendar itemshttp : **35;9942;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;9979;TOOLONG ... title= " The Naramata Inn , built 113 years ago by the town 's founder , J.M. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;10015;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1229478 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Exciting news surrounding the Naramata Inn , built 113 years ago by the town&amp;rsquo ; s founder , J.M. Robinson . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The historic property has been acquired by a quartet of British Columbians with wide-ranging hospitality experience : Culinary talent Chef Ned Bell , known for his seafood activism ; Kate Colley , a highly-experienced communications specialist with a passion for wellness ; Maria Wiesner , who brings her love for design , landscaping and gardens ; and Paul Hollands , retired CEO of A&amp;amp ; W Food Services of Canada , will lead the business charge . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hollands wants to help build a smaller scale , hyper-focused food service and hospitality venture that has the potential to entice guests while making a difference in the local community , and British Columbia as a whole . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s a landmark that deserves a resurrection . Who knows , maybe it could serve as a touchpoint for the wines of Naramata to finally tell the story of its newly-minted sub GI , or appellation. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; The 26th Annual Spring Okanagan Wine Festival presented by Valley First runs April 30 to May 17. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Two signature events will kickoff the Festival : Best of Varietal Wine Awards and reception on April 30 in Penticton , and the Valley First Spring Wine Tastings on May 2 in Kelowna. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There are also winery events scheduled throughout the festival . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For tickets and a look at the lineup go to &lt;a href= " https : **27;10045;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **30;10074;TOOLONG &; ... . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Some early favourites include Best Paella in the Okanagan ( Da Silva ) , Elemental Ensemble ( La Frenz ) , &amp;nbsp ; and The Perfect Pairing of Wine and Cheese ( Upper Bench Estate Winery &amp;amp ; Creamery ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;B.C. wine of the week&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;10106;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;10142;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Nk'Mip Cellars Mer'r'iym White Meritage 2017 , Okanagan Valley&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $27.99 91/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 00063657037881 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One of the best Canadian white wines we have tasted this year , and its in select B.C. Liquor Stores . The blend is 62/33 Sauvignon Blanc/S&amp;eacute ; millon , and it&amp;rsquo ; s as bright and fresh as they come . The attack has a beautiful floral , herbal quality with bright acidity and crispness balanced by a long , creamy , luxurious texture . The S&amp;eacute ; millon is barrel-fermented in new oak , but this merely tempers the electric Sauvignon fruit that gives this wine its life . You can age this for a decade , but many will be rightfully tempted to drink it now while it is fresh and delicious . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Wine for the cellar&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;10172;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;10208;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Antinori Tignanello 2015 , Tuscany , Italy&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $125.99 97/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 8001935124504 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A great Tignanello , and certainly one to layaway if you are a collector . As a chief winemaker , Renzo Cotarella so aptly says , &amp;ldquo ; the place penetrates the wine . &amp;rdquo ; In 2015 , warm days and cool nights were a steady pattern during the growing season , leading to fully ripe , dense , almost sweet black fruit with excellent acidity . It delivers the vibrancy and verticality you expect from Tignanello Sangiovese with licorice and minerality . The tannins are highly polished and would suggest you could drink this now , but I say wait a few years to give this wine a chance to really show off . Undervalued among great wines . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;10238;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? Food ReviewsStaff BlogsSide Dishes : The Great Chocolate Showdownhttp : **32;12269;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;12303;TOOLONG ... title= " The Great Chocolate Showdown judges Cynthia Stroud , Anna Olson and Steven Hodge . " alt= " The Great Chocolate Showdown judges Cynthia Stroud , Anna Olson and Steven Hodge . " data-portal-copyright= " Jeremy Kohm " **28;12339;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1232467 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; They make me feel like a great Canadian loser , but I&amp;rsquo ; m also wowed by the home bakers in The Great Chocolate Showdown ( Food Network Canada ) competing to be ultimate chocolate master and taking home a hefty chunk of cash . On each episode , they are tasked with learning a chocolate technique and applying it to a challenging creation . Baking a cake is just rote to them as they master a mirror-glaze to turn it into edible jewelry , or learn to chocolate pan ( enrobing dried fruits , nuts , etc. in chocolate by tossing around in a pan of chocolate ) and as part of a &amp;ldquo ; spectacle cake that wows&amp;rdquo ; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The judges are West Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s Steven Hodge of the ultra-refined Temper Chocolate and Pastry , as well as well-known Food Network personality Anna Olson and award-winning British cake designer Cynthia Stroud . They teach a technique and challenge contestants to make it part of a larger creation . I love discovering the how-tos of working with chocolate as a viewer . No competing , no time limit to scare me. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The show airs on Tuesdays at 9 p.m. , with the finale on March 24 . Go make a painted chocolate bon bon ! &lt;/p&gt; Mia StainsbyTue , 10 Mar 2020 18:00:56 **45;12369;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsAnthony Gismondi : Coronavirus impacts Vancouver wine festivalhttp : **32;12416;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;12450;TOOLONG ... title= " Coronavirus has impacted the show , with several principles stuck in Italy and Croatia . " alt= " Coronavirus has impacted the show , with several principles stuck in Italy and Croatia . " data-portal-copyright= " CARLOS JASSO " **28;12486;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1228291 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Today&amp;rsquo ; s column was filed on the go at the 42nd Vancouver International Wine Festival . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Although the big International Tasting Room had not opened yet , ( more on that buzz next week ) , a lot was happening across the city . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Coronavirus has impacted the show , with several principles stuck in Italy and Croatia . I&amp;rsquo ; m sure it&amp;rsquo ; s just the beginning of travel troubles for the global wine business . From wine shows to tourists , it is going to be a challenging year for wine producers and consumers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Here is some news in brief : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; The French wineries in the city to headline the festival are rightfully concerned about coronavirus , since many have significant interests in China as an export market , and from all accounts that market is essentially closed with little or no business being conducted . One producer told me they just opened an office in China and the entire team is sitting around at home . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt; * The festival kicked off , as always , with its Bacchanalia Gala Dinner and Auction held at the Fairmont Hotel Vancouver . The nine wine multi-course dinner was the backdrop for both live and silent auctions that eventually tallied more than $233,000 for the festival 's charitable beneficiary , Bard on the Beach . The sold-out raffle for two Delta One international business class tickets , donated by Delta Air Lines , raised an additional $20,000 . The total money raised for the performing arts was on target cross the $10 million mark . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;* Michel and Tina Gassier hosted an exceptional dinner at Hawksworth Restaurant , featuring several Domaine Gassier wines from their home estate in Costiere di Nimes in the southern Rhone Valley . Gassier spoke mostly about a sense of place and his philosophy about winemaking . He likens the effect of terroir or its print on the wine to " a musical score , and it is always deeper , more complex and more interesting than one created by man . The winemaker needs to learn to understand it and nourish it , to let its vibrato resonate through the wine . Later , much like a conductor , the winemaker can give free rein to his creativity through the art of blending . " You will find most of the Gassier in private wine shops and , like most of the dinner attendees , you will be shocked by their quality and value . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; At Chambar Restaurant , the crack sommelier staff and chef Nico Schuermans and his team pulled off a fabulous Ventous Tour de Feast , hosting Fr&amp;eacute ; d&amp;eacute ; ric Chaudiere of Ch&amp;acirc ; teau Pesqui&amp;eacute ; . Pesqui&amp;eacute ; is situated on the hills of Ventoux on the southeast side of the Rhone Valley . Chaudiere made an unusually large impression on attendees by pouring all his wines in magnums . We learned that there is evidence of vine cultivation at Ch&amp;acirc ; teau Pesqui&amp;eacute ; that dates to 2000 years ago . The recent discovery of a pottery workshop and wine pottery from the Roman era ( 30 B.C. ) in the C&amp;ocirc ; tes du Ventoux Appellation confirms that the region is one of the oldest wine-producing areas in France . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Not to be outdone , Gerard Bertrand led a master class on the Languedoc to just under 100 people from the local trade , who had a chance to hear the passion and that drive that has made Bertrand a significant player in the French wine business today . &amp;nbsp ; With 15 estates and now almost 2500 hectares of vineyard farmed biodynamically , he is the largest producer of biodynamic wine in the world . Bertrand&amp;rsquo ; s home base is Chateau l&amp;rsquo ; Hospitalet , a traditional 16th century building that is now a hotel . It is surrounded by a vineyard , 1,000 hectares of pines , bushland and rocky hillsides . The site is entirely farmed biodynamically , and is a shining example of what can be done in agriculture to protect natural biodiversity . The winery restaurant , L&amp;rsquo ; Art de Vivre , highlights the products of the Languedoc region , accompanied by Bertrand&amp;rsquo ; s growing list of grand cru wines . It&amp;rsquo ; s an oasis you should put on your wine bucket list . You will not be disappointed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;Back to the tasting room ! Vive la France . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;12516;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;12558;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;12594;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;12625;TOOLONG ... about the 2020 Vancouver International Wine **29;12661;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **28;12692;TOOLONG " &gt;2 ... Vancouver International Wine Festival edition of Salut ! &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Weekend wine picks&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;12722;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;12758;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Four Shadows Riesling 2018 , Naramata Bench , Okanagan Valley&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $23 91/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 626990339342 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The 2018 Four Shadows Vineyards Riesling caused a stir with the judges at the 2019 National Wine Awards of Canada , and it remains a star in early 2020 . Delicious fresh , juicy and gulpable best describes this Riesling . The palate opens with candied lemon-lime and peachy , red apple fruit flavours . A vibrant , mineral , stony underbelly finishes long and crisp cleaning the palate in preparation for the next sip &amp;mdash ; tasty , dynamic and perfect with brown sugar , soya , mustard wings . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;12788;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;12824;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Fort Berens Reserve Riesling 2018 , Lillooet&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $23.99 I 88/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 626990248958 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We love that the 2018 is &amp;ldquo ; Estate&amp;rdquo ; Reserve Riesling , i.e. it is all Lillooet VQA fruit , the first official release from the newly minted VQA region . It was fermented in stainless steel on its native yeast , where it spent seven weeks before bottling . The lead is juicy , green apple , bitter lime , light melon , and a dusting of spices . It has a slight belly of lees to pad out the clean , crisp frame . Finishing with that bitter lime note , this would be well suited to sushi or green curries . Very promising . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;12854;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;12890;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;CedarCreek Platinum Block 3 Riesling 2017 , Okanagan Valley&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $29.99 91/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; &amp;nbsp ; 778913800306 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Historically , the Platinum Block Riesling gets two picks . In 2017 there were three in a &amp;ldquo ; platinum-like&amp;rdquo ; attempt to create a complexity of flavours and acidities in the wine . It worked . The style is tight and lean with bright minerality and a fresh , linear palate . Look for peach , orange , ginger , and a delicious , floral , mouth-watering finish . Everything is in balance here with a clean , mineral undercurrent to add a dash of complexity . Drink or hold , there is no rush . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;12920;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;12956;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Upper Bench Merlot Estate 2016 , Naramata Bench , Okanagan Valley&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $41.50 90/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 626990244899 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the 2016 we see the finesse we have been craving from this Merlot . We love the vintage and the acidity that lifts the fruit and lightens the load on the palate . It&amp;rsquo ; s still rich and full-bodied , but the balance and freshness will serve it well five years down the road . In the meantime , enjoy the blue fruit , black cherries , and the glossy Naramata Bench tannins . Cheese anyone ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;12986;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;13022;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Clos du Soleil Signature 2015 , British Columbia&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $39.04 91/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 857088000077 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Signature label is just that at Clos du Soleil . It fits the winery philosophy of pursuing the art of the blend as made famous by vintners of Bordeaux . In 2015 , all the main Bordeaux red grapes were involved , albeit in the Similkameen Valley way . At first glance , it looks like a California red blend with its deep colour and dense tannins , but in the mouth , there is a sense of restraint and balance that speaks to the modest 13.8 alcohol in a hot year when runaway alcohols were the norm . Look for silky cassis , blackberry/raspberry flavours with just a squeeze of acid before the tannins kick in . Drink or hold , but any extra time in your cellar will pay off for this bottle , its life has only just begun . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;13052;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;13094;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;13130;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;13161;TOOLONG ... about the 2020 Vancouver International Wine **29;13197;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **28;13228;TOOLONG " &gt;2 ... Vancouver International Wine Festival edition of Salut ! &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; **27;13258;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Buddhist chef and blogger Jean-Pierre Cyr has assembled a collection of 100 recipes that will make you wonder why you cook with meat at all . Among the very classic dishes in The Buddhist Chef is his vegan riff on Beef Bourguinon , prepared with the meaty non-meat , portabello mushrooms . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;13287;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;13323;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Portabello **26;13353;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 portobello mushrooms , quartered&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 carrots , sliced into rounds&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 onion , thinly sliced&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 tbsp ( 45 mL ) vegetable oil&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) ground cumin&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) salt&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt; ? teaspoon ( 1 mL ) ground allspice&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Black pepper , to taste&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 ? cups ( 375 ml ) red wine&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 cups ( 500 ml ) vegetable broth&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt; ? cup ( 60 ml ) soy sauce&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 tbsp ( 45 mL ) tomato paste&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) maple syrup&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 bay leaves&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tbsp ( 15 mL ) cornstarch&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 tbsp ( 45 mL ) water&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Fresh parsley leaves , to garnish&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Preheat the oven to 400&amp;deg ; F ( 205&amp;deg ; C ) . In a large ovenproof pot , combine the mushrooms , carrots , onions , oil , cumin , salt , allspice , and black pepper . Roast for 30 minutes . Transfer to the stove , then stir in the red wine . Simmer for 5 minutes over medium-high heat . Add the vegetable broth , soy sauce , tomato paste , maple syrup , and bay leaves . Bring to a boil , lower the heat , and simmer for 15 minutes . In a small bowl , combine the cornstarch with the water . Add the cornstarch mixture to the pot , stirring constantly . Bring back to a boil , then remove from the heat as soon as the sauce has thickened ( about one minute ) . Remove the bay leaves . Garnish with parsley and enjoy on its own or with pasta or rice . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Makes two hearty servings. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Wine match&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Stick with the same red wine used in your bourguignon recipe . Never cut corners on cooking wine ; if you don&amp;rsquo ; t want to drink it , don&amp;rsquo ; t put it in the food . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;13381;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;13417;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Bartier Bros . Merlot Cerqueira Vineyard 2018 , Okanagan Valley , $21.99&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The density of this Merlot is always impressive . Creamy and silky , naturally dry , savoury , desert blue fruits . Love the mineral notes and salt that cleans up the finish . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;13447;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;13483;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Hester Creek Character 2018 , Golden Mile Bench , Okanagan Valley , $20.95&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The flavours are a bright mix of dark , savoury fruit , dried herbs with a dusting of vanilla . You can drink this now , and the winery suggests you do it with smoked ribs or pulled pork . Excellent value . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;13513;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;13555;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;13591;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;13622;TOOLONG ... about the 2020 Vancouver International Wine **29;13658;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **28;13689;TOOLONG " &gt;2 ... Gismondi : B.C. wine of the week , wine to cellar and calendar itemshttp : **35;13873;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;13910;TOOLONG ... title= " Thierry Busset is celebrating Mardi Gras with a month-long promotion that pays tribute to his mother by recreating her signature recipe for the sweet dough French confection beignets. " alt= " Thierry Busset is celebrating Mardi Gras with a month-long promotion that pays tribute to his mother by recreating her signature recipe for the sweet dough French confection beignets. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;13946;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1223841 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The annual Fetes des Beignets is upon us , and maitre chocolatier and chef patissier Thierry Busset is celebrating Mardi Gras with a month-long promotion that pays tribute to his mother by recreating her signature recipe for the sweet dough French confections that became a childhood favourite . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It is not too late to take in Thierry Chocolate&amp;rsquo ; s Mardi Gras offerings that include half a dozen French beignets made &amp;agrave ; la minute and served with a choice of house made chocolate , raspberry or caramel dipping sauces ( until March 19 ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thierry Chocolates is located at 1059 Alberni Street , Vancouver , and is open from 7 a.m. to 11:30 p.m. on weekdays and 8 a.m. to 11:30 p.m. on weekends . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; The price of wine in the United States is at an all-time low due to an abundance of grapes , and some growers are choosing to leave grapes on the vine because it costs less than making the wine according to a new report from the Silicon Valley Bank Financial Group . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The oversupply of grapes isn&amp;rsquo ; t a reflection of the quality of the wine , but that the wine industry is increasingly missing the mark on consumer expectations . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rob McMillan who wrote the report , told industry types they should be concerned . &amp;ldquo ; We aren&amp;rsquo ; t engaging with the millennial consumer , and boomers who have driven wine sales for the last 30 years won&amp;rsquo ; t live forever . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He suggests better value wines could lead to millennials becoming &amp;ldquo ; more consistent wine buyers , &amp;rdquo ; but for now , the wine supply chain is full . McMillan thinks U.S. wine consumers will discover unprecedented retail value in 2020 through 2022 and should buy up . The lower prices may even last up to three years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;B.C. wine of the **28;13976;TOOLONG &lt;img src= " https : **34;14006;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;14042;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Stag 's Hollow Renaissance Meritage 2016 , Okanagan Valley&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $45 92/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 626990057321 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Wow , what a treat , and a further confirmation that 2016 is the best vintage we have experienced in the modern B.C. wine era . Clean , supple , and simply delicious to drink , it has the balance to go the distance but you can enjoy it now &amp;mdash ; a rare combination . From dark chocolate to red and black fruits to silky tannins , it&amp;rsquo ; s all here but in a tighter package that leans toward complexity rather than pure hedonism . It is already an excellent dinner wine that can take on most major meat dishes or a favourite cheese . It is 76 per cent Merlot , 13 per cent Cabernet Sauvignon , 11 per cent Cabernet Franc . Well done . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Wine for the cellar&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;14072;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;14108;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Col d'Orcia Brunello di Montalcino 2013 , Montalcino , Siena , Tuscany , Italy&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $55.99 92/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 8016760002446 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Orcia River marks the southwest border of the Brunello di Montalcino region . It sits below the hillside vineyard and winery facing south/southwest that bears its name . The nose is complex , earthy and aromatic , with mushrooms , cedar , tobacco supporting scents of bold cherries . The palate is a similar affair , with intense black cherry flavours flecked with spice and just a hint of oak . The tannins are already softening at six years old , making it drinkable , but there is no rush given its undeniable Brunello power in the finish . It will age for decades . Brunello di Montalcino by law must be 100 per cent Sangiovese . Grilled smoky meats or mushroom risotto is the ticket here . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;14138;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **28;14180;TOOLONG " &gt;S ... our quarterly wine and food **32;14210;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;14244;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;14280;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;14311;TOOLONG ... about B.C. **26;14347;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;14375;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ?
