Email &lt;a href= " **28;14404;TOOLONG " **46;14434;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Anthony GismondiWed , 04 Mar 2020 19:00:54 **45;14482;TOOLONG ... review : Atlas Steak + Fish in Langleyhttp : **33;14529;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;14564;TOOLONG ... title= " Josper Smoked Grilled Salmon Steak at Atlas Steak + Fish Langley . " alt= " Josper Smoked Grilled Salmon Steak at Atlas Steak + Fish Langley . " data-portal-copyright= " Aleesha Harris " **28;14600;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1223413 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Atlas Steak + Fish Langley&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; 20393 Fraser Highway , Langley &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Open for dinner daily &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;More info : &lt;/strong&gt; Atlassteakandfish.com ; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " **29;14630;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; Decadent . That was the first word that came to mind when looking to summarize a recent visit to Atlas Steak + Fish Langley . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Owned by Gateway Casinos &amp;amp ; Entertainment , the dining destinations &amp;mdash ; there are Atlas eateries in Langley , Burnaby , Kamloops and Edmonton &amp;mdash ; were designed to provide &amp;ldquo ; exceptional food and memorable dining experiences&amp;rdquo ; for casino-goers and the &amp;ldquo ; surrounding community , &amp;rdquo ; according to Todd Pollock , the vice president of food and beverage at the company . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; From the upscale , almost Vegas-like decor , to the addictive cheesy side dishes ( that we accidentally ordered seemingly en masse for our meal ) , the experience was undeniably rich . Which is to say that it was pretty much exactly what one would expect from a new take on a classic steak house . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The atmosphere is fun , engaging and casually elegant &amp;mdash ; and we take the same approach with our food , &amp;rdquo ; Pollock says . &amp;ldquo ; Our appeal is broad and we&amp;rsquo ; re not your traditional steak house . Atlas embraces femininity and you&amp;rsquo ; ll catch it in the details . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We took away the stuffiness of a traditional steak house and we&amp;rsquo ; ve made it more approachable and welcoming . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Langley location , which is part of the Cascades Casino property , appears to be a welcome addition to the property , and the immediate area , offering a definitively more upscale large dining experience than was previously available in the vicinity . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt; " The response has been excellent , " Pollock says of how the eatery has been embraced . " We 're pleased by how well we 've been supported here . People love the menu , the open kitchen concept , the entertainment and the d ? cor . The patio has a retractable roof which is popular during the warmer months . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;14661;TOOLONG ... title= " The open kitchen at Atlas Steak + Fish Langley . " alt= " The open kitchen at Atlas Steak + Fish Langley . " data-portal-copyright= " Aleesha Harris " **28;14697;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1223411 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The decor and detail may bring to mind modernity &amp;mdash ; the wine lists are made available on a tablet screen at your table , after all &amp;mdash ; but the menu , well , it sticks pretty close to the classics . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After ordering , a small hunk of warm white bread , of the pillowy pull-apart variety , was brought to the table in a mini cast iron pan . With Stilton blue cheese butter as an accompaniment , the little cloud of dough was topped with a generous pinch of course salt . It was tasty . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;14727;TOOLONG ... title= " Caesar Salad . " alt= " Caesar Salad . " data-portal-copyright= " Aleesha Harris " **28;14763;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1223409 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sticking close to the theme of traditional steak house selections , we ordered the Caesar Salad ( $14 per person , minimum of two people ) which features romaine hearts , parmegiano-reggiano cheese and &amp;ldquo ; traditional dressing . &amp;rdquo ; Made the &amp;lsquo ; old fashioned&amp;rsquo ; way , with anchovy paste , egg yolk , olive oil and garlic &amp;mdash ; and prepared tableside &amp;mdash ; the salad proved a nice start the meal . While the croutons were sadly unmemorable , the salad was , overall , balanced , bright , crispy and just a touch tart . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Tableside service for salads and entr&amp;eacute ; es is always a showstopper , &amp;rdquo ; Pollock says . &amp;ldquo ; Guests come in for the experience and that&amp;rsquo ; s what Atlas embodies . It&amp;rsquo ; s an escape from the ordinary and we entertain our guests every step of the way . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The tableside preparation yielded a surprisingly generous portion . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Wooden box in hand , our server approached our table with a choice : which knife would we each like for use with our dinner ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; When guests order a steak , the server brings out a box of carefully curated steak knives , &amp;rdquo ; Pollock explains of the table tool selection . &amp;ldquo ; Knives that vary in size , shape and weight . We tailor each experience to the guest dining at the table . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I chose a modest , white-handled knife for my meal , while my dinner companion chose a more weighty serrated option . Preferring not to pause and ponder what our individual knife selections could possibly mean beyond the need to cut into one&amp;rsquo ; s meal , we continued to tear at the small orb of bread ( with fingers , not the aforementioned new knives ) for the main course to arrive . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Josper Grilled Salmon Steak ( $28 ) served with warm potato salad and a Chimichurri sauce saw a sizable portion of salmon , skin on , with a smattering of halved small red potatoes and a smear of the green sauce . The flavour of the fish was fresh and earthy , the smokiness perfectly balanced and not overpowering . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You have to taste it to really understand how good it is , &amp;rdquo ; Pollock says of the dish . &amp;ldquo ; It is hard to beat the quality that comes with using a Josper Oven . Imported from Spain , the Josper cooks with charcoal at a high , dry heat , which produces a beautiful sear on the salmon . It&amp;rsquo ; s a wonderful dish and one we&amp;rsquo ; re proud of . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;14793;TOOLONG ... title= " A selection of dishes during a dinner at Atlas Steak + Fish Langley . " alt= " A selection of dishes during a dinner at Atlas Steak + Fish Langley . " data-portal-copyright= " Aleesha Harris " **28;14829;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1223410 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; While calling the assemblage of potatoes a &amp;lsquo ; potato salad&amp;rsquo ; was a bit of a stretch , their presence on the plate wasn&amp;rsquo ; t lamented . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But , oh , that green sauce . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The herbaceous mixture was delicious and served to add another layer of flavour to the already flavourful fish . Thankfully , the green goodness made a repeat appearance on the steak plate . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The steak , a 10-ounce New York Striploin was a sensation . Tender and perfectly cooked , the cut of meat was lean and lightly seasoned . We decided to forgo the addition of a sauce option , preferring to taste the full flavour of the beef . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Sourcing beef from North America&amp;rsquo ; s top producers , we seek out the ultimate commitment to quality and stewardship to ensure the best in marbling , tenderness , and flavour , &amp;rdquo ; Pollock says of the meats on offer at the Atlas locations . &amp;ldquo ; Our steaks are hand-butchered and aged a minimum of 40 days before being prepared in our mesquite-fired Josper oven . Only Certified Angus Beef and USDA Prime make the cut and are selected for our dishes . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The portion size ensured a good lunch was had the next day , and yes , we were sure to scoop up the last bit of that heavenly Chimichurri sauce , too . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As is the case with many steak house menus , a selection of side dishes is on offer at Atlas . In the mood for some serious cheese , we ordered the Blue Cheese Gnocchi ( $12 ) and the Gorgonzola Mac and Cheese ( $12 ) . The picks were a tad too similar , in hindsight , and the mac and cheese paled in comparison to the gnocchi. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The decadent gnocchi bites played perfectly in the rich sauce . Topped with toasted bread crumbs , it was divine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The Blue Cheese Gnocchi side dish does have a cult following , &amp;rdquo ; Pollock admits of the addictive side dish . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Pollock says the menu will change throughout the year in order to feature more seasonal ingredients &amp;mdash ; though , here&amp;rsquo ; s to hoping that gnocchi doesn&amp;rsquo ; t disappear . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt; " As ingredients are at their freshest and most available , we take advantage . We work closely with our culinary teams to create the most flavourful and distinctive dishes , " Pollock says . " Our spring/summer feature menu is in development now , and we have a series of wine dinners , which will happen throughout the year . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt; " We pair curated menus with a specific winery 's selected offering to produce a truly remarkable dining experience . Food ReviewsKasey Wilson : There 's nothing like a bowl of chili on a cold winter dayhttp : **37;14963;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;15002;TOOLONG ... title= " Jalape ? o peppers and sriracha earn Katie Mitzel 's vegetarian chili its " killer " sobriquet. " alt= " Jalape ? o peppers and sriracha earn Katie Mitzel 's vegetarian chili its " killer " sobriquet. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;15038;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1227495 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; There&amp;rsquo ; s nothing like a warming bowl of chili after coming in from the slopes , and it was a long-ago family ski trip that earned my chili a nickname that has stuck with it to this day . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The day before our spring-break drive to Whistler , I made a hearty dish-in-one chili in my sturdy , retro-yellow enamelled cast-iron Dutch oven . I put it in the fridge overnight and the next day set it beside two boxes of groceries that were bound for the cabin . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; My husband packed the car and we set out , but soon stopped at a neighbour&amp;rsquo ; s house to drop something off . In rearranging the cargo , he set the Dutch oven on the roof of the car &amp;mdash ; and you can guess what happened next . Soon after we pulled out , we heard a loud bang , looked back and saw the pot on the pavement with chili spread everywhere . From that day on , we&amp;rsquo ; ve called it Roof Rack Chili . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Mine is a meaty chili , but you&amp;rsquo ; ll hardly miss the meat in Katie Mitzel&amp;rsquo ; s Killer Vegetarian Chili , one of many appealing recipes in Rocky Mountain Cooking , a cookbook inspired by her 20 years as a backcountry chef . You&amp;rsquo ; ll want to try her one-layer carrot cake with toasted coconut and her loaded baked potato soup , too . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Roof Rack Chili&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Like most highly seasoned dishes , chili tastes better the second day . You can cut this recipe in half , but it 's great to have in the freezer . Serve with garnishes such as shredded cheese ( Monterey Jack , Cheddar or mozzarella ) , diced avocado , chopped red onions , sliced radishes , lime wedges , sour cream and crushed red pepper flakes . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;frac14 ; cup ( 50 mL ) vegetable oil &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 large onions , peeled and chopped &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 4 fat garlic cloves , peeled and minced &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 4 lbs ( 2 kg ) &amp;nbsp ; lean stew meat , cut into small pieces &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 3 lbs ( 1.5 kg ) bulk pork sausage &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 ( 28oz/796 mL ) cans diced tomatoes &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 ( 6oz/156mL ) cans tomato paste &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 3 to 6 tbsp ( 50 mL &amp;mdash ; 100mL ) chili powder &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 3 tsp ( 15 mL ) ground cumin &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 1 tbsp ( 15 mL ) dried oregano &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 ( 14oz/398 ) cans baked beans &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 tsp ( 10 mL ) salt &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 tsp ( 10 mL ) sugar &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) unsweetened cocoa powder &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 3 ( 19oz/540 mL ) cans kidney beans , drained &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a large , heavy saucepan or Dutch oven , heat oil over medium heat . Saut&amp;eacute ; onion and garlic until soft but not brown , about 5 minutes . Add beef and sausage . Cook until meat is brown , breaking up sausage with a wooden spoon . Pour off fat . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Stir in tomatoes , tomato paste , chili powder , cumin , oregano , baked beans , salt , sugar and cocoa powder . Adjust heat and simmer , partially covered , for 2 hours , stirring often . If the chili becomes too dry , add a little water as it cooks , Stir in kidney beans and pinto beans . Cook 30 minutes longer or until the meat is very tender May be refrigerated or frozen . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Makes 16 servings &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;15068;TOOLONG ... title= " Jalape ? o peppers and sriracha earn Katie Mitzel 's vegetarian chili its " killer " sobriquet. " alt= " Jalape ? o peppers and sriracha earn Katie Mitzel 's vegetarian chili its " killer " sobriquet. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;15104;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1227494 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Killer Vegetarian Chili&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;The array of vegetables , beans and seasonings in this chili will have everyone hoping for a little bit more of this delicious one-pot meal . ( Adapted from Rocky Mountain Cooking : Recipes to Bring Canada 's Backcountry Home by Katie Mitzel , Random House , 2019. ) &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) vegetable oil &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 1 large onion , peeled and chopped &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 4 garlic cloves , peeled and minced &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 celery ribs , chopped &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 carrots , peeled and chopped &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 cups ( 500 mL ) sliced button mushrooms &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 1 red bell pepper , seeded and chopped &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 1 yellow bell pepper , seeded and chopped &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 fresh jalape&amp;ntilde ; o peppers , seeded and chopped ( see Kitchen Hack ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 1 cup ( 250 mL ) vegetable stock ( I buy it at The Stock Market on Granville Island ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) white wine vinegar &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 1 tbsp ( 15 mL ) Dijon mustard &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;frac14 ; cup ( 50 mL ) ketchup &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 ( 28oz/796 ml ) cans whole plum tomatoes &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 1 ( 14oz/398 ) can diced tomatoes &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 ( 19oz/540 ) cans&amp;nbsp ; kidney beans , drained and rinsed &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 1 ( 19oz/540mL ) cans black beans , drained and rinsed &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 to 3 tbsp ( 25 mL to 50 mL ) brown sugar &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 tbsp ( 25 mL ) molasses &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;frac14 ; cup ( 50 mL ) chili powder &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 1 tsp ( 5 mL ) each : ground cinnamon , ground cumin and dried thyme leaves &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 3 tbsp ( 50 mL ) sriracha or your favourite hot sauce ( optional ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sour cream and grated Parmigiano-Reggiano cheese for serving &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a large Dutch oven over medium heat , warm the oil . Stir in onion , garlic , celery and carrots and cook until onion becomes translucent , about 5 minutes . Add mushrooms , peppers and stock . Simmer for 8 to 10 minutes and then stir in vinegar , mustard and ketchup . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Meanwhile , dump the plum tomatoes into a bowl and crush them with your hands , removing stems . Add tomatoes with their juices to the pot along with the beans . Stir in brown sugar ( to taste ) , molasses , chili powder , cinnamon , thyme and sriracha . ( The hot sauce is optional , but this is called killer chili for a reason , so I say go for it . ) Using a wooden spoon or long-handled spatula to get under the ingredients , stir the chili , mixing well . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Turn down the heat and cook , uncovered , for 30 to 40 minutes . Taste for seasonings . If desired , add more brown sugar or molasses to cut down the acidity of the tomatoes or a pinch more cinnamon to enhance the deep flavour profile and develop the aromatics . Serve with a dollop of sour cream and garnish with freshly grated Parmigiano-Reggiano. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Serves 6 to 8 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Kitchen Hack : Seeding Chili Peppers&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Using a knife to remove the seeds and ribs from a hot pepper takes a very steady hand . Here&amp;rsquo ; s safer and effective alternative : Cut the chili in half with a knife . Starting opposite the stem end , run the edge of a small melon baller down the inside of the chili , scraping up the seeds and ribs . Cut off the core with the scoop . &lt;/p&gt; Kasey WilsonWed , 04 Mar 2020 19:00:15 **45;15134;TOOLONG ... Vancouver restaurateur John Bishop calls it a careerhttp : **34;15181;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;15217;TOOLONG ... title= " John Bishop and his wife Theresa at their home in Vancouver . " alt= " John Bishop and his wife Theresa at their home in Vancouver . " data-portal-copyright= " Arlen Redekop " **28;15253;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1230676 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bishop&amp;rsquo ; s has been a staple of the Vancouver fine-dining scene since it opened in 1985 . But probably not for much longer . Owner John Bishop is retiring , and if he doesn&amp;rsquo ; t find a buyer soon , the legendary Kitsilano restaurant will close Aug. 1 . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I wrote that letter ( about retiring ) about two years ago , &amp;rdquo ; said Bishop , 75. &amp;ldquo ; It started to occur to me that , &amp;lsquo ; Gee , this is a tough business now . &amp;rsquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; d never had to give much thought to the financial stuff at all , really , but these days ( the costs of ) zoning and property taxes and all that stuff is just mounting up . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; And I thought , &amp;lsquo ; Well , maybe it&amp;rsquo ; s time . &amp;rsquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m fed up with losing sleep every night just because of the ability to pay bills . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When Bishop&amp;rsquo ; s opened at 2183 West 4th in December 1985 , Bishop said rent and property taxes were around $2,000 per month ; now costs are about 10 times that . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We pay a third of the building&amp;rsquo ; s taxes , that&amp;rsquo ; s the biggest chunk we have to fork out , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; And then licensing , all this stuff mounts up . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That said , it&amp;rsquo ; s been a great run . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Aside from the economics of it , I enjoy it probably more than ever , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; Last week I sat with a couple who were so interesting , had travelled so much and we shared a lot in common that way . When I got home I felt like I&amp;rsquo ; d had dinner with them . I hadn&amp;rsquo ; t , I had just sat with them intermittently through the dinner . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; But I phoned her up the next morning to thank her for the wonderful interaction . I&amp;rsquo ; ve been trying to do a little bit more of that , enjoy the whole thing . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Personal interaction like this is part of what made Bishop&amp;rsquo ; s special , a restaurant that retained a loyal clientele where many competitors fell by the wayside . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re not very large &amp;hellip ; 45 seats , &amp;rdquo ; said Bishop . &amp;ldquo ; On a busy night we&amp;rsquo ; ll probably do 70 meals or something like that . Small is good . There&amp;rsquo ; s an intimacy there that&amp;rsquo ; s always been in the restaurant , it&amp;rsquo ; s like entering someone&amp;rsquo ; s home . People say that quite often , I take that as a big compliment . It has been an extension of my home . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;15283;TOOLONG ... title= " John Bishop at his home in Vancouver . " alt= " John Bishop at his home in Vancouver . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;15319;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1230691 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bishop was born in Shrewsbury , England , and grew up in Newtown , Wales . After graduating from chef&amp;rsquo ; s school , he worked in London , England , Wales and Ireland before he was talked into giving Canada a try . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I came to stay 12 months , &amp;rdquo ; he recounts . &amp;ldquo ; A friend of mine got a job in Canada , in Vancouver , and said , &amp;lsquo ; You need a challenge , why don&amp;rsquo ; t you come to Vancouver with me ? &amp;rsquo ; I said , &amp;lsquo ; Oh well , OK . I&amp;rsquo ; m single , why not ? &amp;rsquo ; So I got a visa and started working down in Gastown at an Irish club . They had a ( liquor ) licence that depended on food , and they said , &amp;lsquo ; Just take the kitchen , we don&amp;rsquo ; t want it . &amp;rsquo ; I met my wife there , she was a server . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In 1975 he moved to Umberto&amp;rsquo ; s. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; People I&amp;rsquo ; d gotten to know in the business said , &amp;lsquo ; Oh God no , don&amp;rsquo ; t go there , it&amp;rsquo ; s a sweatshop , a busy , busy place , &amp;rsquo ; &amp;rdquo ; he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But he went and soon was in charge of &amp;ldquo ; what was then the busiest kitchen in Vancouver . &amp;rdquo ; He stayed a decade , but had a falling out with a manager &amp;ldquo ; and ended up on the street . &amp;rdquo ; Umberto invited him back but Bishop decided to strike out on his own . His dentist , Bud Spiko , helped him put together an investment group that raised $80,000 , and Bishop&amp;rsquo ; s was a success out of the box . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Part of its allure was that it was a leader in the locally sourced food movement , helping to forge a distinctive Pacific Northwest cuisine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Traditionally in Vancouver a lot of restaurant themes were reflected by the ownership&amp;rsquo ; s place of birth &amp;mdash ; Italian , Greek , French , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; I wasn&amp;rsquo ; t going to start cooking Welsh cuisine . I&amp;rsquo ; d trained in French cooking . So it was trying to merge ( styles ) , as they did in California , which was a big inspiration to me. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This appealed to both customers ( Oscar Peterson and Goldie Hawn loved the fresh salmon with apple cider sauce ) and young chefs like Rob Feenie , Vikram Vij , Adam Busby and Michael Allemeier , who all worked at Bishop&amp;rsquo ; s before moving on . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; What&amp;rsquo ; s next for Bishop ? He&amp;rsquo ; s working on a new cookbook , and will lead a Journeys with Heart food tour for charity to Scotland in September . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But frankly , he&amp;rsquo ; s not sure how he&amp;rsquo ; ll fill his days once he leaves Bishop&amp;rsquo ; s. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ll miss it , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; I don&amp;rsquo ; t know what I&amp;rsquo ; ll replace it with . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **28;15349;TOOLONG " **35;15379;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; John MackieWed , 04 Mar 2020 01:20:13 **45;15416;TOOLONG ... NewsNewsMeet the chef : Josh Blumenthal 's plates are local and hyperseasonalhttp : **27;15463;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;15492;TOOLONG ... title= " Josh Blumenthal is the chef and founder of The Trincomali Supper Club . " alt= " Josh Blumenthal is the chef and founder of The Trincomali Supper Club . " data-portal-copyright= " handout " **28;15528;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1220538 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chef Josh Blumenthal has dedicated his career to local , sustainable cuisine and exploring the wild , edible landscape of the Pacific Northwest . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Josh began his kitchen career as a porter in Edmonton before moving to the West Coast , where he attended cooking school . He has &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt; worked in Michelin-starred kitchens in &lt;/span&gt; the UK , Scandinavia and Northern California and some of Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s finest restaurants , including West , Wildebeest , and Bishop&amp;rsquo ; s , as well as co-founding Elementa a group that pioneered the city&amp;rsquo ; s gastronomic pop-up scene . After moving to the Southern Gulf Islands , he founded &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt; his first solo &lt;/span&gt; venture The Trincomali Supper Club . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : &lt;/strong&gt; What motivates and inspires you as a chef ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; I am inspired by the cultivated and natural abundance of coastal B.C. and desire to nourish people through delicious interpretations of the surrounding landscape . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : How would you describe the type of food you like to cook ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; Ingredient driven , sustainable , and hyperseasonal. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What might diners not know about you ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; In a former life , I promoted punk shows in my hometown which surprisingly draws a lot of parallels with putting on pop-up dining events . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Describe a couple of your most recent creations . &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; One of my favourite flavour combinations I discovered over the winter is roasted sunchokes paired with medlar paste . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What 's your favourite local product and how do you use it ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; My favourite local product is seaweed and kelp . It is very abundant and adds a depth of flavour that&amp;rsquo ; s unparalleled to almost any dish . I use it as a seasoning when dried and ground up , serve it as a vegetable , make a umami rich infused oil , pickle certain varieties and use is for a flavour base in soups , stocks and sauces. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : If there 's one important piece of advice you might have for home cooks , what might that be ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; The most important thing a home cook can do is source the best ingredients possible from as close to home as possible . That and season and taste your cooking throughout the process ! &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;15558;TOOLONG ... title= " Stinging Nettle Soup , created by Josh Blumenthal of the Trincomali Supper Club . " alt= " Stinging Nettle Soup , created by Josh Blumenthal of the Trincomali Supper Club . " data-portal-copyright= " handout " **28;15594;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1220570 " /&gt; **32;15624;TOOLONG Nettle **30;15658;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; 2.2 lbs ( 1 kg ) Yukon gold potatoes &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 1 medium-sized sweet onion &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 3 tbsp ( 45 mL ) grapeseed oil &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 quarts ( 2 L ) water &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 16 oz ( 500 g ) fresh stinging nettle &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Kosher salt to taste &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Wash nettles and dry in salad spinner . Peel and thinly slice potatoes and onion . In a large , heavy bottomed pot over medium heat , sweat the onion and potato in grapeseed oil until translucent . Add water and let simmer until potatoes and onion are fully cooked . Add nettles and stir to fully submerge Bring the pot to a boil and cook until the nettles are soft , usually a couple minutes after it begins to boil . Season with salt as each ingredient is added ensuring proper integration . Puree the soup and strain through a fine mesh sieve into a metal bowl set over ice . Stir the soup constantly until it cools down to ensure the vibrant green colour is retained . Reheat soup and serve as is . Garnish with freshly steamed clams or kelp oil , if desired . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Makes 6 servings. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Randy ShoreSat , 29 Feb 2020 19:00:17 **45;15690;TOOLONG ... Gismondi : What is up with wine , and where is it heading in 2020 ? http : **35;15737;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;15774;TOOLONG ... title= " It looks like consumers are turning away from wine and seltzers are on the rise , which means wine producers need to start listening to what those consumers want . " alt= " It looks like consumers are turning away from wine and seltzers are on the rise , which means wine producers need to start listening to what those consumers want . " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;15810;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1223825 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s wine week in Vancouver , but not all is well , down on the farm . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Dr. Elisabeth Thach , MW , a distinguished professor of wine&amp;nbsp ; and management at Sonoma State University , posed a question in Business.com recently , that I have been thinking about for a couple of years now , namely , &amp;ldquo ; is wine at the tipping point ? &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; She points to U.S. retail data that suggests &amp;ldquo ; volume is flattening . &amp;rdquo ; Thach goes on to say the wine category trend line is falling and , according to Danny Brager of Nielsen ( the data folks ) , spirits are back in the lead followed by massive growth in malt-based seltzers , part of the beer category . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The popular drink , darling , is White Claw , a 90 calorie , 5 per cent alcohol seltzer , that grew by 290 per cent in volume sales in the past year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ironically , White Claw is owned by British Columbia wine mogul Anthony von Mandl , who has a long history of developing category-leading drinks ( Mike&amp;rsquo ; s Hard Lemonade ) as well as distributing category-leading wines , not to mention ownership of several showpiece B.C. wineries. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of particular interest to wineries is a direct to consumer ( DTC ) shipping report from Sovos and Wines Vines Analytics , suggesting the &amp;ldquo ; highly lucrative DTC wine channel has matured a bit with a modest 4.7 per cent volume increase compared to double-digit growth in 2016 and 2017. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; All of which leads to the question : What is up with wine , and where is it heading in 2020 ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thach thinks the answer lies &amp;ldquo ; partially in changing consumer demographics and behaviours , but also in the lack of response by the U.S. wine industry to meet consumer desires . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One result is a slowing in legal drinking age growth in the U.S. population . Add to that a burgeoning health and wellness trend , and a Wine Intelligence survey that says 37 per cent of American wine drinkers report they are reducing the amount of alcohol they drink , and it all adds up &amp;ldquo ; to a huge wake-up call to the entire wine industry . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s hardly breaking news , but when you pile it all up , the future of wine seems cloudy . Not that it needs to grow every year ; we would be happy if it levelled off for a decade , and other critical issues became the focus , such as price versus quality . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Wine is way too expensive for what&amp;rsquo ; s in the bottle , from the bottom to the top of the market . It&amp;rsquo ; s driving young consumers to relatively cheap seltzers. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thach points to a few issues that require the attention of the wine industry , and by that , she means all wineries ( large and small ) need to pay attention to consumer needs and not hide behind the excuse that &amp;ldquo ; we are a small terroir-driven winery that only sells DTC. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ingredient communication is one area that could help . Terms such as &amp;ldquo ; gluten-free , &amp;rdquo ; &amp;ldquo ; natural flavours , &amp;rdquo ; &amp;ldquo ; no artificial sweeteners , &amp;rdquo ; &amp;ldquo ; paleo-friendly , &amp;rdquo ; &amp;ldquo ; low carb , &amp;rdquo ; &amp;ldquo ; low calorie , &amp;rdquo ; &amp;ldquo ; plant-based , &amp;rdquo ; and &amp;rdquo ; sustainably produced&amp;rdquo ; are just a few of the phrases that consumers seek out as part of a widespread health and wellness trend , yet we see little or no progress from regulators or wineries to embrace such terminology on labels . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Lower alcohol wines are another area that could be vigorously explored . We know great wine can be made between 8 and 12.5 per cent alcohol by volume , yet we see so few examples in 2020. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There doesn&amp;rsquo ; t need to be wholesale change , but when you are selling your best products in a package that requires the buyer to purchase another device to open your product , it gives you an idea of how thick the mud is on the shoes of the modern wine industry . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I leave it to Lulie Halstead , CEO of Wine Intelligence , who , after reviewing much of the latest data on wine sales , said : &amp;ldquo ; wine is growing increasingly reliant on baby boomers &amp;mdash ; those in their 60s and above &amp;mdash ; to drive volume in the sector . The younger generation is working out what its relationship with alcohol will be for the next 30+ years , and wine should not be taking anything for granted . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;15840;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;15882;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;15918;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;15949;TOOLONG ... about B.C. **26;15985;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **28;16013;TOOLONG " &gt;S ... our quarterly wine and food **32;16043;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Weekend wine picks&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;16077;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;16113;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Paul Mas C ? t ? Mas Ros ? Aurore , Languedoc , France&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $14.99 ( 1L ) 87/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 086785192512 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Buying this one litre of gently sweet , fruity , juicy ros&amp;eacute ; from the South of France for $15 seems like a no brainer to us . The bright but pale , pink/peach hue is appealing to the eye , and a chilled glass of this Grenache/Cinsault/Syrah should appeal to many on a sunny patio or at summer beach parties . Juicy wild strawberries , cherry jam , light peppery spice and candied pink florals on the finish . A simple , crowd-ready ros&amp;eacute ; that celebrates the notion of Le Luxe Rural . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;16143;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;16179;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;M . Chapoutier Belleruche C ? tes du Rh ? ne Rouge 2017 , Rhone Valley , France&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $16.99 88/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 03391181110136 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A highly reliable Cotes du Rhone sold at a price more reminiscent of 1985 than 2020 . Back up the truck for this rare bargain blend of Grenache and Syrah . Look for a fresh , white pepper , black plum , black raspberry palate with bits of savoury licorice with light notes of chocolate . Any grilled meats , fowl or mushroom pasta dishes work here . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;16209;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;16245;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Chateau Montus Tannat-Cabernet Sauvignon 2013 , Madiran , Sud-Ouest , France&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $34.99 90/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 3372220130018 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The blend is 80/20 Tannat/Cabernet Sauvignon that opens with a savoury , southwest of France nose offering dried wild black fruits , herbs flecked with cedar and leather . The oak , aged in 80 per cent new barrels , works seamlessly with the wine and presents itself quite subtly and , at this point , is moving into the background . The palate is lively and fresh with firm black fruits mixed with &amp;ldquo ; country style&amp;rdquo ; tannins that will benefit from additional time in bottle . More Madiran labels , please . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;16275;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;16311;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;M . Chapoutier Crozes-Hermitage Les Meysonniers Rouge 2016 , Rhone Valley , France&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $25.99 90/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 3391181031233 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One of the best value Crozes-Hermitage , made on the lower slopes and the flats south and east of the Hill Hermitage . Floral , white pepper and with an attractive meaty Syrah nose with a twist of garrigue . The attack is more elegant then you might expect with meaty , peppery , savoury roasted pepper and dried herb . Just a baby , this will put on weight and reward cellaring over the next three to five years . Serve with pork or lamb . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;16341;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;16377;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Antinori Pian delle Vigne Brunello di Montalcino 2014 , Tuscany , Italy&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $73.99 91/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 8001935000280 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Pian comes off one of the warmest sites in Brunello . In most years this is a blessing , and the wine is consistently ripe . The nose is open and friendly with a mix of rich black fruits , faintly stewed . Pian delle Vigne is quietly aged in 3000 to 8000 L oak casks , long free from any oak influence , to promote a pure-fruit Sangiovese Brunello . The mid-palate is all black cherry , plums and licorice with soft , dense , fine-grained tannins . Drink 2022-2027 and beyond . It has a magnetic quality that will attract plenty of attention . Mushrooms and pasta anyone ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;16407;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;16449;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;16485;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;16516;TOOLONG ... about B.C. **26;16552;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **28;16580;TOOLONG " &gt;S ... our quarterly wine and food **32;16610;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; **27;16644;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; There is a visceral pleasure in cutting into a warm egg yolk through absolutely tender pasta . This is a show-stopping first course that is typically only served in high-end restaurants , but you can do this with help from Randy Shore . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;16673;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;16709;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Egg Yolk Ravioli&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Egg pasta&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 3/4 cups ( 225 g ) 00 pasta flour&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 large eggs , lightly whisked&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a large bowl , make a pool in the flour and add 3/4 of the egg mixture , about 130g . Set the remainder of the eggs aside for the ricotta filling . Mix the eggs with a fork incorporating flour as you go . When a batter forms , fold in the rest of the flour . Knead by hand until a smooth dough forms . Tightly wrap the dough in plastic and set aside . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **36;16739;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 cup ( 250 mL ) ricotta cheese&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 tbsp ( 45 mL ) finely grated Parmesan cheese&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Scant 1/4 cup ( 50 g ) raw egg&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Salt and pepper&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;8 egg yolks&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/2 cup ( 125 mL ) butter&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2-3 slices of thick bacon or guanciale , cubed&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp lemon zest&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) flat-leaf parsley&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a bowl , combine ricotta , Parmesan and egg from the dough recipe . Season with salt and pepper . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Dust a cutting board with flour . Use a pasta sheeter to make 6-inch ( 15-cm ) wide pasta sheets . Keep going until you get to setting 7 , then lay the pasta on the floured cutting board . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Add the ricotta mixture to a piping bag or a plastic freezer bag ( just nip a 1/4-inch or 1 cm hole in the corner ) . Pipe a circle of ricotta on the pasta dough wide and high enough to hold an egg yolk . Two layers is about right . Repeat leaving plenty of space between the rings . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Place an egg yolk into each well . Moisten your fingertip with water and make a wet circle around each ricotta well . Carefully place a sheet of pasta on top and seal the ravioli . Avoid leaving air pockets . Cut away the excess dough . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a wide pan , melt butter and fry the bacon . Mince the lemon zest with the parsley and set aside . In a large pot of gently boiling , salted water , cook the raviolis for 2 minutes and 30 seconds . Carefully remove , drain and serve topped with butter , bacon and the parsley mixture . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Makes 4-8 servings. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Wine match&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Luxurious pillows of cheese with a touch of bacon call for fresh , white wines that can match the texture and cut through the rich , creamy flavours . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;16777;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;16813;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Le Vieux Pin Va ? la Ros ? 2018 , Okanagan Valley , $25 &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A bone dry ros&amp;eacute ; that is a raspberry/rhubarb affair with a spicy strawberry undercurrent ; the perfect foil to take on this creamy dish . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;16843;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;16879;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Life Cycle of a Hobo 2018 , East Kelowna , Okanagan Valley , $26&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A Riesling-like M&amp;uuml ; ller-Thurgau bright in peachy , apricot , green fruit will refresh the palate and cut through the creamy , cheesy notes of the ravioli. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;16909;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;16951;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;16987;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;17018;TOOLONG ... about B.C. **26;17054;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **28;17082;TOOLONG " &gt;S ... our quarterly wine and food **32;17112;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Anthony GismondiFri , 28 Feb 2020 19:00:58 **45;17146;TOOLONG ... hour ? la fran ? aishttp : **32;17193;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;17227;TOOLONG ... title= " The Sidecar is still one of the greatest of classic cocktails . " alt= " The Sidecar is still one of the greatest of classic cocktails . " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;17263;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1212076 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Think of France , especially on the heels of the Vancouver International Wine Festival , and we tend to think of wine . But France is also a land of spirits , liqueurs , aperitifs , digestifs and , of course , cocktails . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The French have been enjoying mixed drinks for at least 2,000 years , ever since the ancient Romans brought grape vines to what was then Gaul and introduced the practice of stirring honey and spice into their wine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Over time , the French started creating blended beverages in the form of , say , vermouth or herbal liqueur . It was only in the 1920s , when Prohibition drove America&amp;rsquo ; s top bartenders to Europe , that the alchemy of French drinking changed forever &amp;mdash ; and for the better &amp;mdash ; with the introduction of the cocktail . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Enjoy l&amp;rsquo ; heure du cocktail with these three renowned French drinks . &lt;/p&gt; **28;17293;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Perhaps the most influential of the barkeeps to flee the desert of dry America was Harry MacElhone , late of New York City&amp;rsquo ; s Plaza Hotel . He landed in Paris , where he found himself working in a saloon on the rue Daunou ; in 1923 , he bought the place , named it Harry&amp;rsquo ; s New York Bar and ( maybe ) invented the classic cocktail called the Sidecar . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The way the story goes , one day an army captain roared up to the doors of the bar , chilled from his ride in the sidecar of a motorcycle , and demanded something to warm him up . MacElhone combined brandy , cura&amp;ccedil ; ao and lemon juice in a glass with a sugared rim , and history was made . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; However , the Paris Ritz also claims to have created the Sidecar ( and serves a version made with pre-phylloxera Cognac for a mere 1,500 euros or so ) . London&amp;rsquo ; s Buck&amp;rsquo ; s Club has also been credited with the drink . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To confuse things even further , aside from the absence of bitters , the Sidecar is also awfully similar to the Brandy Crusta , which originated in New Orleans in the 1850s. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In any case , when made properly ( which , fair warning , is rarely the case ) , the Sidecar is still one of the greatest of classic cocktails . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;17323;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;17359;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;h4&gt;Sidecar recipe&lt;/h4&gt; &lt;p&gt; Traditionally , this was one of the classic 1:1:1 drinks , but those proportions taste strangely unbalanced today . Modern Sidecars use more brandy , and often skip the traditional sugar rim and cocktail cherry garnish , though of course you can always add them if you wish . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 oz ( 60 mL ) Cognac ( or other fine brandy ) &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 oz ( 30 mL ) Cointreau&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 oz ( 30 mL ) freshly squeezed lemon juice&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Pour the ingredients into a cocktail shaker with ice . Shake well and fine-strain into a chilled cocktail glass . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Serves 1 . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;French 75&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; The French 75 is a swish sort of Tom Collins made from gin , Champagne , lemon juice and sugar , although some rebels will replace the gin with brandy . It dates back to the First World War , or maybe the 1920s , or maybe the 19th century , when a citrusy punch known as a Champagne Cup was all the rage . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; MacElhone is often credited with creating the cocktail and , sure , a &amp;ldquo ; 75&amp;rdquo ; cocktail makes its first appearance in his book , Harry&amp;rsquo ; s ABC of Mixing Cocktails . However , it was made , a little gruesomely , with Calvados , gin , grenadine and absinthe . MacElhone himself credited Buck&amp;rsquo ; s Club , though the drink became truly famous when the French 75 recipe was published in 1930&amp;rsquo ; s The Savoy Cocktail Book . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; What is certain is that it was named for a piece of First World War French artillery , a 75mm Howitzer field gun called the Canon de 75 mod&amp;egrave ; le 1897 , which was exceptionally fast and accurate &amp;mdash ; just like the kick of this bright brunch favourite . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;17389;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;17425;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;h4&gt;French 75 recipe&lt;/h4&gt; &lt;p&gt; Essentially a Tom Collins made with sparkling wine instead of soda water , this is a bright , refreshing and elegant cocktail . If you like , you can also replace the gin with cognac for a drink that is deeper and richer in flavour . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 to 2 oz ( 30 to 60 mL ) London dry gin&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) simple syrup , or to taste ( see note ) &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;0.5 oz ( 15 mL ) fresh lemon juice&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;4 oz ( 125 mL ) brut Champagne or other sparkling wine&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Place gin , sugar , and lemon juice into a cocktail shaker with ice , and shake well . Fine-strain into a flute , top with bubble and garnish with slice of lemon zest . Alternative , pour base drink over cracked ice in a collins glass , then add the sparkling wine and garnish . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Serves 1 . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Note : &lt;/strong&gt; To make simple syrup , heat equal amounts of sugar and water together until the sugar fully dissolves . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Kir/Kir Royale&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Kir , or Kir Royale , has a very different sort of backstory . It was , in fact , the cocktail of the French resistance . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In 1940 , the Nazis marched into Dijon and confiscated Burgundy&amp;rsquo ; s famous red wine to enjoy at their own tables . Not unreasonably , this annoyed Canon Felix Kir , a Catholic priest who had dedicated himself to the resistance through activities such as aiding the escape of 4,000 prisoners of war from a nearby camp , and was variously arrested , condemned to death , and seriously wounded for his efforts . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One of his acts of resistance was to reclaim Burgundian &amp;ldquo ; wine&amp;rdquo ; by combining cr&amp;egrave ; me de cassis ( blackcurrant liqueur ) with a bone-dry local white wine called Aligot&amp;eacute ; de Bourgogne and proclaiming it the official beverage of Dijon&amp;rsquo ; s city hall . It was proudly consumed at all state occasions and became known , to one and all , as Kir. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After the war ended in 1945 , Kir was elected Dijon&amp;rsquo ; s mayor , a post he held until he died in 1968 at the age of 92 . He never stopped promoting the Kir and , somewhere along the way , someone brilliantly decided to create a version with Champagne , known as the Kir Royale. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; So raise a glass &amp;agrave ; votre sant&amp;eacute ; and enjoy France&amp;rsquo ; s great contributions to the cocktail canon . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;17455;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;17491;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;h4&gt;Kir Royale recipe&lt;/h4&gt; &lt;p&gt; Cr&amp;egrave ; me de cassis is a blackcurrant liqueur originally from Burgundy , but there&amp;rsquo ; s an excellent version made right here in B.C. by Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s Odd Society Spirits . It&amp;rsquo ; s based on the secret recipe of Herv&amp;eacute ; Martin , formerly the personal chef to the Late King Leopold III of Belgium . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;0.25 to 0.5 oz ( 7 to 15 mL ) cr ? me de cassis&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;4 oz ( 125 mL ) brut Champagne or other dry sparkling wine&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Place liqueur in the bottom of a flute and top with sparkling wine . If you like , garnish with a slice of lemon zest . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Serves 1&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;17521;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **28;17563;TOOLONG " &gt;M ... from the 2020 Vancouver International Wine Festival edition of **26;17593;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;17621;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? Food ReviewsRestaurant review : ' Fawn over fresh ' in a pasta templehttp : **34;18198;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;18234;TOOLONG ... title= " Lamb and swiss chard maltagliati does not disappoint . The pasta ( cut into small squares ) is delicate yet has bite . " alt= " Lamb and swiss chard maltagliati does not disappoint . The pasta ( cut into small squares ) is delicate yet has bite . " data-portal-copyright= " Mia Stainsby " **28;18270;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1220642 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Oca Pastificio&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; 1260 Commercial Dr. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Open for dinner , Wednesday to Sunday . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;More info : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //ocapastificio.com " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;18300;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; Traffic snarls everywhere foiled our plan to be early birds for dinner at Oca Pastificio . We arrived around 6:30 p.m . Too late ! We put our names on a waiting list and cursed the traffic . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Oca Pastificio on a Friday night is pretty much a waiting game . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thankfully , Commercial Drive is lively into the evening so we bought some groceries and then cooled our heels at Turk&amp;rsquo ; s Coffee Bar nearby until we got the text &amp;mdash ; table&amp;rsquo ; s ready ! &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Expectations ran high , given the restaurant&amp;rsquo ; s DNA . Owner/chef Greg Dilabio had worked at one of my favourite restaurants , La Quercia in Kitsilano , for eight years and that restaurant&amp;rsquo ; s chef/owner Adam Pegg is a partner at Oca Pastificio . Dilabio also worked at Kissa Tanto , where he was the pasta guy officiating a marriage of Japanese and Italian cuisine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When I phoned Dilabio for an interview , he was at La Quercia borrowing kitchen space for his own prep . The partners had both worked at the Slow Food restaurant Osteria Storica Morelli in Trentino , Italy , and on the 50 Top Italy list of best Italian restaurants in the world . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; He&amp;rsquo ; s about the region , he makes pasta to order , &amp;rdquo ; Dilabio says of the chef Fiorenzo Varesco. &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s how we like to do things , too . I&amp;rsquo ; d always studied pasta but there I refined technique and became comfortable making pasta ( dough ) during service . I make pasta and rice to order and it can be done if you time things properly . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Oca Pastificio is a pasta temple . There are some appetizers and antipasti plates but if you avoid gluten , this isn&amp;rsquo ; t the place for you . &amp;ldquo ; I don&amp;rsquo ; t do gluten-free other than risotto . I haven&amp;rsquo ; t &lt;span class= " Apple-converted-space " &gt; &lt;/span&gt; figured it out . We&amp;rsquo ; re a pasta restaurant , &amp;rdquo ; he says . He&amp;rsquo ; s partial to pastas of southern Italy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;18333;TOOLONG ... title= " Greg Dilabio , chef/owner of Oca Pastificio , works the pasta machine . " alt= " Greg Dilabio , chef/owner of Oca Pastificio , works the pasta machine . " data-portal-copyright= " Mia Stainsby " **28;18369;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1220644 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The pastas are rolled and extruded during service . &amp;ldquo ; I believe there&amp;rsquo ; s nothing better than fresh pasta . There&amp;rsquo ; s a reason why people fawn over fresh , &amp;rdquo ; he says . He rolls sheets of pasta in a tight space between the kitchen and dining room . Some are handcrafted into noodles , others , like tortellini and cappellacci , form into origami shapes in his hands . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; All the while , Dilabio is all smiles , heeding social media organizer extraordinaire Marie Kondo and sparking joy . A pasta extruder in the kitchen , meanwhile , squeezes out other forms of pasta like the rigatoni that evening . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He honours tradition but without complete obeisance . &amp;ldquo ; The more you understand history and tradition , the easier it is to bend rules and make it work without confusing people , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &amp;ldquo ; I want to serve what makes me happy and there&amp;rsquo ; s always room for me to keep exploring . It took me a long time to get there with spaghetti . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Pastas change daily , depending on what he finds at the markets ( mostly along Commercial Drive ) and the menu is displayed in chalkboards. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We started with an antipasti platter with a five kinds of cured meats , olives , pickles , pickled zucchini and house-made focaccai ( $21 ) ; some meats were sliced so finely , they were gossamer and sheer . A lovely beginning . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Then a cauliflower sformato with almonds and anchovy ( $15 ) , which our server , Antoine Dumont ( migrated from La Quercia ) described as a less airy souffle . It was more like a flan , nestled in a salad of herbs , cauliflower and almonds . Really lovely . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As for pasta , the lamb and swiss chard maltagliati ( $21 ) intrigued and did not disappoint . More silkiness ! The pasta ( cut into small squares ) was delicate yet had bite . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;18399;TOOLONG ... title= " Antipasti platter , part of the pasta tasting option for the table at $60 per person . " alt= " Antipasti platter , part of the pasta tasting option for the table at $60 per person . " data-portal-copyright= " Mia Stainsby " **28;18435;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1220646 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Braised beef rigatoni ( $21 ) wasn&amp;rsquo ; t quite as impressive . Fresh , yes , but the noodles were a little clingy . But the braised beef shin ( with veal jus , cream , parsley ) created a tasty sauce . &lt;span class= " Apple-converted-space " &gt; &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There&amp;rsquo ; s a pasta tasting option for the table ( $60 per person ) with antipasti , five pastas and dessert . The chalkboard menu is a vocabulary lesson &amp;mdash ; I learned about puntarelle ( chicory-like leaf ) , maltagliati ( pasta shape ) , sformato ( Italian flan ) and taralli ( pretzel-like cracker ) on my visit . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For now , there are two desserts : Almond semi-freddo and orange panna cotta ( both $8 ) . The former was presented in two thin flat slices . One thicker slice would have appealed more . The panna cotta , on the other hand , was a beauty with orange segments and candied orange rind , a contrast of delicate and intense flavours . Dilabio says it&amp;rsquo ; s made the traditional way . The original Piedmontese dessert was bound with egg whites instead of the cheater modern method with gelatin. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The wine and beverage is what you might expect &amp;mdash ; Italian to the core and modestly priced . There&amp;rsquo ; s Prosecco , Peroni beer , Negronis and Amaro , and a short selection of wines that reflects a range of major Italian styles and regions . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When I visited , there were two tables with large groups and the noise level was hard to deal with . &amp;ldquo ; We are a small room and we don&amp;rsquo ; t take reservations , so large groups are very rare , although not impossible , &amp;rdquo ; Dilabio said . &amp;ldquo ; I feel you may have been in on one of those rare occasions . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **27;18465;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **34;18494;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **25;18530;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;18557;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **27;18590;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;18619;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;18650;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;18692;TOOLONG ... coming to popular Thai restaurant in **25;18727;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;18754;TOOLONG ... here to read the latest restaurant **28;18790;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;18820;TOOLONG ... here to read the latest local , national and international food **25;18856;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;18883;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? Food ReviewsStaff BlogsAnthony Gismondi : B.C. wine of the week , wine to cellar and calendar itemshttp : **35;19026;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;19063;TOOLONG ... title= " The annual Oliver Osoyoos Wine Country Pig Out Festival , takes place on May 2 in 2020. " alt= " The annual Oliver Osoyoos Wine Country Pig Out Festival , takes place on May 2 in 2020. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;19099;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1218822 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The annual Oliver Osoyoos Wine Country Pig Out Festival takes place on May 2 , from 1 to 4 p.m. at Covert Farms Family Estate . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The one-day outdoor food and wine festival , complete with a full pig roast and Hawaiian theme , is fit for the whole ohana . It is a lei&amp;rsquo ; d back day of fun and an opportunity to practice your hula atop a giant bouncy pillow , and keep your cool with a unique water feature . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The day includes live music , costume contests , and an abundance of delicious food and wine . All 47 Oliver-Osoyoos Wine Country members , will be pouring new releases and fan favourites alongside Hawaiian-themed pork dishes from notable culinary teams , including Platinum Bench Artisan Bakery , Watermark Beach Resort , and Oliver Eats . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tickets are $69 ( students 12-18 $35 , under 12 free ) from &lt;a href= " https : **33;19129;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;19164;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; If you are attending the &lt;a href= " http : **34;19197;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Vancouver International Wine Festival&lt;/a&gt; and you have a ticket to any of the International Tasting Room sessions , don&amp;rsquo ; t forget you can buy most any wine poured at the festival at the online B.C. Liquor Store at the east end of the tasting room . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; They will also hold your purchases until the end of the night , or ship them to your neighbourhood B.C. Liquor Store for free . Our tip is to visit the store early in the evening for the best selection of wines , and to avoid long lines at the end of the evening . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Many wines will only be available during the festival , so for collectors should concentrate on those labels first . For specific wine recommendations see last weekend&amp;rsquo ; s wine festival edition of &lt;a href= " http : **28;19233;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Salut ! &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;B.C. wine of the week&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;19263;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;19299;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Fort Berens Reserve Riesling 2018 , Lillooet&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $23.99 88/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; UPC : 626990248958 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We love that the 2018 Reserve Riesling is all estate , i.e. , all Lillooet VQA fruit , the first official release of the newly minted VQA region . It was fermented in stainless steel on its native yeast , where it spent seven weeks before bottling . The lead is juicy , green apple , bitter lime , light melon , and a dusting of spices , this has a slight belly of lees to pad out the clean , crisp frame . Finishing with that bitter lime note , this would be well suited to sushi or green curries . Very promising . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Wine for the cellar&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;19329;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;19365;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Fonseca Vintage Port 2016 , Douro Valley , Portugal&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $179 I 97/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; UPC : 5013521100543 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Fonseca seldom , if ever , disappoints . From its deep , almost opaque colour to its inviting , intense , pure fruited nose , it lures you into the glass with high expectations . &amp;nbsp ; Black cherries , blackberries , menthol , violets , and more swirl out of the glass above the rich , jammy , hedonistic fruit that takes over and persists in the mouth for minutes . The key here is the excellent level of acidity that stretches the wine out from front to back , keeping it fresh and exposing a richness of texture that sets it apart from most of its peers . A brilliant wine , and one that will go the distance well into the second half of this century . Buy it before it disappears for good . It will keep for a century . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;19395;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;19437;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;19473;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;19504;TOOLONG ... about the 2020 Vancouver International Wine **29;19540;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **28;19571;TOOLONG " &gt;2 ...
