Email &lt;a href= " **28;19664;TOOLONG " **46;19694;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Anthony GismondiWed , 26 Feb 2020 19:00:06 **45;19742;TOOLONG ... Barnaby : The art of constructing a great vegetable burgerhttp : **34;19789;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;19825;TOOLONG ... title= " Karen Barnaby 's black bean burger . " alt= " Karen Barnaby 's black bean burger . " data-portal-copyright= " Karen Barnaby " **28;19861;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1222114 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; I will confess that I&amp;rsquo ; m not a big burger eater , of either the meat or vegetable persuasion . I sorely miss the &amp;lsquo ; &amp;rsquo ; 80s when I could order a medium-rare beef burger . I can&amp;rsquo ; t do that now , so I petulantly rarely bother . Vegetable burgers generally vary too widely in taste and texture , &amp;nbsp ; and I like reliability . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Making a beef burger is fairly easy &amp;mdash ; a vegetable based one , and especially a vegan one , not so much . Because I like challenges , I decided to go the vegan route . The burger I wanted to create was not one that imitated meat , I wanted it to be identifiable as a bean burger that was savoury , had texture and held together well . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To construct a vegetable burger &amp;mdash ; and I&amp;rsquo ; m saying construct because they have to be built &amp;mdash ; you need ingredients that bind them together . Breadcrumbs , oats , sweet potatoes , legumes , and semolina are commonly used . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After a few trials , I chose wheat gluten as the binder . I know gluten is a polarizing ingredient , but it gave me binding power . It is used in the countless vegetarian meat analogs that are sitting in grocery coolers all over the country , and that&amp;rsquo ; s where I got the idea . For centuries , it has been used as a non-meat protein in China and Japan , where it&amp;rsquo ; s known as mian jin or seitan. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With the binder settled , I chose black beans and brown rice for colour and texture . Moisture is the enemy ; too much moisture prevents the burger from holding together . I roasted the cooked black beans and the mushrooms to drive off the water . The mushrooms also provided a savoury flavour along with the granulated garlic , nutritional yeast , tomato paste , soy sauce and Worcestershire sauce . All of these ingredients are high in naturally occurring umami. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In putting it all together , I tried mixing all the ingredients and forming a log which was wrapped and steamed . Nope , too fussy and mushy . I tried baking which was marginally better . Then I realized that I could cook the wheat gluten , mushrooms and seasonings then fold in the rice and beans to preserve the texture . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a toasted bun with lettuce , tomatoes and onions , it pretty much lived up to what I wanted a bean burger to be ; savoury , tender and texturally satisfying . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If you Google &amp;ldquo ; vegan burger recipe&amp;rdquo ; you get 439,000,000 results . Here&amp;rsquo ; s to burger number 439,000,001. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;19891;TOOLONG ... title= " Picture : Karen Barnaby . " alt= " Picture : Karen Barnaby . " data-portal-copyright= " Karen Barnaby " **28;19927;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1222113 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Black Bean Burgers&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 x 14 oz ( 398 mL ) cans black beans , rinsed and drained&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 lb ( 500 g ) medium sized mushrooms&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/2 lb ( 250 g ) onions , approximately 1 large , peeled and sliced into 1/4-inch ( 0.6 cm ) half moons&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;4 Tbsp ( 60 mL ) extra-virgin olive oil&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 cup ( 250 mL ) cooked brown rice&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;5 Tbsp ( 75 mL ) vital wheat gluten or wheat gluten -- check the package to confirm that it has around 75% protein&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/2 tsp ( 2.5 mL ) salt&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) smoked paprika&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) sweet paprika&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) granulated garlic&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/2 tsp ( 2.5 mL ) ground black pepper&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 Tbsp ( 30 mL ) nutritional yeast&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 Tbsp ( 15 mL ) tomato paste&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 Tbsp ( 30 mL ) tahini&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 Tbsp ( 30 mL ) soy sauce&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 Tbsp ( 15 mL ) vegetarian Worcestershire sauce ( use regular if it 's not a concern for you ) &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 Tbsp ( 30 mL ) water&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Heat the oven to 350 F ( 175 C ) . Spread the beans out in a single layer on a rimmed , parchment lined baking sheet . On a separate rimmed baking sheet , toss the mushrooms and onions with the oil and spread out evenly . Place the both in the oven and roast until most of the beans split open and skins starting to crisp , about 20 minutes . Remove from oven . Give the mushrooms a stir and return to the oven until they are brown and shrivelled , about 35-40 minutes total time . Some of the onions will look black , and that&amp;rsquo ; s fine . Remove from the oven and reduce the temperature to 300 F ( 150 C ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Place the wheat gluten , paprikas , garlic , pepper , yeast , tomato paste , tahini , soy sauce , Worcestershire and water into a food processor . Pulse until the mixture comes together evenly . Add the mushroom mixture and pulse until chopped a bit smaller than a pea . ( You can also chop them by hand if a food processor is unavailable , and mix the wheat gluten mixture by hand . Then , combine the two mixtures. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Liberally oil an 8 x 8-inch ( 20 x 20 cm ) baking pan or line with parchment paper . Press the bean mixture evenly into the pan and smooth the top . Cover tightly with foil and bake for 30 minutes . Remove foil and let sit until cool enough to handle . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Combine the gluten mixture , rice and beans . Mix well with your hands until thoroughly blended . Pick up a patty-sized portion and form it into a patty . A tiny bit of cracking around the edge is fine but if there are a lot of cracks in the patty and the mixture seems dry , add 1 Tbsp ( 15 mL ) of water to the mixture and test again . If more moisture is needed , add water in 1 tsp ( 5 mL ) increments. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Form into 8 small or 6 larger patties , 1/2-inch ( 1.25 cm ) is a good thickness but they will heat through faster if 1/4-inch ( 0.6 cm ) thick . You can use a ring mould or round cookie cutter for shaping . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Heat a liberal amount of your oil of choice in a large , non-stick frying pan over medium heat . Add the patties without overcrowding and turn the heat to medium-low . Low heat is important as the burgers brown quickly . Carefully flip and brown the other side . Keep warm in a low oven and cook the remaining patties . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Serve on buns with the condiments and toppings of your choice . The patties can be frozen for up to three months . Cook them from frozen . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Makes 6 or 8 servings. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;19957;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;19999;TOOLONG ... from Postmedia food columnist Karen **28;20035;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **28;20065;TOOLONG " &gt;S ... our quarterly wine and food **32;20095;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;20129;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? We 'd like to hear from you about this or any other stories you think we should know about . Email &lt;a href= " **28;20158;TOOLONG " **46;20188;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Karen BarnabyTue , 25 Feb 2020 19:00:08 **45;20236;TOOLONG ... Kelowna 's Indigenous World Winery honours First Nations traditionshttp : **30;20283;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;20315;TOOLONG ... title= " Robert and Bernice Louie in the Indigenous World Winery barrel room in West Kelowna. " alt= " Robert and Bernice Louie in the Indigenous World Winery barrel room in West Kelowna. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;20351;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1222029 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; A small Indigenous-owned winery in West Kelowna is generating major buzz and garnering international awards while honouring traditions . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Robert Louie , 68 , who was the elected chief of the Westbank First Nation for 24 years , and his wife Bernice , 48 , are the force behind the Indigenous World Winery brand . Descendants of the Syilx , the Indigenous first peoples of the Okanagan and Similkameen valleys , the couple have five children , two of whom work with them in the winery. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I wanted to create a legacy for my family and for my people that promoted Indigenous culture , and honoured our ancestral stewardship of the land , &amp;rdquo ; said Louie. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The couple had long been interested in winemaking , and purchased the acreage in West Kelowna in 2012 . They started the growing grapes and building the winery a year later . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;20381;TOOLONG ... title= " Indigenous World Winery in West Kelowna. " alt= " Indigenous World Winery in West Kelowna. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;20417;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1222031 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We want to be very respectful of our culture as we create our wines , and that means being respectful of the lands , practicing water preservation through drip line systems , and honouring our people and our history through everything we do , including our naming . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Louie says he wants to &amp;ldquo ; embody&amp;rdquo ; the Syilx familial naming tradition by passing family names down to the vineyard&amp;rsquo ; s wines . Louie&amp;rsquo ; s traditional name Simo means &amp;ldquo ; connected to the land . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; They named their first signature Bordeaux varietal blend Simo &amp;mdash ; and the 2014 vintage has won multiple awards , including double gold at the 2019 All Canadian Wine Championships . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The name was given to Louie by his stemtema , or maternal grandmother . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; She gave me that name as a young boy , I knew the history of it , and it&amp;rsquo ; s been passed down through our family from time immemorial , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Louie , who was born at home on the Westbank First Nation reserve in the Okanagan , started working in the fruit picking industry at the age of 5 , &amp;nbsp ; and by the time he was a young teenager he was working with grapes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He took time to earn a law degree at the University of Victoria , where he was the first Indigenous graduate of their law program and first Okanagan Silyx member to be called to the bar , and later served as councillor and elected chief , all the while &amp;ldquo ; toying&amp;rdquo ; with the dream of the Indigenous winery. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;20447;TOOLONG ... title= " Robert and Bernice Louie in the vineyard at Indigenous World Winery in West Kelowna. " alt= " Robert and Bernice Louie in the vineyard at Indigenous World Winery in West Kelowna. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;20483;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1222044 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Louie credits winemaker Jason Parkes , who he calls &amp;ldquo ; the best in the industry , &amp;rdquo ; with helping the family develop the product that is making its mark on the world . Connecting the winery to his culture added a special responsibility . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It was very important we have to compete at the world level , and I knew it would have to be a product that is the best we could possibly make . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Other labels the winery produces include a white blend called Hee-Hee-Tel-kin , which shares the traditional name of the couple&amp;rsquo ; s youngest son Trenton , who works in the winery . The name means &amp;ldquo ; big stag deer with the massive antlers that resides in the high alpine , an elusive deer and somewhat spiritual and rarely seen , &amp;rdquo ; said Bernice . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I really wanted to honour the women in the family , so I had special Charmat tanks from France brought over to create the sparkling wines . The first one is named after our youngest daughter Cassandra , a sparkling Pinot Noir called La&amp;rsquo ; p Cheet , which means &amp;lsquo ; the sparkle off of the high mountain water creek&amp;rsquo ; , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Another sparkling wine is named for the couple&amp;rsquo ; s eldest daughter Kassina. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Her traditional name is Ho-we-nam Ho-we-nem , which means hummingbird because she is busy like a hummingbird , &amp;rdquo ; said Bernice . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The couple are also developing a line of craft alcohols called Indigenous Spirits , including vodka and gin . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Louie , who frequently shares his story as a speaker , said he&amp;rsquo ; s been questioned by youth over his choice to go into the alcohol industry . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; They say &amp;lsquo ; why are you in it ? &amp;rsquo ; or &amp;lsquo ; how come you make booze and sell it to the people ? &amp;rsquo ; My response is that booze is not bad if you take care with it and honour it : this industry employs people , provides a livelihood , and helps the local economy . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In addition to honouring through naming , the winery celebrates National Indigenous Peoples Day , and hosts special events with Indigenous dancers , story telling and drumming . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Indigenous World winery welcomes visitors daily to their vineyards , where they also run the Red Fox club restaurant , featuring native cuisine and wine tastings. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;20513;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;20555;TOOLONG ... you need to know about the 2020 Vancouver International Wine **29;20591;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;20622;TOOLONG ... about First Nations **27;20657;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;20686;TOOLONG ... about B.C. **26;20722;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **26;20750;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **33;20778;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;20813;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? NewsAnthony Gismondi : Personalities of the 2020 Vancouver International Wine Festivalhttp : **32;20967;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;21001;TOOLONG ... title= " Clovis Taittinger will represent the family firm pouring , among others , Champagne Taittinger N/V Brut at the 2020 Vancouver International Wine Festival . Any way you look at it , the 40 plus years of constant growth is coming to an end . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; What does sell , and always will , is the ability to be genuine , engaged in your customer , and perhaps most important , be able to tell your story compellingly. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If you plan on attending the festival , we offer a shortlist of personalities working the International Tasting Room worth visiting . If you&amp;rsquo ; re not attending , we have noted one of the wines they are pouring you should be able to buy locally . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Jean Fr ? d ? ric Hugel&lt;/strong&gt; , the 13th generation , will be pouring his family&amp;rsquo ; s iconic wines grown at Riquewihr in the Alsace region . You can experience a wonderfully aromatic white labelled &lt;strong&gt;Hugel &amp; Fils Classic Muscat&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt; ( $29.99 ) &lt;/em&gt; ; it&amp;rsquo ; s the perfect antidote to the spicy Asian dishes that abound in Vancouver . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It will be fun to catch up with &lt;strong&gt;James Sichel&lt;/strong&gt; , son of Peter and Diana Sichel &amp;mdash ; the legendary Bordeaux personality who was a force behind the rise of Chateau Palmer and Chateau d&amp;rsquo ; Angludet . Today Sichel is busy with several chateaux , including the very affordable and tasty &lt;strong&gt;Ch ? teau Argadens 2016 Bordeaux Sup ? rieur&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt; ( $24.99 ) &lt;/em&gt; , a terrific introduction to the Bordeaux blend style . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The one and only &lt;strong&gt;Clovis **25;21103;TOOLONG will represent the family firm pouring , among others , &lt;strong&gt;Champagne Taittinger N/V Brut&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt; ( $69.99 ) &lt;/em&gt; . Clovis and his sister Vitalie are firmly in charge of the current day operations at Taittinger at a time when the wines have never been better . Taittinger is all about elegance and complexity . Expect long lineups waiting to speak to Clovis , the philosopher . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bodega Colom&amp;eacute ; should be a bucket list visit for wine aficionados given its high mountain setting at 2300 meters above sea level in Argentina . &lt;strong&gt;Federico Nino&lt;/strong&gt; , the head of global sales , will be here to pour a fantastic selection of wines from Salta including a personal favourite , &lt;strong&gt;Amalaya Gran Corte 2016 Malbec-Cabernet Franc&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt; ( $39.89 ) . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Greg Corra&lt;/strong&gt; is a passionate aficionado of Australian wine and the managing director of Inland Trading Co . He&amp;rsquo ; s been a staple of Oz wine contingent at the festival for close to two decades , and his booth is must stop for fans of wine that you will never see here . Our B.C. Liquor Store pick is &lt;strong&gt;Ochota Barrels Green Room 2014 **29;21130;TOOLONG &lt;em&gt; ( $29.97 ) . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; B.C. winemaker and winery owner &lt;strong&gt;Richard da Silva&lt;/strong&gt; will be in the tasting room pouring several Da Silva Vineyard wines . The family has been growing grapes all over the Okanagan Valley for some 40 years , and da Silva is a fount of local knowledge . The wine to look for is &lt;strong&gt;Da Silva 2015 Cabernet **31;21161;TOOLONG &lt;em&gt; ( $35 ) . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Also new to the tasting room is &lt;strong&gt;Santiago Cilley&lt;/strong&gt; , general manager of Phantom Creek Estates , which officially opens in April . Cilley has an impressive resume of work in with Jackson Family Wines in Sonoma , Bulgheroni Vineyards in Napa Valley and is set to leave his mark on B.C.&amp;rsquo ; s Black Sage Bench . Be sure to check out the wine here and catch up on what is to come from this landmark winery in the south Okanagan . Most tasters will gravitate to the reds , but two labels that will surprise you are &lt;strong&gt;Phantom Creek Estates 2017 Pinot Gris&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt; ( $30 ) &lt;/em&gt; and &lt;strong&gt;Phantom Creek Estates 2017 Riesling&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt; ( $30 ) . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Finally , perhaps the most interesting man in the room is &lt;strong&gt;Ermes Scardova&lt;/strong&gt; . The learned Italian will be modestly pouring wines from Emilia Romagna at the Medici Ermete booth . Scardova is a one-of-a-kind wine guy , whose knowledge and storytelling style is of the sort that will be required to pull the wine business back from the abyss it is heading toward at breakneck speed . Taste the &lt;strong&gt;Medici Ermete N/V Phermento Lambrusco Metodo **25;21194;TOOLONG &lt;em&gt; ( $29.99 ) . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; See you in the tasting room . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;21221;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;21263;TOOLONG ... about 2020 Vancouver International Wine **29;21299;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;21330;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;21366;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **28;21397;TOOLONG " &gt;2 ... Vancouver International Wine Festival edition of Salut , our quarterly wine and food **32;21427;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Weekend wine picks&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;21461;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;21497;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Ch ? teau Argadens Bordeaux Sup ? rieur 2016 , Bordeaux , France&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $21.99 90/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; UPC : 3394150048720 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Argadens red is a modern Bordeaux based on the concentration of fruit with a supple , balanced texture . The vineyard average age is now over 30 years old , and its planted to Merlot , Cabernet Sauvignon and Cabernet Franc sitting on clay-limestone soil in a single plot . The site is the highest among the rolling hills of the Entre-Deux-Mers wine region . The blend is 63/32/5 , almost mirroring plantings in the vineyard . The nose is harmonious , expressing dark fruits with well-integrated oak at this stage ; indeed , it is a wine you can age for five to 10 years . It&amp;rsquo ; s not Palmer or d&amp;rsquo ; Angludet , but there are similarities in style . Real wine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;21527;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;21563;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Famille Perrin Vacqueyras Les Christins 2017 , Rhone Valley , France&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $26.99 89/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; UPC : 631470007086 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Plush , super ripe and dense with dark cherry and black raspberry , the tannins are silky and ample , sliding the plump fruit to a warming finish . Decant and pour into large glasses alongside roasted lamb . The fruit comes off the stony clay terraces of an 8-hectare vineyard in the Sarrians commune . The mix is an 80/20 split of Grenache and Syrah , now averaging 50 years and all fermented and aged in a combination of foudres and large tanks . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;21593;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;21629;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Sorelle Bronca Particella 232 Brut Nature Brut Prosecco Superiore N/V , Veneto , Italy&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $39.99 90/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; UPC : 8033162560206 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There is a next page among the hills between Valdobbiadene and Conegliano , home to Italy&amp;rsquo ; s most excellent sparkling wines . The story is a drier style of Prosecco with a focus on terroir . Enter the Bronca sisters and a new organic cuv&amp;eacute ; e of bone-dry Prosecco from a tiny plot high in the hills of Farro di Follina . Particella 232 Nature is 40 years old , and the fruit is aged four months on its lees sans sugar . The mousse is tiny and refined , the nose floral with a dusting of dried wild herbs . The attack is citrus-forward with a bit of creamy peach , and a long , nutty , salty , finish . Impressive and very food-friendly . The grapes are mostly Glera , with smaller amounts of Bianchetta and Perera. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;21659;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;21695;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Amalaya Gran Corte Malbec-Cabernet Franc 2016 , Calchaqui Valley , Salta , Argentina&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $39.89 88/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; UPC : 779810476116 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Amalaya is a modern Argentine producer located in Cafayate , Salta . The 2016 harvest yielded the lowest production in 20 years . It opens with heightened acidity and spicy , black fruit notes before a cooler , skinny palate with savoury , peppery red fruit flavours and a touch of tomato . There is fine intensity , but the finish is short . It is ready to drink and best served with lamb chops or sausages . The blend is an 85/10/5 mix of Malbec , Cabernet Franc , and Tannat. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;21725;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;21761;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Taittinger Brut Reserve N/V , Champagne , France&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $90 91/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; UPC : 3016570001030 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Floral honey , baked pear , strawberry , and citrus draw you into the glass . The attack is elegant and persistent , with more of the same . Serve with just about anything . Brut R&amp;eacute ; serve is a blend of Chardonnay ( 40 per cent ) , Pinot Noir , and Pinot Meunier ( 60 per cent ) wines from over 35 different vineyards that are aged a full 36 months on its lees , and often longer . The high proportion of Chardonnay , unique among fine non-vintage Champagnes , is what sets this wine apart from so many others . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;21791;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;21833;TOOLONG ... about 2020 Vancouver International Wine **29;21869;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;21900;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;21936;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **28;21967;TOOLONG " &gt;2 ... Vancouver International Wine Festival edition of Salut , our quarterly wine and food **32;21997;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; **27;22031;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Sichuan pepper comes in two distinct varieties , so you are going to want to pay attention . The red ones are mild , zippy and lemony , while the green ones are piney , powerful and create a numbing , tingling effect in your mouth . Neither is hot like a chili pepper , but both complement extreme heat . Which one you choose will greatly influence the flavour of this traditional Sichuan noodle dish , adapted by Randy Shore , author of the diner-driven cookbook Home and Away . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;22060;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;22096;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Dan Dan Noodles with Meat Sauce&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tbsp ( 15 mL ) light soy sauce&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt; ? tsp ( 2.5 mL ) baking soda&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;6 to 8 oz ( 160 to 226 g ) pork , minced&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) grated ginger&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/2 to 1 tsp ( 2.5 to 5 mL ) ground Sichuan peppercorns&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 tsp ( 10 mL ) black vinegar&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) chili bean sauce ( aka doubanjiang or toban djan ) &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) dark soy sauce&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 cups ( 500 mL ) chicken stock , divided&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) corn starch&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) duck fat or lard &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) cracked chilies&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;12-16 oz ( 340-454 g ) fresh ramen noodles&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Chili oil , to taste&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a bowl , combine light soy sauce with baking soda . Mince pork and add to bowl along with ginger and Sichuan pepper . Mix , cover and refrigerate at least two hours . In a bowl , combine vinegar , chili bean sauce , dark soy sauce , 1 cup stock and corn starch . Whisk until combined and set aside . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a small saucepan on medium heat , warm remaining chicken stock and season to taste with salt . In a wok on high heat , warm fat . Add pork and chili flakes and fry until cooked through . Lower heat to medium , stir sauce mixture to incorporate the cornstarch and then add to wok . Stir until thickened , about three minutes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Meanwhile in a large pot , bring three quarts of water to a boil . Cook ramen noodles according to package directions , drain and divide between bowls . Top with meat sauce , Ginger Relish and chili oil . Serve hot chicken broth on the side , to use as needed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Makes 2 servings. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Ginger Relish&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 cup ( 250 mL ) chopped green onion&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) minced fresh ginger&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 tbsp ( 45 mL ) canola oil&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) light soy sauce&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt; ? tsp ( 2.5 mL ) kosher salt&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt; ? tsp ( 1 mL ) sherry vinegar&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a small bowl , combine green onion , ginger , oil , soy sauce , salt and vinegar . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Wine match&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sichuan pepper , chili flakes and ginger relish call for a glass of cooling wine , or one with some residual sugar . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;22126;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;22162;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Van Westen Vineyards Vino Grigio 2018 , Naramata Bench , Okanagan Valley , $19.90&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Fresh and bright , with a collection of ripe Naramata Bench orchard fruit flavours with creamy , ripe honeydew and baking spices . Perfect for taming the heat in the Dan Dan noodle dish . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;22192;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;22228;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Road 13 Roussanne 2017 , Similkameen Valley , $26&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Exotic , unctuous white wine whose rich , honeyed pear , pineapple , creamy lees , and vanilla flavours both freshen and complement the chili/ginger flavours in what is a noisy dish . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;22258;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;22300;TOOLONG ... about 2020 Vancouver International Wine **29;22336;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;22367;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;22403;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **28;22434;TOOLONG " &gt;2 ... Vancouver International Wine Festival edition of Salut , our quarterly wine and food **32;22464;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;CORRECTION : &lt;/strong&gt; This story has been updated with a photo correctly identifying Clovis Taittinger. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;22498;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? Email &lt;a href= " **28;22527;TOOLONG " **46;22557;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Anthony GismondiSat , 22 Feb 2020 18:51:57 **45;22605;TOOLONG ... Paris is for ( wine ) lovershttp : **34;22652;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;22688;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " Aleesha Harris " **28;22724;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1212545 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Paris is consistently ranked as one of the top destinations to visit in Europe . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; From art aficionados to history buffs , to foodies and fashion fans , the French city is a magnet for international tourists . ( More than 89 million people reportedly visited the city in 2018. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But what about for wine lovers ? You&amp;rsquo ; re in luck &amp;mdash ; the City of Light also happens to boast a plethora of options for sipping and discovering wonderful French wines . It&amp;rsquo ; s also a perfect jumping off point for extended sojourns to the country&amp;rsquo ; s various renowned wine regions , including Bordeaux and Burgundy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If you have Paris on the brain , here are a few ideas for where to stay , sip , shop , and more : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;22754;TOOLONG ... title= " The Executive Suite at Le Meurice in Paris . " alt= " The Executive Suite at Le Meurice in Paris . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;22790;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1212544 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;22820;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Le Meurice -- 228 rue de Rivoli&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Considered to be the &amp;ldquo ; original palace hotel in Paris&amp;rdquo ; , this luxury Dorchester Collection destination perfectly blends 18th Century extravagance with modern hotel touches . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Situated across from the Tuileries Garden and within steps of the Louvre Museum , the hotel provides access to the city&amp;rsquo ; s must-see sights , as well as five-star service and amenities . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Twenty nine of the hotel&amp;rsquo ; s rooms have recently been renovated &amp;mdash ; including the formidable top-floor Belle Etoile Penthouse Suite , which offers 360-degree views of Paris , including an enviable vantage point of the most iconic landmark in Paris , the Eiffel Tower . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;22855;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;22891;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Sip and savour&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Vantre -- 19 Rue de la Fontaine au Roi&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A trip to Paris wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t be complete without a few memorable meals . With Canadian sommelier Marco Pelletier at the helm , this bistro-style eatery has earned a reputation&amp;nbsp ; among foodies and wine lovers alike for offering quality dishes , an extensive wine list and top-notch customer service . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; For me , the main motivation is to be on the floor and be with the client &amp;hellip ; to take care of them . For me , that is the most important thing , &amp;rdquo ; Pelletier , who is originally from Montreal and counts positions at Taillevent and Le Bristol among his previous sommelier posts , says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With a new chef recently installed in the kitchen at this 11th arrondissement eatery , Pelletier says diners can expect a new level of dishes &amp;mdash ; and accompanying wine pairings &amp;mdash ; in the coming months that are sure to draw even more visitors to the lunch and dinner-hour sittings . If you plan to visit , reservations are highly recommended , as Vantre is typically fully booked . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;22921;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;22957;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;22987;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;La Cave at Lafayette Gourmet -- 35 Boulevard Haussmann&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Across the street from the historied Galeries Lafayette on the Boulevard Haussmann where shoppers can peruse and purchase a variety of fashion items , beauty finds , souvenirs and more , is Lafayette Gourmet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A unique destination for home decor and food finds ( the dining hall is a must visit for gourmet fare on the go ) , the department store is also home to a wine lover&amp;rsquo ; s paradise : Duclot La Cave . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Open since 2010 , when it was dedicated to wines from Bordeaux , the cellar has since expanded to include 3,500 bottles of wine , Champagne and spirits . The three-level cellar comprises 450 sq m and includes a wine tasting room for personalized wine tours organized through Galeries Lafayette. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At the heart of the impressive space is a piece of art created by the &amp;ldquo ; master carpenters of Paris , &amp;rdquo ; and it is home to an astonishingly large selection of &amp;ldquo ; the finest Bordeaux wines&amp;rdquo ; dating back to a Ch&amp;acirc ; teau d&amp;rsquo ; Yquem from 1899. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23022;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;23058;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; **36;23088;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For those who are in search of discovery during their wine-themed vacation , there are a wide variety of options available to visitors at all times of the year &amp;mdash ; from day trips to visit the Moet et Chandon cellars in Reims or boutique winery stops in Chablis , to multi-day trips to Bordeaux or Burgundy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But , you don&amp;rsquo ; t have to leave the city limits in order to enjoy a good glass of vin fran&amp;ccedil ; ais . There are many options for urban wine tours and market stops available through travel websites like Viator , many of which also see said wines paired with the delicious flavours of France . And , yes , pastries are often featured , too . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Prepare to sip , swill and be very satisfied . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23126;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;23162;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; **35;23192;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Valmont Spa -- 228 Rue de Rivoli &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After all that wining , dining and discovering , the final itinerary item may well be a day at the spa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This luxurious wellness destination features a complete menu of body-benefiting procedures &amp;mdash ; from facials to full-body massages . All of this , of course , means it&amp;rsquo ; s the perfect place to debrief ( and de-puff ) after the itinerary of wine tasting and tours . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In May , the location will become home to the first Maison Valmont boutique in France , which will be familiar to many Vancouverites as the city boasts its own Maison Valmont destination . Here , though , loyal users of the Swiss company&amp;rsquo ; s creations can get a sneak peek of Valmont new releases two months before they&amp;rsquo ; re available at other locations . Email &lt;a href= " **28;23425;TOOLONG " **46;23455;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Aleesha HarrisFri , 21 Feb 2020 19:00:24 **45;23503;TOOLONG ... TravelLifeTravelSalut ! : Discovering rare wines in a small cellar in the heart of Parishttp : **35;23550;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;23587;TOOLONG ... title= " Vintage wines at De Vinis Illustribus. " alt= " Vintage wines at De Vinis Illustribus. " data-portal-copyright= " Aleesha Harris " **28;23623;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1213534 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Lionel Michelin isn&amp;rsquo ; t opposed to the idea of being referred to as an antiques hunter . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In fact , his job as co-owner and sommelier of &lt;a href= " http : //www.devinis.fr/FR/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;De Vinis Illustribus&lt;/a&gt; regularly sees him hunting rare and precious vintage French wines all over the country , much like one might for French Regency furniture finds . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; When I&amp;rsquo ; m arriving in a private cellar , there is no light and there are spiders everywhere , dust and the people say , &amp;lsquo ; The oldest are in this corner , &amp;rsquo ; &amp;rdquo ; he explains of his regular experiences while searching out the near-sacred bottles . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23653;TOOLONG ... title= " Dominique and Lionel Michelin are the owners of De Vinis Illustribus. " alt= " Dominique and Lionel Michelin are the owners of De Vinis Illustribus. " data-portal-copyright= " Aleesha Harris " **28;23689;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1213537 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sometimes , his searches come up empty-handed . But , other times , he stumbles upon wines that make the cobwebs and dirt-floored cellars more than worthwhile . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Big name , great vintage , well kept . I put it up to look at the fill . Fill is good . Most of the time , a good cellar gives that , &amp;rdquo ; he says , motioning toward the frayed and damaged edges of a wine label that he found in just such a way . The bottles , he says , often show their age , but the elixirs inside them &amp;mdash ; well , they&amp;rsquo ; ve more than benefited from the passage of time . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; A good cellar has to be humid . And humidity destroys the labels . But , you don&amp;rsquo ; t drink the labels , &amp;rdquo ; Michelin explains . &amp;ldquo ; When the bottle came out from the Chateau , it was there . This is an &amp;rsquo ; 85 , so in 35 years , it lost half-a-centimetre , which is nothing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; This was close to the earth , just by the label . When you take a look at the bottle , you know whether it was well-kept or not . Ninety per cent of the final result is the way it has been kept . Especially when it&amp;rsquo ; s 100 years old . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For the bottles that no longer have a label , whether rendered illegible by age or having disappeared altogether , Michelin has a unique trick for discovering what&amp;rsquo ; s inside . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; All the wines of Bordeaux have the vintage on the cork &amp;mdash ; the name of the chateau and the vintage year , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &amp;ldquo ; I can sell that to people who trust me , and I sell it half-price . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Trust is a big part of the business . Michelin&amp;rsquo ; s customers have to be confident the wines they&amp;rsquo ; re buying are well worth the price tag they command on the resale market , which can regularly see bottles fetching thousands of euros per bottle . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After all , the wines his customers buy are for drinking not just for show , he assures. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Ninety per cent of our customers are wine lovers . And they drink , &amp;rdquo ; Michelin says proudly . &amp;ldquo ; We give a warranty on our wines , so if there is a trouble , they can bring them back . But , most of the time , they come back to say it was really good and to have another one . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After discovering a particularly spectacular bottle on a buying trip , Michelin knows which of his international clients will be eager to own it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We know who they are going to . Just like antiques . Especially for old bottles , &amp;rdquo ; Michelin says . &amp;ldquo ; For example , I just found some very old bottles from a high-end vintner and I know who will buy them . I&amp;rsquo ; ll send a few emails to the U.S. , to Hong Kong , to Japan and to Australia . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; And the customers will say , &amp;lsquo ; OK , I want it . &amp;rsquo ; &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sometimes , more than one buyer makes a bid for the bottle . &amp;nbsp ; In an instance like this , Michelin sells the rare bottles on a first-come-first-serve basis . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Because we don&amp;rsquo ; t want to make trouble with our customers . Sometimes our customers will say , &amp;lsquo ; You know , I can pay more , &amp;rsquo ; but we don&amp;rsquo ; t make auctions , &amp;rdquo ; he says with a smile . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Located within the Latin Quarter of Paris , the small boutique and wine cellar at 48 , rue de la **33;23719;TOOLONG ; ve has earned a reputation for providing sought-after vintage wines . After starting the business in 1994 at another location , Michelin and his wife , Dominique , moved the operation to its current location in 1997. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The space belonged to a &amp;ldquo ; famous wine merchant , &amp;rdquo ; according to Dominique , named Jean Baptiste Besse who counted Ernest Hemingway among his patrons . After Besse&amp;rsquo ; s death , his children didn&amp;rsquo ; t want to continue the business , so they sold the cellar &amp;mdash ; and all the wines contained within it &amp;mdash ; to the Michelins. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23754;TOOLONG ... title= " A view of wine in the cellar of De Vinis Illustribus in Paris . " alt= " A view of wine in the cellar of De Vinis Illustribus in Paris . " data-portal-copyright= " Aleesha Harris " **28;23790;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1213539 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23820;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;23856;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The establishment has a varying amount of stock at any one time , offering three types of wine : old vintages purchased from private cellars ; &amp;ldquo ; high-end young wines&amp;rdquo ; from the vintners of Burgundy ; and a small selection of &amp;ldquo ; everyday wines&amp;rdquo ; that the Michelin&amp;rsquo ; s would recommend to friends and neighbours for regular drinking . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Michelin is a member of several tasting juries , which allows him to taste many new wines . From these , he makes a small selection of recent releases in order to bring back to the shop . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s really the best of French wines in a range of budgets , which is affordable , &amp;rdquo ; Dominique says . &amp;ldquo ; At a good value . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Open to the public by appointment only , the Michelins also offer limited wine-tasting experiences . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s not exactly like a wine shop , it&amp;rsquo ; s not at all like a wine bar , it&amp;rsquo ; s not a restaurant &amp;mdash ; it&amp;rsquo ; s something different , &amp;rdquo ; Dominique says . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s really a place dedicated to wine , especially old vintages , rare wines . And also the best of French wines . That means we are really focused on French wines and we have very few wines from abroad . Even if we love them . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Love wine the Michelins definitely do . That much becomes apparent within minutes of stepping through the door of De Vinis Illustribus . For Michelin , it has been a long time in the making . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A few months after graduating with his law degree in the &amp;rsquo ; 70s , a friend of Lionel&amp;rsquo ; s approached him to help work at a wine auction . The friend purchased a small batch of the old wines on offer in the sale . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We came back and he bought a baguette , some cold cuts and he started opening the bottles , &amp;rdquo ; Michelin recalls fondly . &amp;ldquo ; And we had a chance to drink those old bottles . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Enjoying the experience , he returned to the auctions to buy old wines for himself . It was through the auctions that Lionel was introduced to a small private wine club where members sampled only rare vintage wines . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There , I had a chance to drink the best French wines , ever . The iconic brands &amp;hellip ; Ch&amp;acirc ; teau d&amp;rsquo ; Yquem 1848 , Ch&amp;acirc ; teau Latour 1929 , Chateau Mouton 1945 . We had them all , &amp;rdquo ; he recalls . Needless to say , the experience further fuelled his personal wine passion . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Even if I was working in telecommunications because it was my job , I used to go out buying and buying and buying , &amp;rdquo ; he says . That predilection for purchasing rare vintage wines served Michelin well when they opened their business . &amp;ldquo ; Our private cellar was something like 5,000 old bottles . And we gave those bottles to the company . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Michelin credits his time buying and trying those wines in the early years with providing him with an edge on his sommelier peers today . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I had a chance to drink all the big names in France , &amp;rdquo ; Michelin says . &amp;ldquo ; Sometimes a person will come to me and say , &amp;lsquo ; I had the chance to try a Ch&amp;acirc ; teau Latour 1986 , &amp;rsquo ; and I&amp;rsquo ; m able to say , &amp;lsquo ; Yes , but the &amp;rsquo ; 85 is better and the &amp;rsquo ; 82 , I had it . And I had the &amp;rsquo ; 70 , and the &amp;rsquo ; 66 , and the &amp;rsquo ; 64 , and the &amp;rsquo ; 61 , and the &amp;rsquo ; 45 , and the &amp;rsquo ; 29 and the 1870. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; And then I can tell you , yes , your &amp;rsquo ; 86 is great &amp;mdash ; but , when you drink the &amp;rsquo ; 82 , you forget the &amp;rsquo ; 86 . And that&amp;rsquo ; s how , now , we can explain what is the best wine for the money . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;23886;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **28;23928;TOOLONG " &gt;M ... from the 2020 Vancouver International Wine Festival edition of **26;23958;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **28;23986;TOOLONG " **35;24016;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;24053;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Unsworth Vineyards 2017 Pinot Noir , Cowichan Valley , $28 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;h3&gt;TERROIR X TEN&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; France is the homeland of terroir . The idea that the soil grapes grow upon , the mesoclimate and the people impart a DNA of place upon the wine is no longer a notion . The truth is every vineyard possesses terroir , and every wine grower must express that sense of place in his or her wine anywhere in the world . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Terroir is the foundation of the Appellation d&amp;rsquo ; Origine Contr&amp;ocirc ; l&amp;eacute ; e ( AOC ) system of France , so we wanted to point you toward 10 French labels inside the tasting room that are brimming with terroir. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; G&amp;eacute ; rard Bertrand 2016 Terroir St Chinian , Languedoc , $24 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Domaine Boutinot 2016 Les Coteaux Schisteux S&amp;eacute ; guret , C&amp;ocirc ; tes du Rh&amp;ocirc ; ne Villages , Rhone Valley , $26 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Alain Brumont 2013 Chateau Montus Pacherenc du Vic Bilh White , South West France , $43 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Jean-Luc Colombo 2016 Terres Brul&amp;eacute ; es Cornas , Rhone Valley , $68 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Georges Duboeuf 2018 Julienas Chateau des Capitans , Beaujolais , $25 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;25031;TOOLONG &lt;img src= " https : **34;25058;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;25094;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &amp;bull ; Paul Jaboulet A&amp;icirc ; n&amp;eacute ; 2017 Crozes Hermitage Les Jalets , Rh&amp;ocirc ; ne Valley , $35 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; M. What we can do is suggest if you are going to spend time at any booths , here is a shortlist of those who have done their best to bring their best to Vancouver . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Nothing is easy in B.C. anymore for anyone selling wine , so we salute those who still believe in the market and bringing their best for you to taste . &lt;h3&gt;PINOT NOIR GOES GLOBAL&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s fair to say that on some level Pinot Noir producers are connected in a way no other grape connect with its grower . If they are not discussing clones , rootstocks , barrels , open fermenters they are likely debating when , how , and for how long , they might submerge the cap of the thin-skinned , temperamental grape . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Specificity is what Pinot is all about , although searching for the perfect one may be more desirable than actually finding it . " data-portal-copyright= " Mia Stainsby " **28;26180;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1217033 " /&gt; **27;26210;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; 2351 Burrard Street , Vancouver &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Open for lunch and dinner , Tuesday to Saturday &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;More info : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //Instagram : %20@unchairestaurant " &gt;Instagram : @unchairestaurant , &lt;/a&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " **29;26239;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; When I first visited Unchai for lunch last September , I got a table as a walk-in . But more recently , I noticed a wait list sign-up sheet at the door . It points to a couple of things . They do damn good Thai food worth a patient wait but also , Unchai is a modest 16-seater that quickly fills up . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; However , the couple behind it are set to make changes &amp;mdash ; Narong Yumonkol and wife Warisara Unchai are searching for a larger space in Kitsilano but the much bigger change ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m five months pregnant , &amp;rdquo ; Unchai said . &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s why we&amp;rsquo ; re hiring new staff . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Right now , it&amp;rsquo ; s the two of them in the small galley kitchen which shrinks as she enlarges . When chilies hit the wok , the air in the dining area becomes pungent and testy. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Yumonkol started cooking at 12 to help out his family and eventually went on to cook at the luxury Sukhothai Bangkok Hotel and Le Meridien Dubai . The couple worked at Maenam &amp;mdash ; he in the kitchen and she in the front of house &amp;mdash ; and had earlier met at Salathai . As testament to how hard Yumonkol has worked those woks over three decades , he has neck and shoulder problems and his doctor ordered a week off cooking last summer at a very busy time . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;26270;TOOLONG ... title= " Boat noodle soup at Unchai , . " alt= " Boat noodle soup at Unchai , . " data-portal-copyright= " Mia Stainsby " **28;26306;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1217030 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The dishes are from throughout Thailand and Yumonkol offers dishes you won&amp;rsquo ; t find in most Thai restaurants in Vancouver , attracting a lot of the local Thai community . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; He wants Vancouver to try new things , &amp;rdquo ; says Unchai. &amp;ldquo ; He creates new dishes and makes changes to the menu . Sometimes , all the guests are Thai. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For instance , you&amp;rsquo ; re not likely to see Boat Noodle Soup ( $14.99 ) in many Thai restaurants . It has pork meat , pork balls , and pork blood &amp;mdash ; a couple of spoonfuls in each serving to thicken and sweeten the broth &amp;mdash ; green onion , bean sprouts , Asian celery and after Thai customers requested it , fried pork rind . The broth , a symphony of flavour with pork bones , a whole peeled pineapple , ginger , galangal , pandan leaf , palm sugar , oyster sauce , soy sauce , fish sauce , fried garlic , fried shallots and herbs takes seven hours to prepare . With the noodle soups , guests choose from won ton , rice sheet , thin rice or vermicelli &amp;lsquo ; glass&amp;rsquo ; noodles. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;26336;TOOLONG ... title= " Pla goong at Unchai. " alt= " Pla goong at Unchai. " data-portal-copyright= " Mia Stainsby " **28;26372;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1217029 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; I&amp;rsquo ; d suggest starting with Plaa Goong ( $13.99 ) a spicy shrimp salad , a very lively dish with prawns , mint , coriander , green onion , shallots , fried shallots unified by chili jam ( mango , lemon grass , herbs , ginger , garlic , Thai herbs , chili , palm sugar ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Roti , which comes with a curry sauce dip ( $5.99 ) has a crunchy exterior and a bit of a chew as you bite in . A slower , longer time in the pan nails this texture . &lt;span class= " Apple-converted-space " &gt; &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The curries ( gang kua ) have the same sauce given the kitchen space but ooh la la , what flavour . It&amp;rsquo ; s mellow compared to the shriekingly hot chili levels one can encounter in Thailand . You&amp;rsquo ; re asked what level of heat and it&amp;rsquo ; s easy enough to turn it up to a shriek or scream . His paste involves garlic , galangal , turmeric , coriander roots , ginger , makrut lime , fish sauce , chili , shallots &amp;mdash ; he balances it beautifully and makes it intoxicating . Coconut milk soothes. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But the curry noodle soup with a free-range chicken leg has a somewhat different composition . The khao soi sauce from Chiang Mai is more strongly flavoured even without the galangal and makrut lime . &lt;span class= " Apple-converted-space " &gt; &lt;/span&gt; Thai omelette with crab ( $14.99 ) drapes over a hillock of rice . It could be bland were it not for a sauce with bite a sweet , sour , spicy , acidy , herby sauce . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; In Thailand the omelette is often eaten with ketchup , &amp;rdquo ; Unchai says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;26402;TOOLONG ... title= " Pad ki mao at Unchai. For now , they need to turn tables quickly . With alcohol , people tend to settle in . Food ReviewsStaff BlogsKasey Wilson : Vive La France ! http : **33;26636;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;26671;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;26707;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1216540 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; During the last week of February , Vancouver gives itself over to a week-long celebration of wine . This year the lineup of festive meals , informative seminars and terrific tastings will showcase 1,450 wines from 163 wineries in 16 nations , with France in the spotlight as the 2020 theme country . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Harry Hertscheg , executive director of the &lt;a href= " http : **34;26737;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Vancouver International Wine Festival&lt;/a&gt; , expects competition to be keen for tickets to the more than three dozen events featuring French wines . &amp;ldquo ; France is one of the most popular regions , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &amp;ldquo ; It sells out before we know it . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;26773;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;26809;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; If you want to get a handle on France before time , dive into Let&amp;rsquo ; s Eat France ! by Fran&amp;ccedil ; ois-R&amp;eacute ; gis Gaudry ( Artisan , 2018 ) . Subtitled &amp;ldquo ; 1,250 specialty foods , 375 iconic recipes , 350 topics , 260 personalities , plus hundreds of maps , charts , tricks , tips and anecdotes and everything else you want to know about the food of France , &amp;rdquo ; it is a feast for food lovers and Francophiles alike . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There are classic recipes , such as pot-au-feu , eight essential composed salads , &amp;nbsp ; p&amp;acirc ; t&amp;eacute ; en cro&amp;ucirc ; te , &amp;nbsp ; blanquette de veau , choucroute , and ratatouille ; profiles of French icons like Colette , Brillat-Savarin , Bocuse , the Troigros dynasty and Victor Hugo also a list of great chefs who died before they were aged 60 ; a region-by-region index of cheeses , charcuterie and recipes ; poster-size guides to the breads of France , the wines of France , the oysters of France &amp;mdash ; even the&amp;nbsp ; frites&amp;nbsp ; of France . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; You&amp;rsquo ; ll meet endive , the belle of the north ; discover the croissant timeline ; understand the art of tartare ; find a chart of wine bottle sizes , from the tiny split to the Nebuchadnezzar ( the equivalent of 20 standard bottles ) ; follow the family tree of French sauces and discover the meaning of &amp;ldquo ; take it in the baba&amp;rdquo ; or &amp;ldquo ; bad taste&amp;rdquo ; in &amp;ldquo ; The Erotics of Cooking&amp;rdquo ; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Here are recipes for two French classics : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Languedoc Beef Stew with Red Wine , Herbs and Olives&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; This adaptation of the classic French daube is a favourite holiday dish of writer Fiona Beckett at her summer home in France . She adds a little extra wine at the end to lift flavour after long , slow cooking . Serve with mashed potatoes and slow-roasted carrots . ( Adapted from Wine Lover&amp;rsquo ; s Kitchen : Delicious Recipes for Cooking with Wine by Fiona Beckett , Rylands , Peter &amp;amp ; Small , 2017. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 lb ( 1 kg ) thickly sliced braising or stewing steak&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 tbsp ( 45 mL ) all-purpose flour&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;5-6 tbsp ( 75-90 mL ) olive oil&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 large onion , thinly sliced&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 large garlic cloves , crushed&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tbsp ( 15 mL ) tomato paste&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;4 cups ( 1 L ) Faug ? res or other full-bodied fruity red wine&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 cups ( 500 mL ) beef stock&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) herbes de Provence&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 thin strip orange zest&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 bay leaves&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 cup ( 250 mL ) black olives&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 heaped tbsp ( 45 mL ) roughly chopped flat leaf parsley&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Sea salt and freshly ground pepper&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Trim any excess fat from the beef , then cut the meat into large cubes . Put the flour in a shallow dish and season it with salt and pepper . Dip the cubes of beef in the flour to coat . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Heat 2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) oil in a large skillet over medium-high heat , add beef and fry on all sides until it is browned . You will need to do this in batches , adding extra oil as you go . Transfer beef to a cast-iron casserole . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Heat 2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) olive&amp;nbsp ; oil in the same skillet , add onion and cook for 3 to 4 minutes , until softened but not browned . Add garlic and tomato paste and cook for 1 minute , stirring . Add 1 cup plus 1 tbsp ( 15 mL ) of the wine plus the stock , herbes de Provence , orange zest and bay leaves . Bring to the boil , then pour the sauce into the casserole . Heat the casserole over a medium heat and bring the sauce back to the boil . Reduce the heat , cover and simmer very gently for 2 to 3 hours until the meat is completely tender . Check occasionally to ensure there is enough liquid , and add a little stock or water if it&amp;rsquo ; s too dry . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; About 30 minutes before the stew is cooked , stir in olives . Just before serving , season to taste with salt and pepper , then stir in parsley , and remaining wine and cook for a further 5 minutes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Serves 4-6&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Chocolate Mousse&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; This delightful recipe has many virtues : zero added sugar , zero cream and zero butter &amp;mdash ; but plenty of chocolate . It&amp;rsquo ; s a mousse in its simplest form . ( Adapted from Let&amp;rsquo ; s Eat France by Fran&amp;ccedil ; ois-R&amp;eacute ; gis Gaudry , Artisan , 2018. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;9 oz ( 250 g ) semi-sweet dark chocolate&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;4 eggs , separated&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 15 mL ) espresso&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 pinch salt&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chop chocolate and melt it over a double boiler . Pour chocolate into a large mixing bowl to cool slightly . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Beat egg yolks and stir them into the cooled chocolate until thoroughly combined . Add espresso and stir to combine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With an electric mixer , beat egg whites with the pinch of salt , starting on medium speed , then increasing to medium-high once the whites start to foam . Continue beating until whites hold firm peaks . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Using a spatula , gently stir one-fourth of beaten egg whites into chocolate mixture , then gently stir in the remaining egg whites . Spoon mixture into serving dishes and refrigerate for at least 3 hours , &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Serves 6&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Kitchen Hack : Choosing the right wine glass&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Restaurants usually offer white wine in smaller glasses than red wine to maintain the lower temperature of the white . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;26839;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;26881;TOOLONG ... national and international food **25;26917;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;26944;TOOLONG ... latest restaurant **28;26980;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;27010;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? Bienvenue to France for 2020 Vancouver International Wine Festivalhttp : **32;27164;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;27198;TOOLONG ... title= " View of the Rhone valley from the church porch of Tupin-et-Semons , France . " alt= " View of the Rhone valley from the church porch of Tupin-et-Semons , France . " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;27234;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1213736 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Vancouver International Wine Festival&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Feb 22 to March 1 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " https : //vanwinefest.ca/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **28;27264;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; The French are back to headline the 42nd Vancouver International Wine Festival . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; They no longer dominate the import market as they did in the 1980s and 90s , but rising tariffs and taxes in the United States and even more uncertainty in China suggest they may be happy to pay more attention to the Canadian market after years of neglect . If ever there was a chance for distributors and buyers to strike for French wines that have disappeared from the mainstream market , now is the time . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Current turmoil and the ever-persistent increase in competition globally aside , there is no denying French wine has the cachet and the selection to continue to attract consumer attention , not to mention owning some of the most notable addresses in the wine business . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Names like Bordeaux , Burgundy , Champagne , the Rhone , Loire and Languedoc have withstood the test of time , and many have been adopted for better or worse by the rest of the wine world to describe their wines . Terms like Burgundian style , Bordeaux blend , and even Champagne-style instead of merely sparkling wine have subliminally kept the French in the game even in places where they have little market presence . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; What you will discover this week about France versus the rest of the world is that the only difference these days is one of style . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While it may be a bit too simplistic to say , New World wines are richer , riper and typically more alcoholic on the palate ; it is no less accurate ( or naive ) to claim that French wines tend to be leaner in structure , bear more acid , firmer tannins and in most cases , are often crafted to accompany food . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In that spirit , we explore the New World versus Old World challenge as it pertains to four principal grape varieties you will find inside the International Tasting Room , adding some tasting room picks you can check out yourself . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the end , wine&amp;rsquo ; s biggest ally is the curiosity of consumers . That should make it a lot easier for Old World producers like France to re-establish itself in British Columbia this week , because it seems everyone here remains curious about wine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27294;TOOLONG ... title= " Vineyard in Chinon , Loire Valley , France . " alt= " Vineyard in Chinon , Loire Valley , France . " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;27330;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1213743 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Sauvignon Blanc&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sauvignon Blanc tasted outside of France is repeatedly described as being Sancerre or Pouilly Fume-like. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The challenge is many consumers are simply unaware that the fresh , mineral , crisp flavours of Sancerre , Pouilly Fume , Touraine are linked to Sauvignon Blanc because under French appellation law producers emphasize the origin ( Sancerre or Loire ) and not grape variety . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the New World , the best examples mimic the Loire-style offering smoky , mineral , pear and green apple flavours , leaner structure and higher acidity . Some of the best examples are coming out of Casablanca , Leyda and Limari in Chile ; Marlborough in New Zealand ; Constantia/Coastal Region of South Africa ; and British Columbia&amp;rsquo ; s Similkameen Valley . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Food brings both styles closer together . Clams , green lip mussels , goat&amp;rsquo ; s cheese , pasta and chicken salads rule the New World , while ch&amp;egrave ; vre , any fish in sorrel sauce , onion tarts and mussels would be the French equivalent . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27360;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;27396;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27426;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;27462;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;New World : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Clos du Soleil Winery 2018 Fume Blanc , Similkameen Valley , $19&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Old World : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Joseph Mellot 2018 Sancerre La Chatellenie , Loire Valley , France , $40&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27492;TOOLONG ... title= " Vineyards , near Corton in C ? te de Beaune , France . " alt= " Vineyards , near Corton in C ? te de Beaune , France . " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;27528;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1213742 " /&gt; **31;27558;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Chardonnay is the perfect vehicle for New World-Old World grape detective work . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The past decade has witnessed an enormous shift in New World Chardonnay production as the best Chardonnay vines have been pushed to the coolest , most marginal ( in terms of weather ) vineyard sites where acidity and fruit are intensified long before the grape gets to the winery . The result is more intensely flavoured fruit and more vibrant , mouth-watering acidity . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sonoma Coast , Monterey and Santa Barbara lead the California transformation , while parts of New Zealand ( Nelson , Central Otago and Hawke&amp;rsquo ; s Bay ) ; and Australia , ( Victoria , Tasmania and Adelaide Hills ) are equally as impressive . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As for the unknown but soon to be front and centre , it is Chardonnay from Canada&amp;rsquo ; s Niagara Peninsula , Prince Edward County , and the Okanagan Valley . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The best of France is labelled Corton-Charlemagne , Puligny Montrachet , Chassagne Montrachet , Chablis , Macon , C&amp;ocirc ; te Chalonnaise , and lately Limoux and the Jura . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The French style is evolving as well with less sulphur and a cleaner style if not necessarily fruitier . Less overt oak , more lees and bread dough characters give them a complexity edge and it makes for excellent food wines . Lobster , tuna , oysters and Caesar salad are more New World dishes , while the French prefer their Chardonnay with wild salmon , turbot , sole , oysters , white bean pur&amp;eacute ; e , and onion tarts . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27591;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;27627;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27657;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;27693;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;New World : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Bodega Catena Zapata 2017 Catena Alta Chardonnay , Mendoza , Argentina , $44&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Old World : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Maison Louis Latour 2017 Corton Charlemagne Grand Cru , Cote de Beaune , France , $255.99&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27723;TOOLONG ... title= " Vineyards in the autumn , Burgundy , France . " alt= " Vineyards in the autumn , Burgundy , France . " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;27759;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1213734 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Pinot Noir&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Does anyone do it better than the French and , more specifically , the vignerons of Burgundy ? When the French get it right , their Pinot can be mind-altering. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The challenge for consumers is a blizzard of producers that share a small number of appellations . But if Burgundy is the temple of Pinot Noir production , the rush is on globally to pull even . Central Otago , Martinborough , Canterbury , Nelson and Marlborough lead the New Zealand challenge , while Russian River , Monterey and Santa Barbara County in California and most of Oregon&amp;rsquo ; s Willamette Valley lead the United States . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Look to Tasmania and Yarra Valley in Australia and Leyda and Casablanca in Chile as well as the Okanagan Valley and Cowichan Valley in British Columbia and Niagara and Prince Edward County in Ontario . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In France , Pinot Noir food matches include rabbit , Maigret de canard , pheasant and coq au vin . Closer to home , match Pinot with quail , duck , turkey , sausages and wild mushrooms . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27789;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;27825;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27855;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;27891;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;New World : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Haywire 2017 Ranch Pinot Noir , Garnet Valley , British Columbia , $35&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Old World : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Louis Jadot 2016 Clos des Ursules 1er Cru , Burgundy , France , $116&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27921;TOOLONG ... title= " A vineyard in Bordeaux , France . " alt= " A vineyard in Bordeaux , France . " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;27957;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1213735 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Cabernet Sauvignon&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Cabernet Sauvignon gained its fame in the Bordeaux region of France , primarily in and about the M&amp;eacute ; doc , where it reigns supreme . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The best Cabernet has abundant but soft tannins with concentration and flavour complexity . Winemakers strive to achieve blackberry , cassis , black cherry and jam fruit flavours with black pepper and earthy spice characters . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The lighter and more modern style of Cabernet , such as those from Chile , Argentina and Australia , place more of an emphasis on fruit flavours and softer tannins . It makes them more drinkable at a young age than traditional Bordeaux or the more serious wines of California , Tuscany , Washington and lately , southern British Columbia . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The French serve it with entrec&amp;ocirc ; te &amp;agrave ; la Bordelaise , woodcock in red wine sauce , or even pigeon with vegetables ; while in the New World it is a T-bone or rib-eye steak , lamb or venison with a blueberry sauce . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27987;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;28023;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;28053;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;28089;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;New World : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Hope Family Wines 2017 Austin Hope 2017 Cabernet Sauvignon , Paso Robles , California , $70&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Old World : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Chateau Lions De Batailley 2016 , Pauillac , Bordeaux , France , $63&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;28119;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **28;28161;TOOLONG " &gt;M ... from the 2020 Vancouver International Wine Festival edition of **26;28191;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;28219;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;28255;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;28286;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ?
