For more information and to book , please email &lt;a href= " **39;28611;TOOLONG " **46;28652;TOOLONG . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Earth to the wine world : Rob McMillan , author of the Silicon Valley Bank&amp;rsquo ; s State of the Wine Industry report , says the strain wine ( industry ) is feeling , and the rise of the hard seltzer is caused by new consumer values . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Speaking to Meininger&amp;rsquo ; s Wine Business International , McMillan said : &amp;ldquo ; The reality is wine sold to boomers was sold on the pretext of boomer values , the industry has struggled with this transition to the new consumer , who doesn&amp;rsquo ; t carry the same values . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He also thinks that spirits and other beverages can tap into notions of wellness and well-being in a way that wine hasn&amp;rsquo ; t. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; ve got to this point where spiked seltzers ( think White Claw ) are seen as a more healthful choice because of the clarity and transparency of the ingredients . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It is a stunning statement that hits deep into the heart of the wine business . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Wine is not going away anywhere soon , but the long free ride as the beverage of choice is coming to an end . Change is in the wind , and it is happening at speed ; a speed many are not ready to handle . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;B.C. wine of the week&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;28700;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;28736;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Quails ' Gate Chardonnay Rosemary 's Block 2017 , Okanagan Valley&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $44.99 90/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; UPC : 778856417012 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rosemary&amp;rsquo ; s Block leads a trio of top-flight Chardonnays at Quails&amp;rsquo ; Gate , named after Rosemary Stewart , the mother of the current Stewart family . It continues to show it can be at the top of the B.C. Chardonnay chain year after year with its quiet , leesy , lemon nose and palate . Elegant and mineral , it is all about the place and not so much about the fruit . Citrus , green apple , pear ever so slightly rises above the oak and lees and spice . You can drink this now , but I say wait for two to three years until it&amp;rsquo ; s at its zenith . The grapes come off two upper estate blocks on the Boucherie ( volcanic ) Bench . Clones 809 and 76 were whole-cluster pressed and fermented in new and old French oak barrels for 11 months , with full MLF , and regular battonage. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Wine for the cellar&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;28766;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;28802;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Rodney Strong Cabernet Sauvignon Reserve 2015 , Sonoma County , California , United States&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $69.99 92/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; UPC : 087512075283 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Artisanal winemaking is easier to say than do but at Rodney Strong , Sonoma County&amp;rsquo ; s first carbon-neutral winery , the art is in the details , and the Reserve Cabernet is made with select Cabernet Sauvignon lots from the Alexander Valley and mountain sites north of Lake Sonoma . It&amp;rsquo ; s all individually harvested , fermented separately , and aged in French oak . At six months , each lot is graded , and only the best is considered for Reserve . The 100 per cent Cabernet is assembled and returned to barrels for an additional 14 to 20 months . Winemaker Justin Seidenfeld uses cluster pre-sorting , berry sorting and extended maceration , and tanks small enough to accommodate the select lots . Lush but with structure and floral mineral notes , it delivers a rush of intense black fruit &amp;mdash ; a stunning Cabernet. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;28832;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;28874;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;28910;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;28941;TOOLONG ... about B.C. **26;28977;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;29005;TOOLONG ... 2020 Vancouver International Wine **29;29041;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;29072;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? Food ReviewsSalut ! : Babette 's stuffed quail a French decadence worth recreatinghttp : **30;29372;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;29404;TOOLONG ... title= " Cailles en Sarcophage ( stuffed quails in puff pastry ) , created by chef J-C Poirier at St. Lawrence in Vancouver . " alt= " Cailles en Sarcophage ( stuffed quails in puff pastry ) , created by chef J-C Poirier at St. Lawrence in Vancouver . " data-portal-copyright= " Amy Ho " **28;29440;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1212148 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tender quail , stuffed with earthy truffles and rich foie gras , baked in flaky puff pastry , surrounded by fresh figs , served with rare Burgundy wine . . . &amp;ldquo ; cailles en sarcophage&amp;rdquo ; might just be the most voluptuously indulgent dish ever concocted . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; By the time it appears as the main course in the 1987 Danish movie Babette&amp;rsquo ; s Feast &amp;mdash ; still the most glorious food film ever made &amp;mdash ; the once-dour diners are flushed with pleasure ( and not a little wine ) , despite their best intentions not to succumb to the sins of sensual luxury . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Little wonder then that the movie and its star dish have gone on to inspire countless chefs and gourmands including J-C ( Jean-Christophe ) Poirier , chef-owner of the multiple-award-winning St. Lawrence restaurant . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; For a cook like myself , trained in French classics , it gave me goosebumps , &amp;rdquo ; says Poirier , who is likely the only chef in Vancouver daring enough to serve this complex dish , whose name translates to &amp;ldquo ; quails in coffins . &amp;rdquo ; ( It&amp;rsquo ; s offered occasionally as a special at St. Lawrence , and only when he can get his hands on all the ingredients. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Nobody really cooks like that any more . And that&amp;rsquo ; s what gets me excited . Rather than inventing something totally new , I like to take something with a little bit of soul , and make it my own . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; What makes dishes like this so memorable , he says , is that they create lingering emotions . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Like that movie Ratatouille , when he ( critic Anton Ego ) had the bite of the ratatouille and remembered his mother , &amp;rdquo ; Poirier says . &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s what makes a dish great . Not just good , but great . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And , he adds , &amp;ldquo ; At St. Lawrence , it happens every night that someone has that reaction . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;29470;TOOLONG ... title= " A scene from the 1987 Danish movie Babette 's Feast . " alt= " A scene from the 1987 Danish movie Babette 's Feast . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;29506;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1212147 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; That is exactly what the film , based on a short story by Isak Dinesen ( Karen Blixen ) , captures so deliciously : the transformative joy of gathering around a table to share food and companionship . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In it , Babette is a refugee from the Franco-Prussian War of 1870 who finds shelter in a strict religious community in rural Denmark , where the pious spinster daughters of the town&amp;rsquo ; s late patriarch take her in as housemaid and cook . Some 14 years later , Babette learns that she has won 10,000 francs in a lottery and decides to cook a &amp;ldquo ; real French dinner&amp;rdquo ; for the town&amp;rsquo ; s few , and increasingly querulous , residents . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; What follows is a remarkable seven-course feast that overcomes the unhappy villagers&amp;rsquo ; resentments , regrets and suspicions of earthly pleasure . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;29536;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;29572;TOOLONG &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;29608;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;29650;TOOLONG ... about the 2020 Vancouver International Wine **29;29686;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; The film , directed by Gabriel Axel , went on to win the Oscar for Best Foreign Language Film . Now it&amp;rsquo ; s being remade by the Academy Award-winning filmmaker Alexander Payne ( Sideways , Downsizing ) , who plans to set his version in small-town Minnesota . No word yet , though , on whether he will recreate the movie&amp;rsquo ; s most famous dish , the cailles en sarcophage. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The origins of the dish are uncertain &amp;mdash ; Dinesen herself may have dreamed it up , for all we know &amp;mdash ; but it has since joined the pantheon of legendary French foods . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I never heard of it before I watched the movie , so I can&amp;rsquo ; t say where it was from , but I would say the Perigord because truffle is involved , and foie gras , and Cognac , &amp;rdquo ; Poirier says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He was impressed not just by the dish itself , but by the way Babette &amp;mdash ; who , it is revealed , was once the greatest chef in Paris &amp;mdash ; produces it . Not only does she have all the precious ingredients shipped to a remote village in Jutland , she cooks them in less-than-ideal conditions . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; That part of the dinner is very exciting and moving because she&amp;rsquo ; s cooking over fire and without proper equipment , &amp;rdquo ; Poirier says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; After seeing the movie , I wanted to do it my own way , and within the logistics of serving 100 people a night . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That meant , for instance , finding the right quail , which Poirier eventually tracked down at Thiessen Specialty Poultry in Mount Lehman. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a jumbo quail , bigger than what you&amp;rsquo ; d find at the grocery store , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the film , Babette tucks &amp;ldquo ; a big slab of foie gras and a layer of truffle&amp;rdquo ; into each deboned quail , but that would be much too costly , so Poirier instead makes a stuffing of foie gras and sausage . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And where Babette serves it with figs , he uses grapes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The first time I had quail was in a classic restaurant where it was paired with grapes and honey , &amp;rdquo ; Poirier says nostalgically. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;29717;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;29759;TOOLONG ... about the 2020 Vancouver International Wine **29;29795;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s a dish that can take several hours , even days to prepare , so he suggests some shortcuts for home cooks . Rather than making puff pastry and foie gras terrine from scratch , you can purchase good-quality pre-made versions instead . He also suggests preparing it in stages , for instance , by starting the pastry cases the day before . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The thing with these dishes is it&amp;rsquo ; s a lot of preparation , and then it&amp;rsquo ; s just assembly , &amp;rdquo ; Poirier says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You can break it down over two days , three days , just little jobs . And when it comes to the day of , it&amp;rsquo ; s pretty simple . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He adds : &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a very festive dish and decadent as well . It takes a lot of time to prepare , but it&amp;rsquo ; s worth it . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s just the sort of classic haute cuisine Poirier learned cooking with Normand Laprise at Montreal&amp;rsquo ; s celebrated Toqu&amp;eacute ; ! and Rob Feenie at Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s Lumi&amp;egrave ; re , and celebrated last year at a collaborative dinner with Canada&amp;rsquo ; s original Top Chef , Dale MacKay , that paid homage to France&amp;rsquo ; s greatest chefs. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It included : Paul Bocuse&amp;rsquo ; s famous truffle soup with its puff pastry dome ; Marie-Antoine Car&amp;ecirc ; me&amp;rsquo ; s eggs Rossini ( poached eggs with foie gras and truffle sauce on brioche ) ; the rich , creamy baked lobster thermidor created by Auguste Escoffier ; and a syrup-soaked savarin , named for the legendary food writer Jean Anthelme Brillat-Savarin , who famously wrote , &amp;ldquo ; Tell me what you eat , and I will tell you what you are . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It was pretty good , &amp;rdquo ; Poirier says with a laugh . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of course , these are not everyday dishes , but rare and special indulgences &amp;mdash ; just like Babette&amp;rsquo ; s Feast , which could never be truly recreated . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Babette spends every last centime of her 10,000-franc winnings to create that one meal for those 12 people . When the sisters learn this , one of them says , tearfully , &amp;ldquo ; Now you will be poor the rest of your life , &amp;rdquo ; to which Babette replies , &amp;ldquo ; An artist is never poor . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And neither is any person who gathers friends , family and even the occasional stranger around the table , to share food and wine and each other&amp;rsquo ; s company . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think it&amp;rsquo ; s important , as that movie showed , to sit around the table and take your time , even if it&amp;rsquo ; s just once a week , &amp;rdquo ; Poirier says . &amp;ldquo ; It brings people together and that&amp;rsquo ; s the power of food . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;The Babette 's Feast menu&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the 1987 Danish film Babette&amp;rsquo ; s Feast the seven-course menu consisted of : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Potage &amp;agrave ; la Tortue ( turtle soup ) , served with Amontillado sherry &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Blini Demidoff au Caviar ( buckwheat pancakes with caviar and sour cream ) , served with Veuve Cliquot Champagne &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Cailles en Sarcophage ( quail stuffed with foie gras and truffles and baked in puff pastry ) , served with Clos de Vougeot Pinot Noir from Burgundy &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Endive salad &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Savarin au Rhum avec des Figues et Fruit Glac&amp;eacute ; e ( rum-soaked sponge cake with figs and candied cherries ) , served with more Champagne &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Cheese and fruit with Sauternes &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Coffee with Cognac &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;29826;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;29868;TOOLONG ... about the 2020 Vancouver International Wine **29;29904;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;29935;TOOLONG ... title= " Photo by Amy Ho . " alt= " Photo by Amy Ho . " data-portal-copyright= " Amy Ho " **28;29971;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1212146 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Recipe : Cailles en Sarcophage ( Stuffed Quails in Puff Pastry ) &lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Recipe by chef J-C Poirier of Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s St. Lawrence restaurant . This will take at least four hours to prepare ; ideally , you should start the recipe the day before so you can refrigerate the pastry overnight and prevent it from shrinking during baking . Serve with Pinot Noir , preferably from Burgundy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Pastry : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 lb ( 454 g ) store-bought all-butter puff pastry , thawed&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 egg yolk&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) water&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Brine : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;4 cups ( 1 L ) water&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt; ? cup ( 60 mL ) kosher salt&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 Tbsp ( 15 mL ) brown sugar&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 bay leaf&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 sprigs thyme&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Quails : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;6 jumbo quails , deboned ( you can find quail at fine butchers , who will also debone it for you on request , although you will have to special order it ahead of time. ) &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;4 Tbsp ( 60 mL ) butter , divided&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 shallots , peeled and finely chopped&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 Tbsp ( 30 mL ) cognac or brandy&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 cup ( 250 mL ) white wine&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;4 cups ( 1L ) good quality chicken stock ( if using a powder base , add 6 sheets of gelatin that have been soaked in cold water to give sticky texture to sauce that can only be achieved with broth derived from bones ) &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 Tbsp ( 15 mL ) honey&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Salt and pepper , to taste&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 Tbsp ( 15 mL ) cornstarch ( optional ) &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 Tbsp ( 30 mL ) water ( optional ) &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt; ? lb ( 115 g ) foie gras terrine or torchon ( such as Rougi ? from Quebec ) &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt; ? lb ( 230 g ) mild pork sausage , casings removed&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;36 seedless green grapes ( peeled if you have the patience ) &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Mustard greens or arugula , to garnish&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prepare the puff pastry cases : &lt;/strong&gt; Roll the puff pastry to a thickness of 4 to 5 mm ( a little less than 1/4 inch ) . Using a round ( and preferably fluted ) pastry cutter of 10 cm ( 4 inch ) diameter , cut the pastry into 12 discs . Slightly twist the cutter on a floured worktop to make sure the disc is cut in a perfectly neat shape . The pastry disc should detach easily from the puff pastry sheet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Arrange six of the pastry discs on a Silpat baking mat placed on top of a baking sheet . Beat the egg yolk with the water to make an egg wash , then , using a pastry brush , glaze the discs with the egg wash . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Place the other six pastry discs on a floured worktop and cut a hole in the centre of each with a smaller pastry cutter , preferably with a diameter of 6 cm ( 2.5 inches ) . You should now have six pastry rings . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Carefully arrange the rings on top of the pastry discs . Glaze with the egg wash and store in the fridge for at least two hours and preferably overnight before baking . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Preheat oven to 400 F ( 200 C ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Remove pastry from the fridge and apply another brush of egg wash . Place small ring moulds ( height 5 or 6 cm , or about 2.5 inches ) in the four corners of the baking sheet and place a second baking sheet on top of the moulds . This will help control the rise of the puff pastry shells while baking . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bake at 400 F ( 200 C ) for 10 minutes , then reduce the heat to 350 F ( 175 C ) and cook for another 15 minutes . Remove the top baking sheet and bake for a further 10 minutes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Make sure the base of the pastry shells is well cooked , then remove from the oven and leave to cool on a rack . Reserve until ready to use . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prepare the quails and sauce : &lt;/strong&gt; Make the brine : In a small sauce pot on high heat , bring to a boil 1 cup ( 250 mL ) of the water along with the salt , brown sugar and herbs . Mix in the remaining 3 cups ( 750 mL ) water , transfer to a deep bowl and cool in the refrigerator . Once the brine is chilled , add the deboned quails , making sure they are completely submerged in the liquid . Macerate for 1 hour . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; During that time , start making the sauce . In a medium saucepan over medium-high heat , melt 2 Tbsp ( 30 mL ) of the butter and sweat the shallots for 3 minutes until soft and cooked . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Deglaze the pan with the cognac and flamb&amp;eacute ; the alcohol , being careful to keep it away from anything that could ignite . Add the white wine and continue cooking until liquid is reduced to &amp;frac14 ; cup ( 60 mL ) . Add chicken stock and continue cooking until you have 2 cups ( 500 mL ) of sauce . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Stir in the honey and season with a good pinch of salt and freshly cracked pepper . If at this point your sauce is too thin for your liking , mix the 2 Tbsp ( 30 mL ) cornstarch with the 2 Tbsp ( 30 mL ) water . Bring sauce back to a simmer and whisk in the cornstarch slurry a little at the time until you achieve your desired consistency . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Set sauce aside until ready to serve . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Preheat oven to 425 F ( 220 C ) . Remove quails from brine and pat dry . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Cut the foie gras terrine into 6 equal portions ; also divide the sausage meat into 6 equal portions . Wrap the sausage around the foie gras to form a ball , then gently stuff each ball inside each quail . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Re-shape the quails into their original form as much as possible . Transfer them to a baking tray lined with Silpat or parchment paper . Gently truss the quails with butcher twine , making one loop starting from the back to the front of the bird , and tie a knot . This will help the birds hold their shapes during cooking . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Season with a little bit of salt and pepper and place in oven . Bake 20 to 25 minutes , depending on the size of the quails . The internal temperature should be 135 F ( 57 C ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Remove the from the oven and let rest for 10 minutes ; and keep warm . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a saut&amp;eacute ; pan over medium high heat , melt the remaining 2 Tbsp ( 30 mL ) butter and add the grapes . Lower the heat and cook gently for 4 to 5 minutes until warm all the way through . Add the sauce to the grapes and keep warm . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Put the puff pastry cases back in the oven for 2 minutes , then take them out and put them on serving plates . Remove butcher twine from the quails and transfer each one into its dedicated sarcophage ( puff pastry case ) . Spoon sauce around the quails , along with 6 grapes per portion , and garnish with a few leaves of mustard greens or arugula. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Serves 6&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;30001;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;30043;TOOLONG ... about the 2020 Vancouver International Wine **29;30079;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;30110;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? We 'd like to hear from you about this or any other stories you think we should know about . Email &lt;a href= " **28;30139;TOOLONG " **46;30169;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Joanne SasvariTue , 18 Feb 2020 19:00:36 **45;30217;TOOLONG ... Gismondi : The nebulous nature of the term terroirhttp : **33;30264;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;30299;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;30335;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1210452 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Some words are synonymous with wine . Words like chaptalization , corked , cru , fermentation , lees , oaky , oxidized , plonk , pruning , racking , riddling , tannin and yeast &amp;mdash ; but none are more talked about , or controversial , as the term terroir , the all-encompassing French word for the place and factors that influence grapes as they grow . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It is not exclusive to wine ; think coffee , chocolate , agave , tea and , lately , cannabis , but wine and terroir can be obsessive . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; French wine producers will arrive in Vancouver in little more than a week , and as France is the theme country of the 2020 Vancouver International Wine Festival , you can bet the concept of terroir will be widely explored during their stay . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The notion of terroir is much broader than what has been discussed at nauseam for the last decade or two , where most of the talk has been focused solely on the soil . Is it chalk , or volcanic ? Is it laced with minerals ? Is it deep or shallow ? Is it young or old ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The questions go on forever , but the concept of terroir is more profound and more inclusive of factors beyond the soil . And we should mention it is not exclusive to France . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Climate is a key to terroir , and one needs only look at the worldwide shift to move north or south , depending on which hemisphere you live in , to get to cooler , more extreme vineyard sites thought to be the best medium to express terroir. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Biodynamics have also taught us that the flora and fauna and microflora that thrive near the vines can also have a significant effect on the health of the vines and , eventually , the grapes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As you can see , it&amp;rsquo ; s a bigger story than just soil . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Then there is the wild card &amp;mdash ; the human factor . Hundreds of decisions go into winemaking , from when to prune to when to pick to how to ferment your grapes using natural or modified yeas. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There are questions about barrels , barrel size , concrete , amphorae , stainless steel , how long to age the wine , and many more . Little discussed but equally important is&amp;nbsp ; tradition and the passing down of information from one generation to another . In the end , it all factors into the terroir equation and , eventually , the wine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This month you will hear about the chalk in Champagne , the famous granitic soils of Beaujolais , and the galets roul&amp;eacute ; es ( round pebbles ) of the Rhone Valley . In the Languedoc , the wind and the sun as well as the altitude and distance of your vineyard from the Mediterranean Sea all figure in the terroir , not to mention the aromas of wild herbs from the dry scrubland &amp;mdash ; la garrigue &amp;mdash ; that surrounds the vineyards of the south . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The French might be interested to know that here in British Columbia we are celebrating 30 years of VQA wines . The letters stand for Vintner&amp;rsquo ; s Quality Alliance , but the message is far simpler ; VQA means 100 per cent British Columbia . You have to start somewhere , and guaranteeing the origin of local wines proved to be a watershed moment for B.C. wine producers in 1990. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Today our wines are far beyond VQA . We have timidly outlined a few regions and even the odd sub-region , but it is fair to say many of the wines produced locally far exceed their official origins . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That is to say , they are brimming with terroir and sense of place , but we have failed to recognize them because , like many , we can not agree on where and what terroir is. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; All of which brings us back to the nebulous nature of the term terroir and the brilliance of the concept as expressed by the French . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Terroir is a word that was never meant to be literally translated . In fact , you can only come to understand it when you are standing in a vineyard and you can feel the wind , the sun on your face and the dirt beneath your feet , or when you are drinking a wine that originates from that special site . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Call it what you like , but the best wines in the room this month will reek of terroir and not all will be from France . Best of all , you will notice it when you taste it . Just don&amp;rsquo ; t try to explain it to anyone . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;30365;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;30407;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;30443;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;30474;TOOLONG ... about B.C. **26;30510;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;30538;TOOLONG ... about the 2020 Vancouver International Wine **29;30574;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Weekend wine picks&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;30605;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;30641;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Domaine Guisset 2016 , Roussillon , South of France , France&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $19.99 91/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 3760102322246 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The 2016 blends 66 per cent Syrah with 20 per cent Carignan and 14 per cent Grenache , turning out a scant 5800 cases . The vinification is traditional from de-stemmed stalks to the daily dump and reloads of the fermenting fruit at the halfway point . Post-fermentation maceration is 7-10 days , followed by eight months in stainless steel for the Carignan and Grenache and one and two-year-old barrels for the Syrah . The colour is dark , the nose complex and inviting , with an electric red fruit demeanour followed by a stony , mineral finished wrapped into garrigue . A charcuterie plate , grilled beef , or lamb chops would be an excellent match for this real wine , as would a decade in your cellar . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;30671;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;30707;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Ch ? teau Pesqui ? Ventoux Les Terrasses Grenache-Syrah 2018 , Ventoux , Rhone Valley , France&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $19.99 90/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 3760149591032 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Pesqui&amp;eacute ; Ventoux red grows at roughly 300 m above sea level on various estate terroirs along the hillsides of Mount Ventoux . The stony limestone soils flecked with iron oxide sands and clays offer up a refreshing wine that is organically grown and will be Demeter certified biodynamic next year . The mix is 60/40 Grenache/Syrah macerated 15 days and then aged for several months in stainless steel and cement . Expect ripe and fleshy black raspberry , black cherry , and mulberry tempered by a mineral crushed stone undercurrent and a clean peppery finish . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;30737;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;30773;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;G ? rard Bertrand Saint Chinian Syrah Mourv ? dre 2016 , Languedoc , France&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $23.99 89/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 351412 0107893 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Saint Chinian is a place made for G&amp;eacute ; rard Bertrand and his love of the Languedoc . Wine has been made there since the Middle Ages , and this Syrah/Mourv&amp;egrave ; dre planted over calcareous/schist soils is dialed . Vinification is traditional , with destemming before a one-month maceration . Only one-third of the wine ages for nine months in barrels ; the rest is kept in large vats to maintain its fruity aspects . I love the smoky , savoury red fruit and black licorice nose . The attack is similar , with smoked meat , espresso , blackcurrant , and prune . When you think it will be big and boisterous , the finish remains fresh with a mineral undercurrent . Impressive for the price . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;30803;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;30839;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Ch ? teau Argadens Bordeaux Sup ? rieur 2016 , Bordeaux , France&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $24.99 90/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 3394150048720 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Argadens red is a modern Bordeaux based on the concentration of fruit with a supple , balanced texture . The vineyard average age is now over 30 years old , and its planted to Merlot , Cabernet Sauvignon and Cabernet Franc sitting on clay-limestone soil in a single plot . The site is the highest among the rolling hills of the Entre-Deux-Mers wine region . The blend is 63/32/5 , almost mirroring plantings in the vineyard . The nose is harmonious , expressing dark fruits with well-integrated oak at this stage ; indeed , it is a wine you can age for five to 10 years . It&amp;rsquo ; s not Palmer or d&amp;rsquo ; Angludet , but there are similarities in style . Real wine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;30869;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;30905;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Domaine Duseigneur Chateauneuf-du-Pape Catarina 2016 , Rhone Valley , France&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $67.99 94/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;UPC : &lt;/strong&gt; 3385191000303 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Catarina label is a blend of fruit grown among pebbles , sand , and limestone from three separate plots near the village of Ch&amp;acirc ; teauneuf-du-Pape . On average , the biodynamically farmed vines are 60 years old . It is mostly Grenache , with less than 10 per cent of Syrah , Mourv&amp;egrave ; dre , and Cinsault . The aging is carried out in old French oak demi-muids for 12 months , where the oxygen egress is more critical than any oak flavours . The 2016 specializes in beautiful black raspberry , with a smooth , supple palate and a fresh , mineral base . A classy wine with a long future . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;30935;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;30977;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;31013;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;31044;TOOLONG ... about B.C. **26;31080;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;31108;TOOLONG ... about the 2020 Vancouver International Wine **29;31144;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; **27;31175;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Half The Sugar combines the skills of cookbook author and healthy food advocate Jennifer Tyler Lee and Stanford University paediatrics professor Anisha Patel . With busy parents in mind , their collection of family friendly recipes for snacks , mains , sides and desserts cut the sugar without sacrificing the flavour . It&amp;rsquo ; s not all kid-food either , as you can see from their raw-fish preparation for poke . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;31204;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;31240;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Poke Bowls&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 medium cucumber , seeds removed&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3/4 tsp ( 4 mL ) kosher salt&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tbsp ( 15 mL ) unseasoned rice vinegar&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 tsp ( 10 mL ) canola oil , for searing ( optional ) &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 lb ( 454 g ) sushi-grade raw tuna or salmon , skin removed&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 cup ( 250 mL ) sushi rice or other short-grain rice&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) low-sodium soy sauce&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 tsp ( 10 mL ) toasted ( dark ) sesame oil&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) honey&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 green onions , trimmed and thinly sliced&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 1/2 tsp ( 7.5 mL ) black sesame seeds&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) finely shredded nori&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Slice the cucumber crosswise into 1/2-inch ( 1.5 cm ) pieces and place in a strainer over a bowl . Sprinkle the salt evenly over the cucumber and toss well to combine . Let sit for 15 minutes . Rinse under cold running water , drain and toss with the vinegar . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; About 30 minutes before you wish to eat , cook the rice according to the package directions . Set aside , covered , to keep warm . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Whisk together the soy sauce , sesame oil , and honey in a medium bowl . Cut the fish into 1/2-inch cubes and place in a large bowl with the green onions , sesame seeds , and nori . Add the sauce to the mixture and toss to combine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To serve , set out bowls and have everyone place a scoop of rice in their bowl and then top with some of the poke on one part of the bowl and their choice of shelled edamame , cubed avocado , cubed mango , pickled ginger , wasabi paste , sriracha , and ponzu sauce . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Makes 4 servings. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Wine match&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Pok&amp;eacute ; bowls with cubed mango , pickled ginger , wasabi paste , sriracha , and ponzu sauce require a B.C. aromatic white . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;31270;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;31306;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;The Hatch Dynasty White 2018 , Okanagan Valley , $29.99&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The goal of Dynasty White is extravagant opulence , and it is the perfect ready-to-drink white for this opulent pok&amp;eacute ; bowl . The vectors are Chardonnay , mixed with S&amp;eacute ; millon and Viognier. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;31336;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;31372;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Bordertown Muscat 2016 , Okanagan Valley , $17.49&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Classic , grapey , Muscat nose with notes of lychee , pineapple , and peach will act like the fruit in this delicious bowl to lift the dish to another level . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;31402;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;31444;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;31480;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;31511;TOOLONG ... about B.C. **26;31547;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;31575;TOOLONG ... about the 2020 Vancouver International Wine **29;31611;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;31642;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? Chowdown Vancouver shines spotlight on sustainable seafoodhttp : **33;31903;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;31938;TOOLONG ... title= " The Chowder Chowdown , presented by the Ocean Wise Conservation Association 's seafood program , sees a selection of B.C. 's most-notable chefs compete using their favourite sustainable chowder recipes . " alt= " The Chowder Chowdown , presented by the Ocean Wise Conservation Association 's seafood program , sees a selection of B.C. 's most-notable chefs compete using their favourite sustainable chowder recipes . " data-portal-copyright= " Leila Kwok " **28;31974;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1214104 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;2020 Ocean Wise Chowder Chowdown Vancouver&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Feb. 20 , 7 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Vancouver Aquarium &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; $79 + tax ; &lt;a href= " http : **34;32004;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sustainability isn&amp;rsquo ; t a competition . Rather , it&amp;rsquo ; s a goal that , collectively , we all should be working toward . But , when the sustainability conversation is centred around the &lt;a href= " https : **33;32040;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Chowder Chowdown&lt;/a&gt; . Well , then it&amp;rsquo ; s game on . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The annual competition sees 13 of B.C.&amp;rsquo ; s &amp;ldquo ; most-notable&amp;rdquo ; chefs go head-to-head in a two-hour cooking session to create their favourite seafood chowder recipes in the hopes of taking home top honours in categories such as People&amp;rsquo ; s Choice , Best Beer Pairing &amp;mdash ; and the ultimate honour &amp;mdash ; Chowder Champion . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; This is one of my favourite events , &amp;rdquo ; Chef Dai Fukasaku of &lt;a href= " http : **27;32075;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Fukasaku of Prince Rupert , &lt;/a&gt; who is competing in the year&amp;rsquo ; s competition , says . &amp;ldquo ; It gives us an opportunity to showcase our northwest seafood , and to meet with chefs who share the same philosophy . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Taking place Feb. 20 at the Vancouver Aquarium , the event is presented by the Ocean Wise Conservation Association&amp;rsquo ; s seafood program . ( Another event will be held in Toronto on Feb. 26 , after which a coast-to-coast Chowder Champion title will be awarded , according to a news release. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But , it&amp;rsquo ; s not just a taste of savoury seafood chowders that are a major draw of the ticketed event . It&amp;rsquo ; s the chance to raise money for the &lt;a href= " https : **33;32104;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Ocean Wise sustainable seafood program&lt;/a&gt; &amp;mdash ; and to offer expanded insight into sustainable seafood , in general . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;32139;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;32175;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Sustainable seafood is seafood that is fished or farmed with minimal impact to the marine environment , &amp;rdquo ; Stacy Johnston , sous chef for Ocean Wise , explains . &amp;ldquo ; Sustainable seafood from the wild is harvested in a way that limits bycatch of other species , is taken from a healthy fish stock and managed in a proper , responsible manner . Sustainably farmed species are those which require minimal inputs and have the lowest possible potential impacts to other species and important habitats . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sustainably sourced seafood products have the least impact on the ocean and environment &amp;mdash ; and provide buyers with a connection to the people who are procuring it , according to Fukasaku. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; By knowing who harvested our seafood , I can pay utmost respect to the seafood and the ingredients talk by itself , &amp;rdquo ; Fukasaku says . &amp;ldquo ; It has genuine nutrition value , it is local &amp;mdash ; fisherman to table &amp;mdash ; and it is tastier . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sustainability sourced seafood , adds Chef Chris Andraza of Fanny Bay Oysters , who was last year&amp;rsquo ; s co-champion and will be returning this year to defend the title , is no longer an optional idea . It&amp;rsquo ; s a necessity . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There should not be any other option at this point , &amp;rdquo ; Andraza says . &amp;nbsp ; &amp;ldquo ; The ocean is the most important part of the planet , it&amp;rsquo ; s the lungs and the only reason we are here . If we overfish and take life out of the ocean , we can be certain to feel that on land . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We have to respect nature , and without knowing where our food is coming from , that will never happen . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And the need to support sustainable fishing practices will only intensify as the population grows , Johnston says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Over one billion people depend on seafood for their primary source of protein . As our population continues to grow , we&amp;rsquo ; ll need to depend even more on our oceans to feed us , &amp;rdquo ; Johnston says . &amp;ldquo ; If we continue to support unsustainable fishing practices , we will lose this precious resource and damage our oceans and food supply beyond repair . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At his eatery in Prince Rupert , which is referred to as one of the first sushi restaurants in B.C. to go fully Ocean Wise with its ingredients , Fukasaku makes it a top priority to educate and inform diners about the importance of paying attention to the provenance of one&amp;rsquo ; s food . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; As an owner-operator of a small restaurant , I try to tell our clients about the importance of traceability in seafood and people appreciate us knowing where everything comes from and who harvested it , &amp;rdquo ; Fukasaku says . &amp;ldquo ; Guests always seem to enjoy food more knowing where it comes from . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;32205;TOOLONG ... title= " Chef Dai Fukasaku of Fukasaku of Prince Rupert . " alt= " Chef Dai Fukasaku of Fukasaku of Prince Rupert . " data-portal-copyright= " Simon Racliffe " **28;32241;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1214101 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; For those who are curious about how to become better informed about where their seafood selections come from &amp;mdash ; whether at a restaurant or the grocery store &amp;mdash ; the chefs recommend always looking for the logo of a sustainable seafood organization such as Ocean Wise . And , to ask questions . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;&lt;i&gt; " &lt;/i&gt;People have the power to vote with their wallet and to hold their suppliers accountable to where their seafood is coming from and if it is being fished or farmed sustainably , " Johnston says . " By asking questions to your local fish monger , your server and your grocer you can choose whether or not to support sustainable fishing and contribute to the health of our oceans . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Three chef tips for getting seafood right at home&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Cooking seafood at home can seem like a daunting task . That&amp;rsquo ; s why we asked three B.C. culinary talents to dish on what often stands between a home chef and perfectly prepared seafood. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Here&amp;rsquo ; s what they had to say : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Overcooking is probably the main thing home cooks get wrong . People are used to the feeling of a well-done chicken and use that as a guideline . Seafood should have a little give to it when you do a touch check . Also I would say anything over medium with seafood is overcooked. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;em&gt; -- Chef Chris Andraza , Fanny Bay Oysters&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I believe a lot of home chefs are intimidated when faced with seafood . We&amp;rsquo ; ve all had a piece of overcooked or poorly prepared seafood , and I just encourage chefs to keep practising ! That , and take your seafood out 30 to 60 minutes before cooking to let it come to room temperature . This will help make sure your seafood is perfectly cooked throughout . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;em&gt; -- Stacy Johnston , sous chef for Ocean Wise&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Not utilizing the ingredient 100 per cent . For example , you can make great broth with shrimp shells , live scallops is not all about scallop muscle but gonad and mantle are edible &amp;mdash ; and they are so yummy ! &amp;rdquo ; &lt;em&gt; -- Chef Dai Fukasaku , Fukasaku of Prince Rupert &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **28;32271;TOOLONG " **35;32301;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Aleesha HarrisFri , 14 Feb 2020 19:00:29 **45;32338;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsCanadian Spirits : Distillery industry alive and well in B.C. , beyondhttp : **34;32385;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;32421;TOOLONG ... title= " Roots &amp; Wings Distillery in Langley . " alt= " Roots &amp; Wings Distillery in Langley . " data-portal-copyright= " Handout " **28;32457;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1194006 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Canadian craft-spirit industry is growing &amp;mdash ; by the week . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s a fact that co-authors Stephen Beaumont and Christine Sismondo say they discovered , a bit unexpectedly , while penning the recent release , Canadian Spirits : The Essential Cross-Country Guide to Distilleries , Their Spirits and Where to Imbibe Them &lt;em&gt; ( $29.95 , Nimbus Publishing Ltd. ) &lt;/em&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;32487;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;32523;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The Canadian craft-distilling scene had been growing like crazy for several years prior to our decision to write the book and we felt it was time somebody documented it , &amp;rdquo ; Beaumont explains . &amp;ldquo ; Little did we know that it would expand further by about 30 per cent between the time we came up with the idea and when we finished the book . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The cascade of Canadian spirits really kicked off in 2013 , Beaumont writes in the book , when changes to liquor legislation in B.C. allowed for craft distilleries to be exempt from the more than 160 per cent markup applied by the province through the Liquor Distribution Board &amp;mdash ; &lt;a href= " http : **34;32553;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;so long as they use local ingredients and produce less than 50,000 litres per year . &lt;/a&gt; &lt;em&gt; ( If they produce more than that amount , the spirits are subject to a markup applied on a sliding scale. ) &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Other provinces soon followed suit , ushering in a broader boom within the craft-distilling industry . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But the relative youth of the country&amp;rsquo ; s craft spirits hasn&amp;rsquo ; t led to an up-and-coming quality level of said sips , Beaumont assures . In fact , the standards set by the more than 165 craft establishments ( all referred to by the author as &amp;ldquo ; actual distilleries , that aren&amp;rsquo ; t simply taking Neutral Grain Spirits or sourcing spirits from elsewhere and repackaging them as their own&amp;rdquo ; ) featured within the pages of the hardcover guide left the authors &amp;ldquo ; pleasantly surprised , &amp;rdquo ; the longtime drinks writer admits . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; When you have an industry as young as Canadian craft distilling , there is always a worry that you&amp;rsquo ; re going to need to sift through a whole bunch of poorly made products to find the gems , but that was simply not the case , &amp;rdquo ; Beaumont says . &amp;ldquo ; In fact , when we were putting together our &amp;lsquo ; Top 5&amp;rsquo ; lists of various spirits types that appear in the front of the book , there were certainly a lot more candidates than there were spaces . Those were tough decisions ! &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The book features 56 distilleries in B.C. , a number that Beaumont points out makes the province home to almost one-third of Canada&amp;rsquo ; s distilleries. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The B.C. government has put in place a tax structure for distilleries certified as &amp;lsquo ; B.C. Craft , &amp;rsquo ; which is the most favourable and generous in the country , &amp;rdquo ; Beaumont explains of the booming business . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;32589;TOOLONG ... title= " Rob Rindt and Rebekah Crowley of Roots &amp; Wings Distillery . " alt= " Rob Rindt and Rebekah Crowley of Roots &amp; Wings Distillery . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;32625;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1193999 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;32655;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Roots &amp; Wings Distillery&lt;/a&gt; in Langley is one of them . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It began as a pet project with a lot of research and scheming , then we hit the point where we had to make the decision on if this was a feasible option for both of us , and luckily it was ; as I could not imagine life any other way now , &amp;rdquo ; Rebekah Crowley , who runs the operation alongside her partner , Rob Rindt , says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The South Langley distillery and tasting room serves up a unique selection of spirits , including vodka , gin and whiskey , each one made using homegrown ingredients from their 30-acre farm . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We have our standard Vital Vodka , which is a potato- and corn-based vodka ; it&amp;rsquo ; s silky and smooth and is really meant to be enjoyed in a martini , with more flavour than a commercial vodka but without the burn , &amp;rdquo ; Crowley explains . &amp;ldquo ; With that as our base , we have infused-vodka options from &amp;mdash ; coffee to horseradish , to truffle to hops to cranberry. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We are also in the process of launching our second gin , but just like our first it is soft on the juniper so it makes a great gin for those that aren&amp;rsquo ; t big on the piney taste of juniper . Then , of course , there are the aged spirits from a young whiskey to a cinnamon whiskey to a barrel-aged old-fashioned , to a bourbon-style whiskey . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Aged brandy , rye whiskey and gin are in the works , as well , with a proposed release date within the next year or so . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m a bit of a whiskey girl , &amp;rdquo ; Crowley says of her preferred sip . &amp;ldquo ; We have a limited-edition young whiskey-ish that was aged for a year in small maple syrup barrels . I have it neat . It is lovely . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While Crowley admits that neither she nor Rindt had distilling experience when they first got started , she says that what they lacked in know-how , they made up for in interest . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; ( It&amp;rsquo ; s a ) passion project all the way , &amp;rdquo ; Crowley says . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s still a bit surreal that I get to create these great spirits to turn into cocktails for people to enjoy . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Since opening their tasting room in February 2017 with two flavours , Roots &amp;amp ; Wings has expanded to include a lounge offering 10 different spirits and a &amp;ldquo ; revolving&amp;rdquo ; craft cocktail list , a new addition in 2020. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When asked to summarize the current distilling-industry scene in B.C. , Crowley echoed the idea initially mentioned by the authors of Canadian Spirits that , when it comes to craft-distilling , it&amp;rsquo ; s all about expansion . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s fantastic ; the growth over the last few years has been amazing , &amp;rdquo ; she says , pointing toward &amp;ldquo ; higher taxes and fees&amp;rdquo ; as pitfalls in the process and &amp;ldquo ; more resources and events that promote local B.C. distilled spirits&amp;rdquo ; as growth factors . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And a resource like Canadian Spirits will likely help promote the industry too . At least , that&amp;rsquo ; s the hope of its co-author. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We see this book as having three purposes : a travel guide for people who might want to pay a visit to a distillery or two as they tour this remarkable country ; a guide to craft spirits that might encourage readers to step out of their comfort zones and discover some truly wonderful , Canadian-made spirits ; and a compendium of the many great and fascinating stories behind the people who built these distilleries , &amp;rdquo ; Beaumont says . &amp;ldquo ; If any one of those , or even two or all three , resonates , then I&amp;rsquo ; ll be happy . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Craft distilleries in Metro Vancouver -- and beyond &lt;img class= " aligncenter size-full wp-image-1194003 " src= " https : **34;32690;TOOLONG ... alt= " " width= " 700 " height= " 918 " srcset= " https : **30;32726;TOOLONG ... 700w , https : **39;32758;TOOLONG ... 114w , https : **39;32799;TOOLONG ... 229w " sizes= " ( max-width : 700px ) 100vw , 700px " &gt;&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Searching for spirited sips in the Lower Mainland. ? St. Lawrence is located at 269 Powell Street in Vancouver . " alt= " Array of dishes from St. Lawrence restaurant 's cabane au sucre . St. Lawrence is located at 269 Powell Street in Vancouver . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;34498;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1211280 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;St . Lawrence&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; 269 Powell St. , Vancouver &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Open for dinner daily &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;More info : &lt;/strong&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;a href= " http : **26;34528;TOOLONG " &gt;stl ... , 604-620-3800 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; On my first Cabane au Sucre dinner , I begin with an idiotic move , throwing back a glass of maple syrup &amp;mdash ; really delicious but , oops . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I was at St. Lawrence restaurant where chef/owner J.C . Poirier launched the Quebecois tradition . It wasn&amp;rsquo ; t the authentic country sugar shack experience during maple syrup production season but Poirier&amp;rsquo ; s food was miles better than you&amp;rsquo ; d get at the real thing &amp;mdash ; except perhaps at Martin Picard&amp;rsquo ; s Cabane Au Sucre Pied de Cochon , one of Canada&amp;rsquo ; s best restaurants . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Red and white checkered tablecloths covered tables and a server matched with a red plaid shirt . On the table , there were little glasses filled with a dark amber elixir which I thought were aperitifs . But it was maple syrup for amping up the dishes that would come marching out of the kitchen . It was gorgeous artisanal syrup which had been collected in buckets and evaporated over a wood fire , the old-fashioned way , produced by a friend of a friend of Poirier. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Such is the allure of this Quebecois tradition that when Poirier launched the Cabane au Sucre , it was to be a three-evening event with two seatings &amp;mdash ; the $125 tickets sold immediately so he added another two nights . Again , poof ! Gone in a day . The last one was held on Jan. 23 but watch for it again next January . Sugaring off season is in the spring but Poirier thought January was the best chance for snow and maple syrup snow taffy in Vancouver . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Typically Cabane au Sucre restaurants are informal and family oriented. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Most often , they serve breakfast &amp;mdash ; eggs cooked in maple syrup , sausages , ham , beans , nothing fancy , and lots of beer . It&amp;rsquo ; s meant to be fun , &amp;rdquo ; Poirier says . &lt;span class= " Apple-converted-space " &gt; &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;34556;TOOLONG ... title= " A cabane au sucre dinner at St. Lawrence , located at 269 Powell St. in Vancouver . " alt= " A cabane au sucre dinner at St. Lawrence , located at 269 Powell St. in Vancouver . " data-portal-copyright= " Mia Stainsby " **28;34592;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1211279 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; His grandfather , father and brother produce small batches of syrup for family and friends and so for Poirier , it&amp;rsquo ; s nostalgia central ; and Cabane au Sucre at St. Lawrence , as you can imagine , is a big proud deal for him . Guests power through six courses &amp;agrave ; la carte and with each of them , there were family-style sides . Added up , there were 15 different dishes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; So ! We started with a cocktail , maple oreilles de crisse in a maple syrup can &amp;mdash ; deep-fried pork rind chips &amp;mdash ; so very yummy ! &amp;mdash ; and maple dinner rolls . Sliced pickled pork tongue sat atop crostini with maple mustard and pickled shallots. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;34622;TOOLONG ... title= " Maple oreilles de crisse from St. Lawrence , located at 269 Powell St. in Vancouver . " alt= " Maple oreilles de crisse from St. Lawrence , located at 269 Powell St. in Vancouver . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;34658;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1211277 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; My grandfather and uncles like their pickled pork tongue with beer , &amp;rdquo ; Poirier says . &amp;ldquo ; They even serve it in taverns. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The food kept coming &lt;span class= " Apple-converted-space " &gt; &lt;/span&gt; &amp;mdash ; pork sausages in maple syrup ; split pea and ham hock soup with a puff pastry roof ; beautiful salmon gravlax cured in gin ; duck tourtiere with foie gras , onion puree and duck jus . Brussels sprouts with Quebec cheese ; baked navy beans with smoked bacon , onion and garlic maple syrup. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s Quebec&amp;rsquo ; s risotto , &amp;rdquo ; Poirier laughs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;34688;TOOLONG ... title= " Split pea and ham hock soup from St. Lawrence , located at 269 Powell St. in Vancouver . " alt= " Split pea and ham hock soup from St. Lawrence , located at 269 Powell St. in Vancouver . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;34724;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1211276 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; There was ham pork loin , brined , cooked slowly then rolled in mustard and parsley served with meatballs and veg ; gratin savoyard ( scalloped potatoes ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For dessert , flamed house-made marshmallows , fudge and pudding chomeur ( cake baked in maple syrup ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Oh , not quite done . As well as the cocktail , we had a glass of &amp;ldquo ; blue collar beer&amp;rdquo ; and a &amp;ldquo ; Maple Jack . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; In Quebec , people might have a shot of Jack Daniels , &amp;rdquo ; Poirier says about the latter . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s not the best whisky so I mixed maple syrup and lemon juice with it to make it tasty . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If you&amp;rsquo ; re thinking that&amp;rsquo ; s a lot of food going down the gullet , yes , quite true . You can control portions by scaling back on the family-style side dishes but seriously , if you want the authentic experience , you&amp;rsquo ; ll leave groaning and unable to breathe . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Typically , there is a lot of food and there&amp;rsquo ; s a lot of drinking and it&amp;rsquo ; s loud , &amp;rdquo ; says Poirier. &amp;ldquo ; Quebec people are loud and excessive when it comes to food and drinking . It&amp;rsquo ; s festive . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And a reality check , maple sap running so freely these days thanks to climate change . On Poirier&amp;rsquo ; s father&amp;rsquo ; s property , last year , the season lasted four weeks instead of the usual six to eight weeks . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s probably been happening for a few years but people are really noticing more than ever in the last two years , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;span class= " Apple-converted-space " &gt; &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " Apple-converted-space " &gt; &lt;/span&gt; &lt;a href= " **27;34754;TOOLONG " **34;34783;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **25;34819;TOOLONG " &gt;twit ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **27;34846;TOOLONG " &gt;in ... &lt;/p&gt; Mia StainsbyWed , 12 Feb 2020 23:11:21 **45;34875;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsStaff BlogsAnthony Gismondi : B.C. wine of the week , wine to cellar and calendar itemshttp : **35;34922;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;34959;TOOLONG ... title= " University of California 's first institute for organic research and education will be established in the UC 's Agriculture and Natural Resources Division . " alt= " University of California 's first institute for organic research and education will be established in the UC 's Agriculture and Natural Resources Division . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;34995;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1206339 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Get ready to embrace organics as the world farming community continues marching toward sustainability , organics and biodynamics. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The latest news , sure to be of interest to British Columbia growers , is the announcement that the University of California&amp;rsquo ; s first institute for organic research and education , which will be established in the UC&amp;rsquo ; s Agriculture and Natural Resources Division to the tune of $1 million . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Organic is the fastest-growing sector of the food economy , but funding for research lags far behind support for conventional agriculture . Farmers interested in transitioning to organic or improving the performance of their organic systems often lack the guidance required to succeed . This endowment will allow UC advisers to work directly with farmers throughout the state to implement the latest in organic farming techniques to be applied quickly . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; The newly minted Canadian Chapter of the International Wine Law Association ( likely better known by its French acronym AIDV or the Association Internationale des Juristes du Droit de la Vigne et du Vin ) will meet in Vancouver during the upcoming Vancouver International Wine Festival . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Given the multiple jurisdictions in the country , the myriad aspects of law and policy touching the wine industry , AIDV Canada was established to coordinate , communicate and advance knowledge on wine law and policy across the country for agents retailers and winery owners . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The first conference under the AIDV Canada banner will take place on Feb. 24 in Vancouver , with Chris Wilson of Norton Rose Fulbright and Mark Hicken of Vintage Law Group as co-hosts . For more details , go to &lt;a href= " https : //www.aidv.ca/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;aidv.ca&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;B.C. wine of the week&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;35025;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;35061;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tinhorn Creek Oldfield Reserve Merlot 2016 , Okanagan Valley&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $28.99 90/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; UPC : 624802011127 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A B.C. Valentine red that runs long and deep on the palate . Red and black fruits , perfect acidity , and firm , dense , yet almost sweet tannins balance the finish . Already impressive , this is a wine you can cellar for a decade . Serve with a favourite steak cut , a pork roast or a Bolognese pasta dish . The final blend is 85 per cent Merlot , 10 per cent Cabernet Franc , 3 per cent Petit Verdot , 1 per cent Malbec , and 1 per cent Cabernet Sauvignon. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Wine for the cellar&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;35091;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;35127;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;La Crema Sonoma Coast Pinot Noir 2016 , Sonoma Coast , Sonoma County , California , United States&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $38.99 89/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; UPC : 049331002307 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A perfect Valentine&amp;rsquo ; s Day wine , this is a mix of estate and grower vineyards but all within the cool , marine swept boundaries of Sonoma County . The parcels are kept separate until the final blend to capitalize on the best lots . Look for an earthy wild strawberry nose with a dusting of baking spices . On the palate , supple textures preview a palate loaded with cherries , plums , pomegranate and spice flavours . It is aged in 95 per cent French oak &amp;mdash ; only 25 per cent is new . Delicious , drinkable and authentic . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;35157;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;35199;TOOLONG ... " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;35655;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1211300 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Breakfast , it 's not just a morning meal&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; I&amp;rsquo ; m so into breakfast these cold , ornery days . Food ReviewsMeet the Chef : Ian McHale drives Wildebeest 's nose-to-tail cookeryhttp : **33;35732;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;35767;TOOLONG ... title= " Ian McHale is executive chef of Wildebeest . " alt= " Ian McHale is executive chef of Wildebeest . " data-portal-copyright= " handout " **28;35803;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1211558 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ian McHale fuses an old-school approach to food with contemporary technique in pursuit of &lt;a href= " https : //wildebeest.ca/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Wildebeest&lt;/a&gt; &amp;rsquo ; s farm-to-table ethos . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He takes pride in collaborating with local farmers and butchers to run the Gastown hotspot&amp;rsquo ; s innovative nose-to-tail culinary program ; and he relishes the gastronomical freedom he has to work with offal and other unique cuts of meat , and spearheads the restaurant&amp;rsquo ; s 100-per cent in-house charcuterie program . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In May 2016 , McHale came to Vancouver to be sous chef at Wildebeest and was soon promoted to chef de cuisine before rising to the role of executive chef in 2018. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What motivates and inspires you as a chef ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; The fact that we&amp;rsquo ; re committed to working with small , independent farmers and foragers . I never know what new and interesting products they will bring through our doors . They are the reason we continue to change up the menu every week , keeping it fresh for our guests . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : How would you describe the type of food you like to cook ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; I am not necessarily tied down to a specific type of cuisine or style . I pride myself on cooking what is local , seasonal and sustainable . It is incredible what grows here in B.C. , and sometimes even surprising . I take great pleasure in preserving ingredients for next year ; it gives us a bountiful larder to pull from in the winter months . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What might diners not know about you ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; I always wear funky socks &amp;mdash ; I am currently rocking my Star Wars socks ! &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Describe a couple of your most recent creations . &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; We deal with a farm in Princeton that does the only pureblood Canadian Wagyu beef . It is super small scale and only produces four animals a year . We&amp;rsquo ; ve made a beautiful tartare dish from that animal and served it with pickled cucumbers from Cropthorne Farms on Westham Island , grated horseradish from Local Harvest in Chilliwack , house-made mustard , and shallots. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What 's your favourite local product and how do you use it ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; Tree tips &amp;mdash ; it truly embodies our philosophy here at Wildebeest and the Pacific Northwest . Each tree tip is wildly different from the other . We use tree tips in infused vinegar , house-made mustard , ice cream , infused salt , sugar , sauce , and caramel. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : If there 's one important piece of advice you might have for home cooks , what might that be ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; Get outside of your comfort zone with food : Go to the farmer&amp;rsquo ; s market , buy local and in-season ingredients , learn from a farmer , try new vegetables you may have never heard of , and be a local hero . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;35833;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;35869;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; **29;35899;TOOLONG **33;35930;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tbsp ( 15 mL ) canola oil&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;7 oz ( 200 g ) Wagyu beef&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 ? inch ( 3.5 cm ) piece of fresh horseradish&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) gherkins , small dice&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) shallots , small dice&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Chili flakes , to taste&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) whole grain mustard&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 lemon , juiced&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Sea salt , to taste&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 medium eggs&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Warm a saut&amp;eacute ; pan on high heat and add canola oil . Once the oil begins to smoke , quickly sear the meat for a few seconds on all sides . Place in fridge to cool . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Once cooled , shave off seared meat and place into freezer for a minimum of one hour , along with horseradish . Once meat has chilled , slice into 1/4-inch ( 1/2 cm ) strips and then dice into 1/4-inch cubes . Place in a large mixing bowl . Season with salt and add in gherkins , mustard , shallots , chili flakes and lemon juice . Once meat is sufficiently seasoned , place into serving bowls . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Crack both eggs to separate the yolks . Make a divot with your spoon in the beef mixture and gently place the yolk in it . Remove horseradish from the freezer and gently microplane over meat . Serve with your favourite potato chips or fresh sourdough toast . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Makes 2 servings. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;35965;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;36007;TOOLONG ... national and international food **25;36043;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;36070;TOOLONG ... latest restaurant **28;36106;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;36136;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ?
