Email &lt;a href= " **28;36165;TOOLONG " **46;36195;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Randy ShoreWed , 12 Feb 2020 19:00:27 **45;36243;TOOLONG ... chefs reveal the features that make their kitchens functionhttp : **32;36290;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;36324;TOOLONG ... title= " Andrew Richardson , executive chef at Elisa Steakhouse , pauses in front of his wood-fired grill as cooks prepare dishes on the " pass " for servers to pick up . " alt= " Andrew Richardson , executive chef at Elisa Steakhouse , pauses in front of his wood-fired grill as cooks prepare dishes on the " pass " for servers to pick up . " data-portal-copyright= " Arlen Redekop/PNG " **28;36360;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1210165 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Your designer kitchen may be beautiful , but is it actually functional ? Not if you ask many professional chefs . We chatted with three of them , from three very different restaurants , about what really is essential in a kitchen . What they had to say may just change how you plan the most important room in your home . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re set up for speed , so it&amp;rsquo ; s obviously functional , &amp;rdquo ; says Andrew Richardson , executive chef of Toptable Group&amp;rsquo ; s Elisa Steakhouse in Yaletown . Richardson , who also oversees CinCin Ristorante + Bar , was able to design the kitchen at Elisa on a relatively generous budget . It is as elegant as it is efficient . &amp;ldquo ; We just try to build systems to make things easier , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A very different kitchen is the one at Farina a Legna in North Vancouver . Alessandro Vianello , executive chef of Kitchen Table Restaurants , took over the former Il Castello Pizzeria&amp;rsquo ; s tiny , open kitchen , which is dominated by a large pizza oven . He had little time , space or budget to renovate before opening last September , but with a few smart changes , made it into an attractive and user-friendly space . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s remarkable to me how we can pump out so much food from this small kitchen , &amp;rdquo ; Vianello says . &amp;ldquo ; You can do a lot with a small space . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;36390;TOOLONG ... title= " For Fairmont Chateau Whistler 's executive chef Isabel Chung , a well-designed workflow and meticulous organization are essential to operations in the hotel 's seven kitchens . " alt= " For Fairmont Chateau Whistler 's executive chef Isabel Chung , a well-designed workflow and meticulous organization are essential to operations in the hotel 's seven kitchens . " data-portal-copyright= " courtesy of Fairmont Chateau Whistler " **28;36426;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1210167 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Meanwhile , as the Fairmont Chateau Whistler&amp;rsquo ; s executive chef , Isabel Chung oversees seven sprawling kitchens , where 10 head cooks and 100 line cooks prepare food for four restaurants , as well as banquets , in-room dining and a staff cafeteria that feeds 800 people . As if that weren&amp;rsquo ; t challenge enough , the building is 30 years old &amp;ldquo ; and each chef has added something , so it feels cobbled together , &amp;rdquo ; Chung says . Plus , it has features such as supporting walls that are virtually impossible to alter . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There are things in the building you can and can&amp;rsquo ; t change . Kitchen design is very different now than it was then , very different . You can have all these amazing plans , but when it gets into the space . . . . &amp;rdquo ; She sighs . &amp;ldquo ; We use a lot of kitchen designers , but there are still things that don&amp;rsquo ; t work . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Different as all these kitchens are , though , they have similar needs&amp;mdash ; and chances are , so does your home kitchen . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Flow &amp; efficiency&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; At home , 90 per cent of the time , I&amp;rsquo ; m a one-woman show in the kitchen . So it&amp;rsquo ; s my ease of use and logical organization that matters , &amp;rdquo ; says Chung. &amp;ldquo ; Here at the hotel , it has to make sense for each of the stations , and it&amp;rsquo ; s still evolving . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;br&gt; The key element in a commercial kitchen is flow . Things need to be organized logically , so food can be prepared efficiently and safely . Work is done at different stations , which are organized in logical progression&amp;mdash ; a single line , perhaps , or in clusters around a central module where the cooking is done . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;36456;TOOLONG ... title= " Executive chef Alessandro Vianello leads his team in a discussion in the kitchen at Farina a Legna . Although the kitchen is open to the dining room , guests ca n't see the storage that was built into the bulkhead above . " alt= " Executive chef Alessandro Vianello leads his team in a discussion in the kitchen at Farina a Legna . Although the kitchen is open to the dining room , guests ca n't see the storage that was built into the bulkhead above . " data-portal-copyright= " Courtesy of Kitchen Table Restaurants " **28;36492;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1210169 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At Elisa , the kitchen is essentially split in two . Prep work is done at stations hidden behind a wall , with an area for dishes to the far right . &amp;ldquo ; They&amp;rsquo ; re at the end of the line so a cook can just grab and go , &amp;rdquo ; Richardson says . Plating is done in the open kitchen with its majestic Grillworks Infierno wood-fired grill . In front of the grill is the heated &amp;ldquo ; pass , &amp;rdquo ; where plates stay warm as the line of cooks season , slice and arrange steaks and sides before servers pick them up . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At Farina a Legna , the built-in pizza oven was both an attractive feature and a real obstacle . &amp;ldquo ; We added some menu items that were challenging with the space , with the oven . We had to set up like a triangle where everything comes to a point , &amp;rdquo ; Vianello says . &amp;ldquo ; We didn&amp;rsquo ; t have an extra oven , so we added a convection oven for prep and two induction burners . And we converted the private dining room so it&amp;rsquo ; s a prep pace . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While food should move easily around a kitchen , those who are cooking it shouldn&amp;rsquo ; t have to . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The less distance you have to walk anywhere , the more efficient you&amp;rsquo ; re going to be , &amp;rdquo ; Vianello says . &amp;ldquo ; Have the things you use frequently within arm&amp;rsquo ; s reach so you don&amp;rsquo ; t have to bend over or reach into a cupboard . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Line cooks should be able to do their job &amp;ldquo ; without taking more than a step in any direction , &amp;rdquo ; Chung says . &amp;ldquo ; In a professional kitchen , it&amp;rsquo ; s about spacing and efficiency of space . If everything is not within arm&amp;rsquo ; s length of you , it&amp;rsquo ; s too much . Efficiency of space utilization is the most important thing . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Gadgets &amp; gear&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; If there&amp;rsquo ; s one thing professional chefs know , it&amp;rsquo ; s that your kitchen probably has too much stuff in it&amp;mdash ; and chances are , it&amp;rsquo ; s the wrong stuff . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Limit the specialty tools and have tools that can do multiple things , &amp;rdquo ; Vianello says . Think : knives , spoons and tongs , rather than , say , a garlic press , avocado slicer and salad chopper. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At home , Chung loves her gadgets . She has two smokers , numerous countertop appliances , at least four rolling pins and a pantry overflowing with baking gear . ( &amp;ldquo ; I have an ungodly amount of sprinkles , &amp;rdquo ; she says . ) But all of it has a purpose , and all of it is in regular use . &amp;ldquo ; I would not let Marie Kondo touch my kitchen , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &amp;ldquo ; My kitchen has a lot of stuff in it , but it all has a functional use . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In commercial kitchens , functionality is more important than beauty ( fancy wood-fired grills aside ) . Things just need to work . Cooktops need to be hot enough , coolers cold enough , mixers powerful enough to meet the demands of high-volume cookery . That said , chefs consider certain appliances essential that home cooks likely wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t , such as a sous vide circulator or tabletop deep fryer . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Natural gas is still the standard for cooktops , but increasingly , induction is becoming the preferred choice . &amp;ldquo ; I love working with induction , &amp;rdquo ; Richardson says . &amp;ldquo ; The big thing for me is no heat pollution . It&amp;rsquo ; s a much cooler environment . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vianello adds : &amp;ldquo ; They&amp;rsquo ; re also portable , so they&amp;rsquo ; re good for a smaller space . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And while constant wear and tear drives many chefs to use inexpensive , easily replaceable pots and pans , Richardson isn&amp;rsquo ; t one of them . &amp;ldquo ; All of our cookware is Le Creuset or Staub . The Staubs are wonderful for retaining heat , &amp;rdquo ; he says , noting that he uses Staub cast iron for Elisa&amp;rsquo ; s famous roast chickens and gooey mac and cheese . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Because it gets so hot , it develops a great crust and it stays hot for a really long time . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Storage &amp; organization&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Your biggest challenge is also the biggest challenge in a commercial kitchen : storage . &amp;ldquo ; There&amp;rsquo ; s never enough of it , &amp;rdquo ; Chung says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At Elisa , Richardson makes the most of space with hanging hooks , open shelving , plastic bins and a special rack for cutting boards that lets them slide easily in and out . Pots , pans and plates are stacked neatly with the same styles and sizes kept together . Staff have dedicated drawers for their own tools . And storage bins are on wheels . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s important to take them out so we can clean behind them , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a lot of work . The guys spend a lot of time cleaning up every night . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Cleanliness is especially important when you have an open kitchen and nothing is hidden from customers . At Farina a Legna , that was a special challenge because the space was so small . &amp;ldquo ; We added a bulkhead for storage , but it&amp;rsquo ; s also a really nice designed feature with mirrors , &amp;rdquo ; Vianello says . He tucked industrial wire shelving behind the pizza oven and installed open shelving under the counter so plates and other essentials are easy to access . &amp;ldquo ; During service , it looks tidy and nice , &amp;rdquo ; he says , &amp;ldquo ; and we did it on a budget . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Everywhere , dry goods are stored in sealed plastic containers and clearly labelled . &amp;ldquo ; In a commercial kitchen , there are so many people using it that everything has to be labelled , &amp;rdquo ; Chung says . &amp;ldquo ; Otherwise , someone is going to put saffron in the chowder and you&amp;rsquo ; ll be sad . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of course , you likely don&amp;rsquo ; t have to produce dinner for dozens&amp;mdash ; or hundreds&amp;mdash ; of people a day . But there are plenty of aspects of commercial kitchens that you can bring home to yours . As Chung says : &amp;ldquo ; I think there are a lot of situations today where we value design over function . I just don&amp;rsquo ; t think I could live with a show kitchen . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;36522;TOOLONG ... title= " The kitchen at Farina a Legna had to be designed around the pizza oven inherited from the pizzeria that it replaced . " alt= " The kitchen at Farina a Legna had to be designed around the pizza oven inherited from the pizzeria that it replaced . " data-portal-copyright= " Courtesy of Kitchen Table Restaurants " **28;36558;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1210174 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Five tips for a chef-approved kitchen&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;ul&gt;&lt;li&gt;Get rid of anything that is n't actually useful . &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Store as much as possible in plastic or glass containers , and label them . &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Invest in good-quality knives , pots and appliances . Also consider an induction cooktop , which is as effective as natural gas , but wo n't raise the temperature in your kitchen . &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Consider your kitchen 's flow . Are essential tools within arm 's reach ? Do you have to take more than a few steps between sink , fridge , stove and prep area ? If so , it might be time to reconfigure your space . &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Make your kitchen as easy to clean as it is to cook in. &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;36588;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;36630;TOOLONG ... unplanned kitchen reno becomes a serene **27;36665;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;36694;TOOLONG ... finishes and functional features make this kitchen one for the family of the **27;36729;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;36758;TOOLONG ... fare from chef David Robertson 's new **29;36793;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Joanne SasvariSat , 08 Feb 2020 14:44:58 **45;36824;TOOLONG ... Homes and DesignMadeleines : French butter cakes are the perfect anytime treathttp : **35;36871;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;36908;TOOLONG ... title= " Classic French Madeleines from French Pastry 101 by Betty Hung , Page Street Publishing Co. " alt= " Classic French Madeleines from French Pastry 101 by Betty Hung , Page Street Publishing Co. " data-portal-copyright= " Betty Hung " **28;36944;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1208028 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Whether you&amp;rsquo ; re hosting a fancy dinner party or just having the neighbours over for coffee , nothing impresses your guests like a plate of madeleines straight from the oven . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I love madeleines , &amp;rdquo ; says Betty Hung , co-owner of Beaucoup Bakery and author of the Taste Canada Award-winning French Pastry 101 ( Page Street Publishing Co. ) . &amp;ldquo ; They are like a little treat when you don&amp;rsquo ; t feel like a big , massive dessert . They&amp;rsquo ; re halfway between a cake and a cookie . They&amp;rsquo ; re rich , and not as dry as a cookie . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; These sweet , buttery , shell-shaped pastries are a classic of French baking , with a delicate taste and comforting texture that makes them something you will want to reach for again and again . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; No one is quite sure when or where the madeleine originated , although it&amp;rsquo ; s generally believed they came from Lorraine , in northeastern France , sometime in the early 18th century . But these days , you&amp;rsquo ; ll find them everywhere from Michelin-starred restaurants in Paris to the Starbucks in your local suburban mall . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; They are simple&amp;mdash ; but not that simple&amp;mdash ; to make . They start with a sponge-like batter called a genoise , and get most of their lift and volume from beaten eggs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Key is finding the proper baking pan with shell-shaped wells called a moule , which fittingly means both &amp;ldquo ; mould&amp;rdquo ; and &amp;ldquo ; mussel . &amp;rdquo ; Then your challenge is to achieve the distinctive hump that is the mark of a properly baked madeleine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; One of the secrets to making good madeleines is you should make the batter ahead of time and chill it , for flavour development , &amp;rdquo ; Hung says . &amp;ldquo ; The gluten gets a chance to relax and the flour absorbs all the moisture . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Madeleines are best enjoyed as soon as they&amp;rsquo ; ve been baked , as they get stale quickly . A good idea is to scrape the batter into a piping bag and keep it in the fridge ( making sure the ends are tightly closed , of course ) , and just bake a few at a time . The batter will keep for up to three days and it&amp;rsquo ; s super stylish to bake them &amp;agrave ; la minute for your guests . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And if plain vanilla seems boring , you can also switch them up with different flavours . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You can do lemon madeleines with lemon zest , and with a lemon glaze made with icing sugar , &amp;rdquo ; Hung suggests . You can also flavour the batter with gingerbread spices , rosewater , lavender , orange , matcha , almond or savoury flavours like parmesan , chilies and herbs . You can also dip madeleines in chocolate or mix chocolate chips , fresh berries or chopped nuts into the batter . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Best of all , you can enjoy madeleines just about any time you like , for your mid-morning break , your afternoon tea , during cocktail parties or after a long , formal feast . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; In Paris , they&amp;rsquo ; re available every day , &amp;rdquo ; Hung says . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a nice treat when you&amp;rsquo ; re having coffee or tea . I can eat them any time . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Recipe : Vanilla Madeleines&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Recipe from French Pastry 101 by Betty Hung , Page Street Publishing Co . Since madeleines are best fresh out of the oven , you can keep the batter in the fridge for a couple of days and bake as needed . Note that you will need a special madeleine pan for this . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Makes 24 cookies &lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt;&lt;li&gt;1 ? / ? cups ( 200 g ) all-purpose flour&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;2 ? tsp ( 8 g ) baking powder&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt; ? tsp salt&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt; ? cup ( 150 g ) granulated sugar&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt; ? vanilla bean , split and seeds scraped&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;3 large eggs , room temperature&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt; ? cup ( 113 g ) unsalted butter , melted and cooled to room temperature&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt; ? / ? cup + 1 tbsp ( 75 g ) whole milk , at room temperature&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Powdered sugar , for garnish ( optional ) &lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a medium bowl , whisk together the flour , baking powder and salt . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a separate bowl , measure the sugar and mix in the vanilla seeds with your fingers to evenly distribute them . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a large mixing bowl , beat the eggs and sugar vigorously until it becomes frothy , about 2 to 3 minutes . Fold in the dry ingredients with a spatula until the flour is just incorporated . Add in the melted butter and milk , and fold until they&amp;rsquo ; re incorporated . Cover and rest the batter in the fridge for at least 2 hours , preferably overnight . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When you&amp;rsquo ; re ready to bake , preheat the oven to 500&amp;deg ; F ( 250&amp;deg ; C ) . Generously butter the wells of the madeleine pans , dust with flour and tap out the extra . Fill a piping bag with batter and pipe it into the pan . If you prefer , you can also spoon the batter into the moulds . Once filled , lightly tap the pan on the counter to level the batter and remove any large air pockets . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bake them for 3 minutes , then lower the temperature to 425&amp;deg ; F ( 220&amp;deg ; C ) and bake for 7 to 8 minutes more . Be careful not to over-bake them ; the edges should be golden and the tops should spring back when you press them with your finger . &lt;br&gt; While they are still warm , turn the madeleines out onto a cooling rack . Dust with powdered sugar , if desired . Serve warm ; they are best served the day they are baked . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tip : To melt the butter , place it in a heatproof bowl set over simmering water . This is the easiest way to melt butter without having to worry about it boiling over or burning . You can also use a microwave . Place the butter in a microwave-safe bowl and heat for 30 to 40 seconds on high , watching carefully so it does not boil over and spatter. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;36974;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;37016;TOOLONG ... fare from chef David Robertson 's new **29;37051;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; Joanne SasvariSat , 08 Feb 2020 14:16:48 **45;37082;TOOLONG ... Homes and DesignAnthony Gismondi : Wines to woo and wow this Valentine 's Dayhttp : **37;37129;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;37168;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;37204;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1206251 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Today we preview several wine styles that you might consider for Valentine&amp;rsquo ; s Day along with some matching food ideas . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If you are planning a night at home &amp;mdash ; which for most wine drinkers usually means more wine for less money &amp;mdash ; you have plenty of time to select the appropriate wine . We also offer some thoughts on food to support the wines , and vice versa , and keep it simple but fun . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s fair to say the cooking at home can be a challenge for those not used to it , but our advice remains steady : keep the menu simple and stick to comfort dishes and the evening will be a success . Failing that takeout is equally acceptable , especially given that you can buy nearly anything you desire already prepared and get it delivered to your door . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ros&amp;eacute ; is always a fun wine at Valentine&amp;rsquo ; s , and we begin with highly affordable and delicious drinking &lt;strong&gt;G ? rard Bertrand C ? te Des Roses Ros ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt; ( $24.95 ) &lt;/em&gt; . You will get full points for serving this Sud de France ros&amp;eacute ; that comes in a spectacular bottle whose base looks like a floral bouquet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bertrand will be in Vancouver for the 2020 Vancouver International Wine Festival , giving you another reason to open it for knowledgeable wine drinkers . Ros&amp;eacute ; is also food friendly , allowing you to serve it with sushi , Jamon , cheese , or even a whole-roasted chicken or pork tenderloin . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Pinot Noir will be a big seller on the 14th , given its rich , round , supple textures and voluptuous fruit flavours . There&amp;rsquo ; s plenty to choose from in B.C. or further south from Oregon or California . Duck , mushrooms , fowl , and root vegetables all work with Pinot and its soft tannins . The ultimate bargain pick is the &lt;strong&gt;Santa Carolina Pinot Noir Reserva&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt; ( Leyda Chile , on sale at $10.99 ) &lt;/em&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If there is one day made for sparkling wine with a highly attractive ros&amp;eacute ; colour , it is Valentine&amp;rsquo ; s Day . Among my current ros&amp;eacute ; Champagne favourites are the &lt;strong&gt;Louis Roederer Brut Ros ? Champagne 2013&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt; ( $97.99 ) &lt;/em&gt; . A rare infusion style ros&amp;eacute ; made with Pinot Noir grapes that sit around in the tank for 7-10 days before joining some fermenting Chardonnay . Juicy and bright , you will love its raspberry , cherry , red apple flavours streaked with grapefruit acidity . Similarly , the &lt;strong&gt;Laurent Perrier Cuv ? e Ros ? Brut&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt; ( $99.99 ) &lt;/em&gt; with its light raspberry salmon colour and nutty , citrus , creamy raspberry aromas and creamy strawberry , baked apple flavours should not be missed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Consider matching your sparkling ros&amp;eacute ; with store-bought or homemade stuffed pasta . Ravioli , agnolotti or tortellini stuffed with your favourite romantic fillings could include ricotta cheese , mushrooms , lobster or butternut squash . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If there is to be meat , there is nothing simpler or tastier than grilled T-bone steaks or veal chops . Add a Caesar salad and let that big red wine fill in the edges . Any number of California Cabernet Sauvignon will fit the bill , as well as local Cabernet Franc , Merlot or Meritage . We like &lt;strong&gt;Bartier Bros . Cabernet Franc Cerqueira Vineyard &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt; ( &lt;/em&gt; &lt;em&gt;Okanagan Valley , $25.99 ) &lt;/em&gt; , &lt;strong&gt;Rodney Strong Cabernet Sauvignon 2016&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt; ( Sonoma County , California , $27.49 ) &lt;/em&gt; , &lt;strong&gt;Stags ' Leap Cabernet Sauvignon 2015&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt; ( Napa Valley , California , $69.99 ) &lt;/em&gt; , or &lt;strong&gt;Phantom Creek Estates Phantom Creek Vineyard Cabernet Sauvignon 2016&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt; ( Oliver , $80 ) &lt;/em&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For extra-added visual impact , not to mention some flavour enhancement , we suggest you decant all the still wines a minimum of 15 to 30 minutes before serving . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Dessert should be equally simple as dinner . Strawberries and fresh cream , pannacotta , cr&amp;egrave ; me br&amp;ucirc ; l&amp;eacute ; e , cheesecake , or a few well-chosen high-quality chocolates and you are all set . Now , the rest is up to you . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;37234;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;37276;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;37312;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;37343;TOOLONG ... about B.C. **26;37379;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;37407;TOOLONG ... 2020 Vancouver International Wine **29;37443;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Weekend wine picks&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;37474;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;37510;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;G ? rard Bertrand C ? te Des Roses Ros ? 2018 , Languedoc , France&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; $24.95 89/100 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; UPC : 3514123111002 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; C&amp;ocirc ; te des Roses is a celebration of the Mediterranean lifestyle , something Gerard Bertrand has done with style for three decades . It is still a baby , and will keep a decade before it begins to open up . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;37804;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;37846;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;37882;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;37913;TOOLONG ... about B.C. **26;37949;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;37977;TOOLONG ... 2020 Vancouver International Wine **29;38013;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; **27;38044;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Maki Watanabe is an award-winning cookbook author in her native Japan . Her newest book , Asian Noodles , is a collection of noodle dishes from every corner of East and Southeast Asia , mainly simple preparations that trade on the flavours unique to each micro-region . This dish is amped up with Korean gochujang chili paste , but you could just as easily use Sichuan doubanjiang. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;38073;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38109;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Spicy Stir-Fried Mi Fun with Gochujang&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;6 oz ( 175 g ) dried rice vermicelli&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;16 to 20 medium shrimp , about 8 oz ( 220 g ) &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/4 cup ( 60 mL ) cornstarch&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;6 chives&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tbsp ( 15 mL ) sesame oil&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1-inch piece of ginger , peeled and thinly shredded&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;11/4 cups ( 120 g ) soybean sprouts , trimmed&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) gochujang&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 ? tbsp ( 50 mL ) sake rice wine&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) soy sauce&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;38139;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Put the rice vermicelli in a bowl , and add enough boiling water to cover . Leave to soak for 10 minutes , then drain well . If the noodles are very long , cut in half . Peel the shrimp , cutting into each to remove the vein . Sprinkle with cornstarch , rubbing it in and then rinsing it off . Pat the shrimp dry with paper towels . Cut the chives into 11/2-inch ( 4 cm ) long pieces . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Heat a frying pan over medium , and add the sesame oil and ginger . When the oil is fragrant , add the noodles and stir fry while loosening them up . Combine gochujang , sake and soy sauce and add to the pan . Stir the noodles while they absorb the liquid . Add the shrimp and fry until cooked through , about 3 to 4 minutes . Season with salt , if necessary . Add the chives and bean sprouts , and toss to combine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Makes 2 servings. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Wine match&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Spicy Stir-Fried Mi Fun with Gochujang , a thick , sticky condiment that&amp;rsquo ; s spicy with a concentrated pungent in flavour , call for wines higher in residual sugar . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;38166;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38202;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;JoieFarm A Noble Blend 2018 , Okanagan Valley , $25.90&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Juicy , ripe pear fruit with a hint of sweetness balanced with acidity and pepper that will easily take on this dish . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;38232;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38268;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tightrope Winery Riesling 2017 , Naramata Bench , Okanagan Valley , $22&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Honey , green apples , and pear flavours are the key to balancing the spicy and pungency of the Gochujang coated noodles. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;38298;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;38340;TOOLONG ... from wine expert Anthony **29;38376;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;38407;TOOLONG ... about B.C. **26;38443;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;38471;TOOLONG ... 2020 Vancouver International Wine **29;38507;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;38538;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? Email &lt;a href= " **28;38567;TOOLONG " **46;38597;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Anthony GismondiFri , 07 Feb 2020 19:00:57 **45;38645;TOOLONG ... Day recipes : 8 sweet , savoury creationshttp : **31;38692;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;38725;TOOLONG ... title= " Satisfy your sweet tooth this Valentine 's Day . Strawberry &amp;amp ; Rose Hearts created by Betty Hung of Beaucoup Bakery. " alt= " Satisfy your sweet tooth this Valentine 's Day . Strawberry &amp;amp ; Rose Hearts created by Betty Hung of Beaucoup Bakery. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38761;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1200742 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;If there 's any one thing that 's universally beloved , it 's probably food . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;That 's why we decided to mark this Valentine 's Day by asking a few chefs and companies -- in Vancouver and beyond -- to share their favourite recipes of the moment . &lt;/span&gt; You know , the ones that they ( or their patrons ) can&amp;rsquo ; t get enough of or are hungry to eat on repeat . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; From sweet spring rolls to even sweeter heart-shaped cookies , here are five lovely recipes to cook up for your loved ones &amp;mdash ; or just for yourself &amp;mdash ; come Feb. 14. &lt;/p&gt; **28;38791;TOOLONG &lt;img src= " https : **34;38821;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38857;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Pandan Spring Rolls&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Created by chef Yen Do of House Special &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 cups ( 500 mL ) whole milk&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 oz ( 58 g ) fresh chopped pandan leaves&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;5 whole eggs&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/4 cup ( 60 mL ) white sugar&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) cornstarch&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) all-purpose flour&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;15-20 sheets of spring roll pastry&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Simmer milk in a saucepan . Remove from heat before milk comes to a boil . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Whisk eggs , sugar , flour , and cornstarch together in a mixing bowl until sugar is fully dissolved &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Blend fresh pandan leaves with roughly 4 oz ( 115 mL ) of water . Strain through a cheese cloth to extract pandan juice . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On low heat , incorporate the egg mixture into simmered whole milk . When thickened , slowly fold in pandan extract . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Spoon a small serving of the pandan custard onto one spring roll pastry . Roll into a spring roll . Repeat until custard is used up . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Enjoy with condensed milk or confectionary sugar . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Serves 4-6. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;38887;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38923;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Strawberry &amp; Rose Hearts&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Created by&amp;nbsp ; Betty Hung of Beaucoup Bakery &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Cookie dough&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2/3 cup ( 160 mL ) unsalted butter , softened&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/4 tsp ( 1 mL ) salt&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/4 cup ( 60 mL ) granulated sugar&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 1/2 tsp ( 12 1/2 mL ) light brown sugar&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tbsp ( 15 mL ) egg whites , room temperature&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) pure vanilla extract&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 cup ( 250 mL ) all-purpose flour&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/4 cup ( 60 mL ) light rye flour&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;38953;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3/4 cup ( 180 mL ) strawberry chocolate , preferably Valrhona Strawberry Inspiration&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) cocoa butte&lt;/em&gt; r &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 1/2 tsp ( 7 1/2 mL ) canola , or other neutral tasting oil&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **36;38989;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Dried rose petals&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Preheat oven to 325 F ( 160 C ) and place a rack in the middle . Line two baking sheets with parchment paper . Use a separate sheet of parchment paper to make a stencil for the heart shapes you are going to pipe the cookie dough onto later ( about 2 inch or 5 cm large heart rings spaced 1 1/2-inches or 4 cm apart ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To make the dough , beat the butter , salt , both sugars in a stand mixer with the paddle attachment at medium speed for 2-3 minutes . Add in the egg whites , vanilla , and beat on medium speed until the mixture is cohesive . Add in the flour and rye flour and mix until just well incorporated , scraping down the bowl as needed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Transfer the dough into a piping bag fitted with a 1 cm open star piping tip . Place the stencil parchment paper underneath the clean parchment paper on the baking sheet . You should be able to see the heart shapes on the paper below . Slowly pipe cookies onto the parchment lined baking sheet into the shapes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bake the cookies one tray at a time for 20-25 minutes in the preheated oven until the edges are golden brown , rotating the tray halfway through . Cool the cookies to room temperature before glazing them . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To make the glaze , heat the ingredients in a double boiler over low heat until it reaches 30-35 C ( 86 to 95 F ) , while stirring the chocolate , and careful not to overheat it . Take it off the heat when the glaze is ready . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Dip each cookie half and let the excess drip off . Place the cookies back onto the parchment lined tray . Before the chocolate sets , sprinkle each one with crushed dried rose petals . Let the chocolate set and cool completely before serving . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Makes 20 cookies&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;39027;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;39063;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; **34;39093;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Created by Claire Livia Lassam of LIVIA FORNO e VINO &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 baguette&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;8 good-quality anchovies , packed in oil&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/2 cup ( 125 mL ) extra virgin olive oil&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/2 cup ( 125 mL ) parsley , finely chopped&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 large balls fresh mozzarella , thinly sliced&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Preheat the oven to 425&amp;nbsp ; F ( 220 C ) . Without ever cutting fully through the baguette , cut it into 1/2-inch ( 1.3 cm ) slices , so it can fan open but stays one large baguette. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Slide a slice of mozzarella in each cut in the baguette , breaking them down if they&amp;rsquo ; re too much larger than the bread . Put the cheesy baguette onto a parchment-lined tray . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Meanwhile , warm up the olive oil over medium-low heat . Add the anchovies , and let them dissolve into the oil , helping it along with some pressure from the back of a wooden spoon . Remove from heat and add the parsley ( this will hiss a bit , and make a lovely bit of noise ) and then pour the whole thing over the baguette. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bake until the cheese has fully melted , and the baguette has started getting crispy around the edges , about 12 minutes . Serve while hot . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Serves four . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;39129;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;39165;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Asagao Cocktail&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Created by Vince Caparas , bartender at Miku Restaurant &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 oz ( 60 mL ) Roku Gin&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 oz ( 30 mL ) lemon juice&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 oz ( 30 mL ) egg white&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 oz ( 30 mL ) Raspberry-Shiso Syrup ( see recipe ) &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Add all ingredients to a shaker cup and shake vigorously , this will ensure that you get quality foam . Add ice to cool the cocktail and shake again . Double strain into a martini or coupe glass . Finish with a spritz of lavender bitters and purple food colouring and create your own design . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **29;39195;TOOLONG Syrup&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 cup ( 250 mL ) Raspberry Wheat Ale ( Caparas used Twin Sails ' Would Crush ) &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 cup ( 250 mL ) sugar&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;8 shiso leaves&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Combine all ingredients into a pot and simmer on low until the alcohol and CO2 have burned off . Let cool before using . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Serves one . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Sexy Orange Truffles&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Created by Angie Quaale of Well Seasoned Gourmet Food Store &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/2 lb ( 225 g ) best quality bittersweet chocolate&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/2 lb ( 225 g ) best quality semi-sweet chocolate&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 cup ( 250 mL ) whipping cream&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) orange flavoured liqueur ( Grand Marnier , Cointreau ) &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tbsp ( 15 mL ) prepared coffee&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/2 ( 1/2 mL ) tsp vanilla extract&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Confectioner 's sugar&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Cocoa powder&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chop the chocolates finely with a sharp knife . Place them in a heatproof mixing bowl . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Heat the cream in a small saucepan until it just boils . Turn off the heat and allow the cream to sit for 20 seconds . Pour the cream through a fine-meshed sieve into the bowl with chocolate . With a wire whisk , slowly stir the cream and chocolate together until the chocolate is completely melted . Whisk in the orange flavoured liqueur , coffee and vanilla . Set aside at room temperature for one hour . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With a small cookie scoop or melon baller , spoon round balls of the chocolate mixture onto a baking sheet lined with parchment paper . Roll each ball of chocolate in your hands to roughly make it round . Roll the balls in confectioner&amp;rsquo ; s sugar , cocoa powder , or both . These will keep refrigerated for weeks , but serve them at room temperature . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Makes 48 truffles. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Madagascar vanilla and lime-marinated pineapple&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Created by chef Paul Moran of Dinner With A View &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 whole pineapple&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;.39 oz ( 10 g ) vanilla sugar&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 lime&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;4 cups ( 1 L ) 17 per cent fat coconut milk&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;9 oz ( 260 g ) coconut oil&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;7 oz ( 200 g ) raw sugar&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 1/2 oz ( 100 g ) gluten free all-purpose flour&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 1/2 oz ( 100 g ) shredded coconut&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;10 freeze-dried blackberries&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 bunch fresh mint&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Begin by heating the coconut milk , 160g coconut oil and 100g raw sugar . Bring to a simmer , mix with a stick blender and chill overnight in the fridge . Transfer to a whipped cream canister and charge with one to two C02 cartridges . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Mix in a bowl 100g melted coconut oil , 100 g raw sugar , gluten-free flour and shredded coconut . Bake spread out on a sheet pan for 10-20 minutes at 350 F ( 175 C ) until golden brown . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Peel and dice the pineapple into 1 inch ( 2.5 cm ) pieces , marinate with the zest of one lime and the vanilla sugar . Keep in the fridge for plating . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To finish the dish place pineapple pieces into the bottom of a dessert bowl , add a large dome of coconut cream , and liberally sprinkle with the crumble . Finish by sprinkling with freeze-dried blackberries and fresh chopped mint . &lt;br&gt; Serves four . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;39226;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;39262;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; **34;39292;TOOLONG **33;39328;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 oz ( 60 mL ) Volc ? n di mi Tierra Blanco Tequila&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3/4 oz ( 22 mL ) fresh lemon juice&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3/4 oz ( 22 mL ) 1:1 simple syrup&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 oz ( 30 mL ) fresh grapefruit juice&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;soda water to top&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Shake tequila , lemon juice , simple syrup and grapefruit juice with ice . Strain into a double rocks glass over a large cube of ice . Top with soda water . Garnish with a grapefruit wedge . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Serves one&lt;/em&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;39363;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;39399;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Southern Fried Chicken and Cornmeal Buttermilk Pancake with Cranberry Sauce&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Created by The Holy Crab &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Cornmeal Buttermilk Pancake&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 cup ( 250 mL ) all purpose flour&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 cup ( 250 mL ) cornmeal&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 1/2 cup ( 375 mL ) buttermilk&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 eggs&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/4 cup ( 60 mL ) melted butter&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) white sugar&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/2 tsp ( 2 1/2 mL ) baking powder&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/2 tsp ( 2 1/2 mL ) salt&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Mix all the dry ingredients in a mixing bowl . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Combine all the liquid ingredients ( egg , buttermilk and butter ) in another bowl and mix well . Stir the liquid mixture into the dry mixture until thoroughly combined . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Heat a lightly oiled frying pan on medium-high heat . Pour 1/4 cup of the pancake batter on the pan and cook until the bottom side is browned for about two minutes . Flip and cook for another one minute . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Cranberry Sauce&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 cups ( 500 mL ) cranberries&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 cup ( 250 mL ) white sugar&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 cup ( 250 mL ) orange juice&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Put together the sugar and orange juice in a sauce pan and heat until the sugar is all dissolved . Add in the cranberries into the saucepan and cook on medium heat until most of the cranberries are softened . Remove from heat and place sauce in a bowl to cool down before storing in containers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Dry Spice Mix ( for fried chicken batter ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) Cajun Spice&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tbsp ( 15 mL ) paprika powder&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tbsp ( 15 mL ) garlic powder&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/2 tsp ( 2 1/2 mL ) cayenne pepper powder&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) salt&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) sugar&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/2 tsp ( 2 1/2 mL ) black pepper&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Put all the ingredients in a mixing bowl and mix well . Set aside and make sure to keep them dry . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Buttermilk Fried Chicken&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 lbs ( 32 oz ) chicken thigh&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 cup ( 250 mL ) buttermilk&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 egg&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) dry spice mix ( from the recipe above ) &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) salt&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) sugar&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 tbsp ( 30 mL ) pickle juice&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 cups ( 500 mL ) all purpose flour&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a mixing bowl , combine buttermilk , egg , salt , sugar and pickle juice together and mix well until all the ingredients are combined . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Coat the chicken thigh with the dry spice mix until they are uniformly coated . In a container , cover the chicken thigh with the buttermilk mix , seal and marinade for at least two hours up to eight hours in the fridge . After marinating , drain the chicken thigh , keep the buttermilk mixture . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Coat the marinated chicken thigh with flour , press the flour onto the chicken so it sticks . Put the flour coated chicken back into the buttermilk mixture and , repeat with the flour one more time . Set aside the double-coated chicken and let it rest for five minutes , do the same with the rest of the chicken thighs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Heat cooking oil in a frying pan on medium heat until the temperature hits 375 F ( 190 C ) then carefully place the double-coated chicken into the oil . Turn the chicken a few times to cook and brown evenly , until the meat is thoroughly cooked and the centre of the meat reaches 165 F ( 74 C ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After cooked , let the chicken rest for two minutes before sprinkling a handful of dry spice mix on the fried chicken to season . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;To Serve : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On a dinner plate , stack three cornmeal-buttermilk pancakes on top of each other . Drizzle cranberry sauce on top of the pancakes and place some of the cranberries around the pancakes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Put the fried chicken on top of the pancakes , garnish it with woody herbs such as rosemary or thyme twig . Serve with maple syrup and house-made chicken gravy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Serves four . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;39429;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;39471;TOOLONG ... latest restaurant **28;39507;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;39537;TOOLONG ... national and international food **25;39573;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **28;39600;TOOLONG " **35;39630;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;39667;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? Email &lt;a href= " **28;39696;TOOLONG " **46;39726;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Aleesha HarrisWed , 05 Feb 2020 19:00:53 **45;39774;TOOLONG ... Wilson : Easy and romantic Valentine 's Day dinnershttp : **33;39821;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;39856;TOOLONG ... title= " Rustic olive toasts complement Chef Ned Bell 's simple stew of mussels , caramelized onions and cherry tomatoes . " alt= " Rustic olive toasts complement Chef Ned Bell 's simple stew of mussels , caramelized onions and cherry tomatoes . " data-portal-copyright= " Kevin Clark " **28;39892;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1204781 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; If you&amp;rsquo ; re spending Valentine&amp;rsquo ; s Day at home &amp;aacute ; deux , either of today&amp;rsquo ; s easy-to-prepare seafood dishes &amp;mdash ; a rustic mussel stew or elegant fish fillets fish en papillote &amp;mdash ; is sure to kindle romance . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chef Ned Bell , who created the first recipe , is a big fan of bivalves . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Mussels are farmed in protective coves and cultivated on ropes , floating rafts and nets , &amp;rdquo ; he explains . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; As filter feeders , they will leave the ocean cleaner than they found it . They are also incredibly versatile &amp;mdash ; they can be steamed in white wine , beer or even apple juice in less than five minutes . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s also hard to beat a fillet of fish surrounded by aromatic vegetables and cooked in parchment . En papillote , as we say in French , is one of the most effective way of steaming because it seals in flavours . And cleanup couldn&amp;rsquo ; t be simpler : Just toss the paper after you&amp;rsquo ; ve scraped up every last delicious morsel . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hands-free means more time for massages , wine uncorking , or diving into these delectable dishes that have a complexity of flavours born from the simplest of ingredients and preparation . &lt;/p&gt; **28;39922;TOOLONG &lt;img src= " https : **34;39952;TOOLONG ... title= " Photograph by Kevin Clark . " alt= " Photograph by Kevin Clark . " data-portal-copyright= " Kevin Clark " **28;39988;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1204779 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Mussel Stew with Caramelized Onions and Olive Toast&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; This fragrant stew with its crusty toasts comes together in no time . After purchasing mussels , place them in a bowl , cover loosely with a damp towel and store in the coolest part of the fridge . Don&amp;rsquo ; t clean them until you&amp;rsquo ; re ready to cook . ( Adapted from Lure : Sustainable Seafood Recipes from the West Coast by Ned Bell with Valerie Howes , Figure 1 , 2017. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2lb ( 1 kg ) live mussels&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Olive oil&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 medium red onion , peeled and thinly sliced&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 cups ( 500 mL ) pitted Ni ? oise or Kalamata olives&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;4 thick slices crusty artisan bread&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 large garlic cloves , peeled and minced&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 cup ( 250 mL ) cherry tomatoes&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/2 cup ( 125 mL ) dry white wine or water&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 tbsp ( 45 mL ) chopped flat-leaf parsley&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Zest and juice of 1 lemon&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rinse mussels under cold water and discard any that are broken or are open and won&amp;rsquo ; t close when tapped . Scrub off any debris and pull off beards . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Heat 3 tbsp ( 45 mL ) oil in a large pot over medium-low . Add onions and saut&amp;eacute ; for 20 to 30 minutes , stirring often , until they turn a deep golden brown . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Meanwhile , preheat broiler and set oven rack to the topmost position . Pulse olives in a food processor into a coarse pur&amp;eacute ; e. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Brush one side of each bread slice with olive oil . Set it on a baking sheet and broil for 1&amp;nbsp ; to 2&amp;nbsp ; minutes , until golden brown . Top each toast with a spoonful of chopped olives . Turn off broiler and return toasts to oven to keep warm . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Increase heat under onions to medium . Add garlic and 1 tbsp ( 15 mL ) oil and saut&amp;eacute ; for 2 minutes . Add tomatoes and cook for 5 minutes , until softened . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Add the wine , bring to a boil over medium-high heat and add mussels . Cover and cook for 2&amp;nbsp ; to 3&amp;nbsp ; minutes or until mussels have just opened . Remove pot from heat and discard any that are unopened . Stir in parsley and lemon zest and juice . Serve with olive toasts. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Serves 2&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Fillet of Fish in Parchment&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; These parchment packets are Valentine&amp;rsquo ; s Day gifts in themselves , and when you cut them open the heady aromas will tell your guests how much you love them . ( Adapted from The Pleasures of Cooking for One by Judith Jones , Knopf , 2009. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Olive oil&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;4 to 6 smallish new potatoes , cut into ? -inch ( 1.3 cm ) slices&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 -- 6 oz ( 175 g ) skinless fillets of halibut , salmon or red snapper&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 small zucchini , cut into julienne strips&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 medium carrot , peeled and cut into very thin julienne strips&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 green onions , white and tender green parts , cut into strips&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;6 ( ? -inch or 6 mm ) slices fresh ginger , peeled and cut into julienne strips&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 splashes white wine&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Minced fresh parsley , chives , tarragon or and/or summer savoury&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Preheat oven to 425 F ( 230 C ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Line a small sheet pan with foil , oil it lightly and spread on potato slices in a single layer . Season with salt and pepper and roast for 10 minutes , turning once . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For each serving , cut an 18-inch ( 46 cm ) piece of parchment paper , join the short ends and fold it in half , creasing the centre . Place paper on a work surface , lift top layer and place fish alongside fold . Season fish and top it with zucchini , carrot , green onions , and ginger . Season again and splash with enough wine to bath the fish lightly . Arrange pre-roasted potatoes slices on top and sprinkle herbs over all . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bring top piece of parchment down over the ingredients , join the edges and fold them in twice . Pleat parchment all around , forming an airtight semicircular packet . If it gaps , secure with large metal paper clips. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Place packets on a sheet pan and roast for 12 minutes . Test with a skewer ; if it goes in easily , the fish is done . Roast a minute or two longer if needed . Present packets on dinner plates and snip the centre open with scissors . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Serves 2&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Kitchen Hack : Reheating leftover fish&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; To reheat cooked fish fillets , place them on a wire rack set in a rimmed baking sheet , cover with foil and put in a 275 F ( 135 C ) oven until the fish registers 125 C to 130 C ( 52 C to 54 C ) . A 1-inch ( 2.5 cm ) thick filet will take about 15 minutes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;40018;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;40060;TOOLONG ... latest restaurant **28;40096;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;40126;TOOLONG ... national and international food **25;40162;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **28;40189;TOOLONG " &gt;S ... our quarterly wine and food **32;40219;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;40253;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? Don&amp;rsquo ; t let the production scare you . It is a reserve style red , &amp;nbsp ; fresh and vibrant with everything you want in a 2016 Cabernet Sauvignon . The blend is a tour of Napa soils , including valley floor well-drained loam and clay ; western mountaintop soils shallow and acidic with low fertility ; and eastern mountaintop soils that are volcanic based , with poor fertility and incredible minerality . Look for intense , spicy , fresh , juicy boysenberry and bing cherry with milk chocolate , cedar , and garrigue . There is plenty of life here , and there is no rush to drink this wine . " data-portal-copyright= " Mia Stainsby " **28;41164;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1202475 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Vallarta Food Tours&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : //vallartafoodtours.com/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;41194;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; I&amp;rsquo ; ve said it before : When travelling , I love to go on food tours for insider scoops on where to eat , and often they&amp;rsquo ; re outside the tourist orbit . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41227;TOOLONG ... title= " Luis , tour guide of Versalles at Versalles Food Tour . Mia Stainsby photo . " alt= " Luis , tour guide of Versalles at Versalles Food Tour . Mia Stainsby photo . " data-portal-copyright= " Mia Stainsby " **28;41263;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1202480 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; On a recent trip to Puerto Vallarta in the state of Jalisco , Mexico &amp;mdash ; home to many Canadian snowbirds &amp;mdash ; I did a couple of tours with Vallarta Food Tours &amp;mdash ; a walk and eat tour in the Versalles neighbourhood ; and&amp;nbsp ; a tour by van that took us to some restaurants in the non-touristy suburb of Pitillal. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Versalles is an up and coming but still raw neighbourhood filled with great food &amp;mdash ; rents are cheap and modernizing young chefs are finding affordable bliss there . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Restaurants and hospitals . Those are the main things going on here , &amp;rdquo ; our guide Luis said of the neighbourhood . I don&amp;rsquo ; t know if I&amp;rsquo ; d walk there at night but you&amp;rsquo ; re only an Uber away from getting back to your hotel . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41293;TOOLONG ... title= " Octopus dish at Abulon Antojeria del Mar . Mia Stainsby photo . " alt= " Octopus dish at Abulon Antojeria del Mar . Mia Stainsby photo . " data-portal-copyright= " Mia Stainsby " **28;41329;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1202481 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At &lt;b&gt;Abulon Antojeria del Mar &lt;/b&gt; ( Hamburgo 137 ) with its treed outdoor seating , Luis introduced us to raicilla , a local spirit distilled like tequila and mescal from the agave plant ; it&amp;rsquo ; s smokier and more herbaceous than either . Made in a clay oven , it was a moonshine until it was legalized , and now it&amp;rsquo ; s a best-kept secret . &amp;ldquo ; Breathe out when you sip it and don&amp;rsquo ; t breathe in when you&amp;rsquo ; re tasting it because the alcohol expands and burns , &amp;rdquo ; Luis warned . Oh , and the shrimp tacos with al pastor sauce , roasted pineapple and avocado mousse was about the best I&amp;rsquo ; ve had . We eagerly returned to this place for dinner a few days later . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41359;TOOLONG ... title= " Prawn taco at Abulon Antojeria del Mar , Puerto Vallarta . Mia Stainsby photo . " alt= " Prawn taco at Abulon Antojeria del Mar , Puerto Vallarta . Mia Stainsby photo . " data-portal-copyright= " Mia Stainsby " **28;41395;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1202482 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Luis deemed &lt;b&gt;Los Monos Bichis &lt;/b&gt; ( Viena &lt;b&gt; &lt;/b&gt; 209 B ) the best place for seafood . We wolfed down tostadas with shrimp , marlin pate , octopus salad and downed a shrimp broth . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a hangover cure , &amp;rdquo ; said Luis . Funny , we were served a hangover cure in another restaurant on another tour . Did we look that wasted ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Onward to &lt;b&gt;El Aguaje&lt;/b&gt; restaurant ( Viena 253 ) , a family operation with a brick wood-burning pit inside to cook skewered meats on long iron rods . While watching a woman slap around tortilla dough and grill them to order , we sipped horchata and munched on tacos with meats from the pit . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Luis enlightened the group on the origin of the word taco . Silver miners took tortillas for lunch into the mines and when rolled they resembled the explosive gunpowder charges they used , called tacos . Huh , didn&amp;rsquo ; t know that ! &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41425;TOOLONG ... title= " Lamara restaurant . Mia Stainsby photo . " alt= " Lamara restaurant . Mia Stainsby photo . " data-portal-copyright= " Mia Stainsby " **28;41461;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1202486 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; En route to **25;41491;TOOLONG ( Hamburgo 108 ) , also known for great seafood , Luis excitedly pointed mid-street : &amp;ldquo ; Right there , right there , at night , there&amp;rsquo ; s this lamb birrieria . They make it with spices , toasted sesame and spicy arbol sauce . &amp;rdquo ; Birria , a Jalisco specialty , is a slow-braised stew . &amp;ldquo ; The meat&amp;rsquo ; s cooked for eight to 12 hours , then shredded onto a taco with some broth , &amp;rdquo ; said Luis . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s heaven . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We sat in a super casual area adjacent to the dining room which is always packed . We had tacos ( tuna , avocado and pineapple ) and taco chips with a tamarind and cranberry salsa. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41518;TOOLONG ... title= " Tacos at La Matona , Puerto Vallarta . Mia Stainsby photo . " alt= " Tacos at La Matona , Puerto Vallarta . Mia Stainsby photo . " data-portal-copyright= " Mia Stainsby " **28;41554;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1202474 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;b&gt;La Matona&lt;/b&gt; ( Hamburgo 1046 ) is best known for their gut-busting quesadillas . The name of the restaurant translates to thug or killer , a reference to the lethal size of the quesadilla. &amp;ldquo ; When I had it , I was full for three days , &amp;rdquo ; Luis said . We were rather full already so opted for the fresh and lively tacos which came with a multitude of salsas . At another table , a guy sat with three of the killer quesadillas . I have to believe family or friends were going to join him . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41584;TOOLONG ... title= " Making tacos at La Matona in Puerto Vallarta . Mia Stainsby photo . " alt= " Making tacos at La Matona in Puerto Vallarta . Mia Stainsby photo . " data-portal-copyright= " Mia Stainsby " **28;41620;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1202483 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; To cap the afternoon , we stopped for some very good ice cream at &lt;b&gt;La Nieveria&lt;/b&gt; ( Francia 106 ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; The Vallarta by Road tour took us further out to some restaurants in Pitillal , a suburb where the workers in the tourist industry live . &amp;ldquo ; This isn&amp;rsquo ; t a tourist area and you can buy groceries at a fraction of Walmart prices , &amp;rdquo ; said Bernardo , our guide . Restaurants have bargain prices as well , and the ones we hit each had unique offerings . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41650;TOOLONG ... title= " Texcoco Barbacoa at Pitillal. " alt= " Texcoco Barbacoa at Pitillal. " data-portal-copyright= " Mia Stainsby " **28;41686;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1202484 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At &lt;b&gt;Barbacoa Texcoco&lt;/b&gt; ( Ecuador 14461 ) we had pit-cooked lamb in large tortilla shells . It was heated and assembled at a portable grill on the street . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We drove on to **30;41716;TOOLONG &lt;b&gt;ortas Ahogadas&lt;/b&gt; ( Lisboa 128 ) known for the tortas in baguette. &amp;ldquo ; It was started by accident , &amp;rdquo ; said Bernardo. &amp;ldquo ; The French baguette became popular , but it&amp;rsquo ; s a bit different because of the altitude at which it&amp;rsquo ; s made . It&amp;rsquo ; s denser and gets a different texture . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At &lt;b&gt;Mariscos Pichis&lt;/b&gt; ( Emiliano Zapata 365 ) , Bernardo explained the owner had really wanted a dove as a kid but got a goat and Dove ( pichis ) became his forever nickname . The specialty here is a really fresh seafood tostada , and it was . And , again , a hangover cure made with a seafood broth . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The owners of &lt;b&gt;Birria Robles el Lic&lt;/b&gt; ( Hidalgo 200 ) raise their own goats and beef , and the restaurant is known for birria , the Jalisco stew . &lt;span class= " Apple-converted-space " &gt; &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41748;TOOLONG ... title= " Enchilada with mole sauces at Gaby 's Restaurant and Bar . " data-portal-copyright= " Mia Stainsby " **28;41784;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1202485 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;b&gt;Gaby 's Restaurant and Bar&lt;/b&gt; , near downtown ( Calle Mina 252 ) , has a nice terrace and the food was bright and cleanly presented . &amp;ldquo ; This place started selling food on the street level and now it&amp;rsquo ; s a full restaurant and they give cooking classes , &amp;rdquo ; said Bernardo . The highlight was an enchilada with a lovely mole . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We concluded the tour at an ice cream spot in town but , meh ! &lt;span class= " Apple-converted-space " &gt; &lt;/span&gt; Not worth mentioning when compared to La Nieveria in Versalles. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Not bad wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t you say ? We sampled specialties at a dozen places in two afternoons . Thankfully , the El Malecon ocean boardwalk in Puerto Vallarta offered hours of walking off the calories . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;41814;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;41856;TOOLONG ... national and international food **25;41892;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **27;41919;TOOLONG " **34;41948;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **25;41984;TOOLONG " &gt;twit ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **27;42011;TOOLONG " &gt;in ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;42040;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? Email &lt;a href= " **28;42069;TOOLONG " **46;42099;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Mia StainsbyWed , 05 Feb 2020 19:00:24 **45;42147;TOOLONG ... BlogsKaren Barnaby : Fried rice an easy family favouritehttp : **31;42194;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;42227;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " Karen Barnaby " **28;42263;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1200306 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; A friend of mine returned from teaching in Beijing and asked me if I was an egg first , or an egg last person . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I immediately knew that he was referring to cooking fried rice . I told him that I started as an egg first person , but had switched to being an egg last person . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The history I have with fried rice is long and filled with failures and triumphs . My family would venture to the Marco Polo restaurant a few times a year and we rarely strayed from almond chicken guy ding , sweet and sour pork , a vegetable chow mein , egg rolls , and fried rice . The rice was brown from soy sauce and deliciously salty . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; My mother would occasionally cook what I&amp;rsquo ; ll term as Chinese-inspired food and serve it with Dainty Rice Chinese-Style rice in a can . I could barely contain my excitement when the red and white box appeared because I was allowed to make it . The rice was vacuum-sealed and made a satisfying hiss when pierced by the can opener . Inside the can , there were firm little grains of rice . They were transformed into delicious plump grains after being heated in a frying pan with the seasoning packet , oil , and water . It was a miracle . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In my teens , my peers also had a fascination with fried rice . While other teens were drinking and getting high on Friday night , we were at each other&amp;rsquo ; s homes , trying to crack the code . We used converted rice , or instant rice , because that&amp;rsquo ; s what was in our parent&amp;rsquo ; s kitchen cupboards . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The converted rice was usually undercooked , overcooked , or burned , and the instant rice only resembled rice in appearance . Add to that big glugs of Western soy sauce and you have a brown , salty mess . We didn&amp;rsquo ; t care , we thought we were culinary geniuses. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Across from my high school , Ming&amp;rsquo ; s restaurant was mobbed at lunch and after school with obnoxious teens , yelling out orders for french fries , fried rice and toast . One late afternoon , I was sitting in view of the kitchen and saw Mrs. Ming take a large container out of the fridge filled with beautifully separate grains of rice that were dyed brown by soy sauce and dotted with flecks of green onion . Mr. Ming tossed a scoop into his smoking wok and in seconds , a beautiful dish of fried rice was in front of me . Cook rice , coat it with soy sauce , refrigerate . That was the secret . I could now rest in peace . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; My Thai mentor Vanipha shook me out of my Canadian-Chinese fried rice daze . I watched her fry garlic in oil until it was golden brown then scramble in an egg . She added cooked rice and fried it until it was glistening with oil . Fish sauce , a drop of oyster sauce&amp;shy ; , a liberal amount of black pepper , green onions , and finely diced seeded tomato were the crowning touch . This was my second coming of fried rice . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; My current favourites are the pristinely white Chinese-style fried rice seasoned only with salt , and it&amp;rsquo ; s opposite , kimchee fried rice . New favourites are yet to be discovered and while I&amp;rsquo ; m waiting , I&amp;rsquo ; ll get a package of Dainty Rice to see if it lives up to my memory . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **30;42293;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **37;42325;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; **28;42364;TOOLONG &lt;img src= " https : **34;42394;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;42430;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Basic Egg Fried Rice&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; If you want to add cooked , leftover meats , fry them in the oil before anything else is added . It is important to use cold , cooked rice that has been refrigerated for 24-72 hours . Break up the clumps by wetting your hands and squeezing them to separate the rice into grains . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 Tbsp ( 45 mL ) neutral flavoured vegetable oil&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;4 cups ( 1 L ) cold , cooked rice&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;42460;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 eggs , beaten&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 green onions , thinly sliced&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Heat a large , heavy frying pan on high heat until smoking . Add the oil , swirl it around the pan then add the rice . Spread out and toss the rice with a spatula until heated through . Season with salt , I usually start with 1/4 tsp ( 1 mL ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Make a well in the rice and add the eggs , stir rapidly until the eggs are half done then incorporate the rice . When the egg is evenly distributed stir in the green onion and serve immediately . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Note : &lt;/strong&gt; If you want to be an egg first person , scramble the egg in the pan first and when it&amp;rsquo ; s half cooked , add the rice . My favourite egg last method is drizzling the egg over the rice and stirring until it is cooked on to the grains . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Serves 2 as a main , 4 as a side&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;42487;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;42523;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Sheet Pan " Fried " Brown Rice with Shiitake Mushrooms and Kale&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; The results are excellent with this dish and there is no stovetop to clean . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;4 Tbsp ( 60 mL ) neutral flavoured vegetable oil , divided&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/2-inch piece of fresh ginger , peeled and minced&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;2 cloves garlic minced&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1/2 lb ( 250 g ) fresh shiitake mushrooms , stems removed , caps thinly sliced&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;6 cups ( 1.5 L ) coarsely chopped curly kale leaves&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;pinch of salt&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;4 cups ( 1 L ) cold cooked brown rice&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 Tbsp ( 45 mL ) soy sauce&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;3 green onions thinly sliced&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;1 tsp ( 5 mL ) sesame oil&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;toasted sesame seeds&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Heat the oven to 500 F ( 260 C ) . 500 F is not a typo . Place an 11 x 17-inch ( 28 x 43 cm ) rimmed sheet pan in the oven while it&amp;rsquo ; s heating . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Place 3 Tbsp ( 45 mL ) of the oil in a large bowl . Add the ginger , garlic , mushrooms , kale and a large pinch of salt . Toss to coat with the oil . Remove the baking sheet from the oven and spread the mixture evenly on it . Bake for 5 minutes . The kale will be brown and crispy in spots . Stir and redistribute the mixture . Bake for 3 minutes longer . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Add the rice to the vegetable mixture and drizzle with the remaining oil and soy sauce . Stir until well mixed and spread out evenly . Bake for 5 minutes , the rice should be hot and tender . Stir in the green onion and sesame oil . Garnish with the sesame seeds . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Serves 2 as a main , 4 as a side&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;42553;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ?
