Email &lt;a href= " **28;42582;TOOLONG " **46;42612;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Karen BarnabyMon , 03 Feb 2020 19:00:30 **45;42660;TOOLONG ... the Chef : Kiko Nakatahttp : **34;42707;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;42743;TOOLONG ... title= " Kiko Nakata is the head pastry chef at Miku restaurant . " alt= " Kiko Nakata is the head pastry chef at Miku restaurant . " data-portal-copyright= " Cody Chan " **28;42779;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1189926 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Kiko Nakata brings the philosophy of ningenmi to her work as a pastry chef at Miku restaurant , specifically &amp;ldquo ; finding happiness in one&amp;rsquo ; s life by bringing joy to others . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;Nakata grew up surrounded by food lovers . Her Japanese-born mother and grandparents fostered her love for cooking , encouraging her to pursue her culinary dreams . She studied her craft at Le Cordon Bleu in London , England , and spent two years working and learning from her mentor Thomas Haas , a skilled chocolatier. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;She moved to Japan to further connect with her heritage , working at Dominique Ansel Bakery in Tokyo . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What motivates and inspires you as a chef ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; Growing up , my parents put a lot of emphasis on the importance of family and friends . Food is something that always seems to bring people together , especially really good food . The whole culture of coming together around a meal really lights the fire in my heart to work hard and produce dishes I would be proud to serve my own loved ones . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : How would you describe the type of food you like to cook ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; Fine-dining restaurants and pastry shops are what I have always gravitated towards in my career , but when I&amp;rsquo ; m at home it&amp;rsquo ; s all chocolate chip cookies and banana bread ! &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What might diners not know about you ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; I am not outdoorsy , at all . As much as I love and appreciate nature , I&amp;rsquo ; m happiest curled up by the fireplace with my partner and watching Marvel movies on repeat . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Describe a couple of your most recent creations . &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; The most recent additions to our roster are a Fig &amp;amp ; Mascarpone Mille Feuille ; Caramelized fig compote , spiced white chocolate mascarpone cream , apricot gel , almond crumble and pear sorbet . As well as a mikan ( mandarin ) mousse cake which we ran as our New Year&amp;rsquo ; s Eve special ; white chocolate orange mousse , mandarin gelee , matcha sponge , white sesame namelaka , sesame tuile and champagne jelly . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What 's your favourite local product and how do you use it ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; Any kind of fresh fruit to make into sorbet . Summertime in B.C. is a dream , we have access to so much fresh , local produce . Peaches , strawberries , blueberries and nectarines are some of my favourites . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : If there 's one important piece of advice you might have for home cooks , what might that be ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; Get a scale ! There are some good , small ones for under $30 , and it&amp;rsquo ; s worth every cent . It&amp;rsquo ; s much easier to nail a recipe when it&amp;rsquo ; s down to grams rather than scoops. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Coconut Yuzu Panna Cotta&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;42809;TOOLONG ... title= " Coconut Yuzu Panna Cotta , by Kiko Nakata , head pastry chef at Miku. " alt= " Coconut Yuzu Panna Cotta , by Kiko Nakata , head pastry chef at Miku. " data-portal-copyright= " Cody Chan " **28;42845;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1189924 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; 1 &amp;frac34 ; cups ( 400 mL ) coconut milk &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 1 &amp;frac12 ; tbsp. ( 5 sheets ) powdered gelatin &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 1 &amp;frac14 ; cups ( 310 mL ) heavy cream &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 3/4 cup + 1 tbsp. ( 195 mL ) whole milk &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 1 cup ( 250 mL ) condensed milk &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Place gelatin in a bowl of ice water and allow to soak until soft , or follow the instructions on the packet of gelatin to bloom . In a medium-sized pot , heat coconut milk , whole milk and heavy cream while whisking continuously . Once at a boil , squeeze any excess water out of the gelatin and add into the pot ( or add powdered gelatin mixture ) . Whisk well and remove from heat . Add condensed milk and mix in using a spatula to avoid creating bubbles . Distribute evenly into six glasses . Set in the fridge for 3 hours minimum , overnight if possible . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Yuzu Curd&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 5 tbsp. ( 75 mL ) yuzu juice &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 5 tbsp. ( 75 mL ) lemon juice &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 3/4 cup ( 180 mL ) granulated sugar &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 3 eggs &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 3/4 cup ( 180 mL ) unsalted butter , cut into pieces &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a large bowl , crack eggs and whisk together with sugar . Set aside . In a medium-sized pot , bring lemon juice and yuzu juice to a boil . While mixing , gradually pour yuzu mixture into the eggs . Make sure you are whisking continuously and don&amp;rsquo ; t pour too fast or the eggs will cook . Once the two are combined , return to the pot over heat and cook on medium heat until thickened . Make sure to stir continuously as the bottom burns easily . Remove from heat , gradually whisk in butter . Once the butter is fully incorporated and the curd is slightly cooled , evenly pour into the glasses on top of the panna cotta . Allow to set in the fridge for a minimum of 2 hours . Decorate with fresh fruits and serve . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Makes 6 servings. &lt;/p&gt; Randy ShoreSat , 01 Feb 2020 19:00:30 **45;42875;TOOLONG ...
